#!/usr/bin/env python3
# shopread.py [window_min] [--selftest] -- the read for canary `shop-01` (the building tier's WORKSHOP: a fitted, frozen record
# per town, built by up to two builders on slot and segment leases; design docs/reports/building-tier-2026-10-23.md section 6.1
# row 3). CANARY_DRYRUN=pool[,pool]:sha:iso for dry runs (never emits). Judges the CANARY ARM; DiD against control for the
# no-harm lines; DENOMINATORS FIRST. Needs townplanread.py beside it (kv, pool_of, read_plans, zones_of, in_box, did_z, ck).
#
# THE CHANGE (what only this build writes):
#   _build_mode          once per spawn: the licence (phase=mode v=1 shop= blueprints= builders_max= reserve=); also marks a NEW
#                        CONNECTION of that bot (its unknown cells start empty)
#   _build               op=sited|place|replant|clear|unknown|known  project seg cell block kind saplings gen
#   _build_seg           one per segment worked in a visit: seg open done
#   _build_visit         every build_shop run, every exit: outcome stop placed refused silent cleared planted unknown slot gen took back
#   _build_bag           one per visit that reached its bag: name=bag0/took/crafted/placed/back/bag1 (shop.mjs bagBalance)
#   _structure_complete  the record's completion transition, once per town
# THE TOWN FILES (the oracle; /var/lib/mcai/_pool-<pool>/): project-<home>.shop.g<N>.json (the record chain: gen, prev, prevSha
#   sha256 of the bytes), project-<home>.shop.<slotK|seg-X>.lease.g<N>.json (+ .released), protected-zones.json.
# THE WORLD (the structure count's mirror on this host, ~/structcount/in/gen-<UTC>/structcount-day.json, one per night).
#
# GATES (deterministic; each has a failing fixture and a passing control in --selftest)
#   G1 off_record      a `_build op=place` cell that is not a cell of the town's record
#                      project=road|tower|shrine|dress (picker-01, the town sinks; towndress-01's lights and signs): not a cell of a
#                      generation of the plot's chain IN FORCE when it was placed (sink_place_ok: a growing record; an instance's
#                      cells count from its siting on). A dress SIGN is a record cell too (role L<n>-sign, block *_wall_sign, kind=sign)
#   G2 clear_outside   a `_build op=clear` cell outside the record's clear list (kind=foreign: outside the record's cells)
#                      project=road|tower|shrine|dress: outside the clear list (kind=foreign: the cells) of every generation in force (sink_clear_ok)
#                      project=tidy (tidy-01 + groundskeeper-01, tidyread-01 11-03): outside the cut + clear lists of EVERY
#                      generation of the tidy record's chain (stage 0 and each land-plan stage), or ON another project's record
#                      cell or an expedition marker's block that stood when it was dug (tidy_clear_ok)
#   G3 zone            a placement, replant or clear inside a protected zone
#   G4 lease_overlap   two holders live on one lease (a later generation by ANOTHER holder taken before the earlier one was
#                      released or lapsed + RECLAIM_MS), or a slot name beyond builders_max
#                      project=tidy, THE GROUNDS CREW (gkhelpers-01, 11-03: a keeper + at most CREW_HELPERS_MAX helpers,
#                      bots/src/groundskeeper.mjs): one bot holding two crew leases at once (keeper + helperN, helper1 + helper2), a
#                      helperN beyond CREW_HELPERS_MAX, or two bots holding segments within CREW_SEG_GAP columns of each other for
#                      longer than CREW_YIELD_MS (the bot yields a raced take at once) -- leases taken after the read's cut only
#   G2 (tidy, gkhelpers-01) a `kind=gather` clear (the park gather) is a plant block (GATHER_BLOCKS: park.mjs GATHER_NAMES), never
#                      another project's record cell or a marker (tidy_guard), never in the park (the land plan's park box + 1), never a
#                      FILL cell of the tidy chain (gather_ok)
#   G9 farm_dig (cropfarm-01, 11-03; counted as clear_outside, so the tripwire's G2 line trips on it): project=cropfarm
#                      op=harvest -- a crop block (wheat, carrots, potatoes, beetroots) dug on a crop cell (role wheat) of any
#                      generation of the farm's record, never anything else, never in a protected zone (G3 judges harvests too);
#                      op=clear kind=seedgrass (the seed gather, bots/src/skills.mjs cropSeedGather) -- a grass plant (short/tall grass,
#                      fern, large fern), never another project's record cell or a marker when it was dug (tidy_guard: the farm's own
#                      cells too -- its bed's plants are runVisit's listed clears, kind=plant), never in the park's box + 1, and within
#                      SEEDGRASS_R of the farm's box (crop_harvest_ok, seedgrass_ok)
#   G5 felled_unplanted  a `kind=tree` clear whose row says fewer replant cells read a sapling than the record has (saplings=a/b,
#                      a < b); a tree clear without the field is g5_unjudged (also a gate)
#   G6 unknown_stood   a bot's OWN build row whose position (its stand) is IN or ON a cell its own `op=unknown` row named, before
#                      an `op=known`/clear of it or its next `_build_mode` (a new connection)
#   G8 bag_loss        a `_build_bag` part (any name but cobblestone and planks, which the pathfinder may scaffold) the visit
#                      cannot account for (unexplained < 0) -- judged only on a clean exit with silent=0 (bag_unjudged: reported)
#   ORACLE             oracle_missing: a canary town with build mutations and no readable record; zone_unknown: ... and a
#                      protected-zones.json that is present but unreadable (a MISSING file is no zones, as the bot reads it)
#   ARMS               control rows of these kinds after the deploy (must be 0; build_shop is chatOnly -- a player's chat to a
#                      CONTROL bot writes `_build_visit outcome=off`: read that row before calling it a leak)
#   LIVENESS           canary `_build_mode` >= 1 and canary `_build_visit` >= 1
#   EXPOSURE           RECORD CONFORMANCE AND PERSISTENCE (Codex read r3; owner 10-25): a canary town counts when it has a
#                      `_structure_complete` row AND two VALID conformance surveys of ITS CURRENT RECORD (gen and sha) -- observed
#                      times (the survey's `at` less SAVE_LAG_MIN) after the completion, >= CONFORM_GAP_MIN apart -- both F1 >= 0.98, its
#                      NEWEST survey also >= 0.98 (damage since revokes it); >= EXPOSE_TOWNS such towns. The surveys are
#                      scripts/host/shopconform.py's (the record's cells judged by the builder's own rule against a copy of the
#                      region files), mirrored to SURVEY_DIR on this host by scripts/host/shopconform-run.sh. No survey directory:
#                      conformance_source 0 and exposure NEVER ready (fail closed). Invalid surveys (any unknown cell) never count.
#                      PRELIMINARY, reported: a LIT ROOM INSIDE THE RECORD'S CELL EXTENT in the first structure count after the
#                      completion (rooms_in_box), none there before the deploy; missing room data is unknown.
#   NO HARM (WATCH, DiD of success shares; fails only below its tolerance with z < -2; UNEXPOSED under MIN_CELL)
#     gather_ok, rung_ok (wooden/stone/iron pickaxe crafts); litter cells (structcount, canary vs control towns, reported);
#     deaths: the default gate (two-death floor); immobile: immobiledid
#   SH1 kept_thrown (storehub-01 pieces A + B, 11-04; bots/src/storehub.mjs): THE HALL CLERK'S WELL THROWS, judged by the keep lists.
#                      A `_well_dispose` row tagged `via=clerk` (skills.mjs disposeWell `via: 'clerk'`) whose items= names a kind, or a
#                      `_storehub_clerk` row whose junked= (every name the well reported lost, the row's second field) names a kind, that
#                      is OFF HUB_THROWABLE or ON HUB_NEVER_THROW (the module's lists, copied here; --selftest asserts the copy against
#                      bots/src/storehub.mjs when that file is beside the repo). Counted as kept_thrown AND as a bag_loss item (a lost
#                      material: the showcase tripwire's H6 TRIPs on it with no change to showcase_trip.py). Every other well row (a
#                      bot's own junkout, the peaceful sword rule) is not this gate's. A via=clerk well row whose items= the logger cut
#                      (no lost= and no whole items= field) is kept_unjudged, reported as a bag_unjudged item (the tripwire's report).
#                      The bot writes `via=clerk lost=<name:n,...>` as the row's first two fields (skills.mjs disposeWell viaOf), so the
#                      cut cannot reach them.
#                      THE HALL is an ordinary blueprint record (project=storehub): its `_build` placements and clears are G1/G2's like
#                      every project's. Its SIGNS (`_hub_sign op=place at=`, sortstore's label writer) are G1 too: the cell above a hall
#                      chest of a generation of the storehub chain (`chests[].sign`), else off_record. `_hub_sign op=edit` places nothing.
#                      The hub's rows never feed G6 (a clerk at the well is not a builder's stand).
#   WX1 wall_ext (wallext-01, 11-04; bots/src/wall.mjs siteWallExt): THE WALL EXTENSION is an ordinary record (project=wallext) judged
#                      like the town wall (WALL_PLOTS): its placements are G1's (a record cell, or the wall's footing 1..3 under a column's
#                      lowest record cell: footing_ok), its clears G2's (its clear list, a foreign dig of its own cell, the cap over a
#                      sealed column: overburden_ok any role). Nothing of the OLD wall is the extension's: the old wall's cells are not on
#                      the extension's record, so an extension placement onto, or a dig of, an old wall cell is off_record / clear_outside
#                      (the old section is LEFT standing by design: no removal rule exists, and none is granted).
#   WD1 wall_demo (wallext-02, 11-04, owner O1 "remove left over blocks"; bots/src/wall.mjs demoCells / demoRecord): THE OLD LINE'S
#                      DEMOLITION (project=walldemo) is the only project that digs old wall cells. A `_build op=clear project=walldemo`
#                      dig is on the record ONLY when all of: kind=demolish; the cell is on a generation of the walldemo chain's clear
#                      list; it is a cell (or a `retired` cell) of a generation of the OLD WALL's chain; its column is one of the
#                      demolition record's line columns (`cols`) and it lies 0..3 in from that line (the record's `line`: axis, line,
#                      inward); and a generation of the WALLEXT chain stood complete (completeAt) at or before the dig (+ 60 s skew).
#                      Anything else is clear_outside (` demo`). The walldemo record has no cells: any placement of it is off_record.
#   CF1 cane_cut (canefarm-01, 11-04; bots/src/canefarm.mjs; counted as clear_outside, so G2's line trips on it): THE CANE BED's
#                      placements are G1's (a record cell, role cane). Its HARVEST (`_build op=harvest project=canefarm cell= block=
#                      sugar_cane kind=cane`) is on the record ONLY as sugar cane cut at the SECOND block: the cell directly above a
#                      cane cell of any generation of the bed's chain. The bottom block itself, any other cell, any other block is
#                      clear_outside (` harvest`). A harvest row is a mutation (G3), as the crop farm's.
#   DC1-3 decor (decorator-01, 11-04; bots/src/decorator.mjs): THE DECORATOR's sink (project=decor: flower borders and door pots) is a
#                      growing sink record (SINK_PLOTS): its placements are G1's and its clears G2's against the generations in force,
#                      plus: DC1 meal -- a `_build op=meal` (bone meal used on the nursery) only on a NURSERY centre (`nursery.at` or a
#                      `nurseries[].at`) of a generation in force, else off_record (` meal`), a mutation G3 judges; DC2 bloom -- a
#                      `_build op=clear kind=bloom` (the nursery's harvest) only a flower or the grass a meal leaves (DECOR_BLOOMS) within
#                      DECOR_SPREAD columns of a nursery centre in force, never a record cell or a marker that stood then (tidy_guard: the
#                      decor's own border flowers too: ` guarded`), never in the park's box + 1 (` in_park`) or on a cell of the tidy head
#                      in force (` tidy_cell`), else clear_outside (` bloom`); DC4 fern (decorator-02, owner 11-05) -- a `_build op=clear
#                      kind=fern` (the fern gather, shears) only a fern or a large fern within DECOR_FERN_R columns of the decor record's
#                      home, never guarded, in the park + 1 or a tidy head cell, else clear_outside (` fern_rule`); DC5 leaves (decorator-03)
#                      -- a `_build op=clear kind=leaves` (the hedge's leaves, shears) the same rule over DECOR_LEAVES (` leaves_rule`); DC6 wild
#                      (flowers-01, owner 11-07) -- a `_build op=clear kind=wild` (the wild flower pick, by hand) the same rule over the
#                      small flowers (DECOR_SMALL) within DECOR_WILD_R of home (` wild_rule`); the protected villages are G3's; DC7 lawn
#                      (flowers-02, owner 11-07) -- a `_build op=meal kind=lawn` (the lawn scatter's one bone meal) only ON a lawn centre
#                      (`lawns[].at`) of a generation in force, never a nursery's, else off_record (` lawn_meal`); a nursery meal
#                      (no kind=lawn) is never a lawn's. Every
#                      decor refusal ENDS in a tag the showcase tripwire's tree-debris split cannot read as a block (Claude r1 BLOCKER: ` fern`
#                      made a stone cleared under kind=fern a WATCH); the selftest checks every tag against showcase_trip.py's TREE_DEBRIS; DC3 pot -- a `_build op=place kind=pot_fill` only
#                      on a POT cell (role D<n>-pot) of a generation in force with a potted block (potted_*), else off_record (` pot`).
#   EF1 enchant_fit (enchantshop-01, 11-04; bots/src/enchantshop.mjs): THE ENCHANTING WORKSHOP is an ordinary record (project=
#                      enchantshop): its shell's placements and clears are G1/G2's like every project's. Its FIT-OUT (the anvil, the
#                      enchanting table, 15 bookshelves) is NOT record cells: the record's `fit` list [{slot, item, at}], placed by the
#                      fitter one cell at a time (buildrun's visit on a one-cell record: `_build op=place project=enchantshop seg=fit
#                      block=<item>`). A placement at a `fit` cell of any generation of the chain is on the record ONLY with that slot's
#                      own item (an anvil slot takes an anvil; a shelf slot a bookshelf); any other block there, or a fit item anywhere
#                      else, is off_record. Its labels (`_enchantshop_sign op=place at=`, sortstore's writer) are G1 too: a `signs[].at`
#                      cell of a generation of the chain, else off_record, judged only once placed (the hall signs' rule).
#   SW1 stepwell_seal (stepwell-01, 11-05; bots/src/stepwell.mjs, bots/src/stepwellvisit.mjs): THE STEPWELL is an ordinary record
#                      (project=stepwell): its cut is its `clear` list (kind=cut digs), its stone its `cells` (floor, trim, wall, body,
#                      under, post, light, stand: placements, and kind=foreign digs of a bad block in one), its felling the clear list's
#                      kind=tree/leaves entries with the record's `replant` (runVisit's rows: G2 and G5 as for every record). ONE
#                      addition: a SEAL -- `_build op=place project=stepwell kind=seal` -- is on the record in a CUT cell (kind cut) of any
#                      generation of the stepwell chain too (a liquid met in the volume is sealed by a block, which the cut then digs);
#                      any other placement in a cut cell, or a seal anywhere else off the record, is off_record (` seal`).
#                      stepwell-02 (the descent): a record GROWS -- a stepwell-01 record gains its descent as a later generation, and
#                      the court wall's portal cells move from `cells` to `clear` -- so its CLEARS are judged over the chain too: a
#                      kind=foreign dig on a cell of any generation, any other dig on a clear entry of any generation (` stepwell`).
#   FR1-FR4 forest (forest-01, 11-06, OWNER "remove the tree farm from inside city walls ... when they cut down a tree outside the walls
#                      replant in an clean organized way"; bots/src/forest.mjs, skills.mjs buildForest): THE MANAGED FOREST outside the walls is
#                      an ordinary record (project=forest): its floor fills, soil and SUNK saplings are G1's (record cells), its cuts G2's (its
#                      clear list) and a foreign dig of a plot's floor G2's (its own cell). Plus: FR1 fell -- `_build op=harvest project=forest
#                      kind=grown|site` (a log dug from the plot's stand) is on the record ONLY as a *_log block in a PLOT COLUMN of a
#                      generation (the plot cell up to FOREST_TRUNK above it: kind grown), or one of a generation's listed SITE-TREE logs
#                      (`trees[].logs`: kind site); anything else is clear_outside (` forest_fell`); a `*_failed` row digs nothing and is not
#                      judged; a felling is a mutation (G3). FR2 leaves -- `op=clear kind=stand|aisle` (the leaves in a stand's or an aisle's
#                      feet and head) only a *_leaves block inside a generation's box + 1, from its level - 3 to its levelMax + 5 (forest-02: strips at their own levels; levelMax absent: level), else clear_outside
#                      (` forest_leafrule`). FR4 meal -- `op=use kind=bonemeal` only on a PLOT cell of a generation, else off_record
#                      (` forest_meal`), a mutation (G3). No-change rows (op=sited|waived|rotate|sweep|garden_sited) are no mutation.
#   RF1 farm_retire (forest-01): THE IN-WALL TREE FARM'S RETIREMENT (project=treefarm, its rows written by the forest's keeper): op=harvest
#                      kind=retire -- a *_log in a plot column of a generation of the farm's chain (cells or `retired`); op=clear kind=retire
#                      -- a *_sapling on a plot cell or a torch on a light cell of a generation; op=clear kind=stand -- a *_leaves block in a
#                      generation's box (or `was`) + 1, up to 3 above its plots; anything else clear_outside (` farm_retire`). The retired head
#                      (bots/src/forest.mjs farmRetired: cells [] and `retired` / `retiredClear` / `retiredAt`) judges the farm's EARLIER rows
#                      as the wall's retirement does (a row before retiredAt also against the retired cells and clears: rec_pre), and its
#                      cells stop being another project's (tidy_guard) from ITS OWN retiredAt -- the garden laid on its ground is the
#                      groundskeeper's then (the wall's retired cells wait for the demolition's completion; the farm has no demolition).
#   RO1 reorient (townstyle-01, 11-04; bots/src/buildrun.mjs misorientedAt, bots/src/townstyle.mjs): A STATE-BEARING CELL'S OWN
#                      BLOCK IN THE WRONG STATE (a stair facing away, a log on its side) is dug and placed again aimed: `_build op=clear
#                      kind=reorient`. On the record ONLY when the cell is a record cell that names a world state (`ws`) in a generation of
#                      the plot's chain, the block dug is an oriented block (stairs, slab, log/wood/stem/hyphae, trapdoor, lantern) AND it
#                      is one of THAT cell's role names in THAT generation's own palette (the styled record's `palette`; a generation with
#                      no palette authorizes nothing: Codex r1 BLOCKER -- a post cell never authorizes digging a trapdoor or another wood).
#                      Anything else is clear_outside (` reorient`): a reorient of a stateless cell, of another block, or off the record.
#                      Its placements are G1's like every record's (the cell); the place row's `st=` (the state made) is reported only.
#   RT1 retrofit (townstyle-03, 11-05; bots/src/reroof.mjs): A RE-ROOFED BUILDING is a new generation of ITS OWN record that keeps every
#                      old cell (the flat roof stays, as the ceiling) and adds the pitched roof above it -- nothing is dug, so no new
#                      rule: its placements are G1's against the head record (the new roof's cells and the old cells both on it), a
#                      misoriented new stair's dig is RO1's (the generation carries its own `palette`). The selftest pins it: a
#                      placement one above the new roof is off_record; a reroof dig of an OLD flat-roof cell is clear_outside.
#   RT2 reroof digs (reroof-02, 11-06; bots/src/reroof.mjs reroofObstacles): a retrofit generation may LIST digs in its head record's
#                      `clear`: what stands in the new roof's cells. kind=decor_post (a decorator ornament -- a lantern post's fence
#                      and lantern, a pot, a flower, a hedge -- taken back by the retrofit) is clean only when the head lists THAT cell
#                      with that kind and that block AND a decor generation RETIRED that cell by this retrofit (by=reroof:<plot>); else
#                      clear_outside (` decor_post`). kind=leaves (canopy) and kind=foreign (scaffold clutter in a roof cell) need no
#                      new rule: a listed clear (G2's list), a foreign dig of the head's own open cell (G2 as before). The decor
#                      record's `_build op=retired project=decor` row judges nothing. A DOORSTEP (`seg=reroof-earth`, role fill,
#                      cobblestone) is a cell of the head (G1). The selftest pins each and its positive control.
#   CR2 copper trips (copperrun-02, 11-06; bots/src/copperhall.mjs copperRunOrder, skills.mjs copperTripVisit): the copper hall's
#                      trip to a known copper find logs `gather` rows of copper_ore / deepslate_copper_ore far from town (the
#                      tunnel's digs are gather's own, never `_build` rows) and one `_copper_trip` row (not a ROW_KIND: never judged).
#                      NO NEW RULE: a `gather` row feeds only the arms counters (gather_<per>_<arm>), never H1-H7. The selftest pins
#                      it (the rows reach the judge, no gate moves) with a mutant that judges a gather row.
#   MK1-MK3 the market (market-01, 11-06, OWNER "what should the bots unlock first?" -> live villager trading; bots/src/market.mjs,
#                      skills.mjs marketRun / marketJob): THE MARKET'S rows are judged ahead of every build-row rule (never G6, never the
#                      arms line), on the canary arm after the cut (the showcase: every row). MK1 job -- `_market_job op=placed block= at=`
#                      (a job block a bot crafted and placed beside free jobless villagers; claim / skip rows place nothing): the block one
#                      of MARKET_BLOCKS (the six makeable job sites), else off_record (` market_block`); the cell OUTSIDE every protected
#                      zone box grown by MARKET_PAD on each side, else zone (` market_job`); at most MARKET_PER_DAY placements per pool in
#                      any 24 h, else off_record (` market_cap`). MK2 run -- `_market_run ... at=` (the merchant where the window opened):
#                      outside every zone box + MARKET_PAD (a run never walks into the village), else zone (` market_run`); spent= at most
#                      MARKET_SPEND emeralds, else bag_loss (` MK2 market_spend`). MK3 sale -- `_trade op=sell item=` only one of
#                      MARKET_GOODS (renewable or surplus goods; never diamonds, lapis, redstone, tools, books), else bag_loss
#                      (` MK3 market_sold`, a kept material sold: H6, which the showcase tripwire REPORTS; a zone line TRIPS). `_trade op=buy` and the
#                      `_trade_seen` row change nothing (not a ROW_KIND). The water bucket a run fills takes one source of open water whose
#                      8 neighbours are sources (it refills at once): no record cell, no rule. The lists are copied from market.mjs; the
#                      selftest asserts the copy when bots/src/market.mjs is beside this file's repo.
#   MK1-MK2 market-02 (11-07, OWNER D1-D2): beds at a village's edge are market job rows too (`block=<colour>_bed`, one row per half,
#                      the head's `why=bed_head`): outside the zone + MARKET_PAD like every market cell (zone); beds count by their foot
#                      against MARKET_BEDS_PER_DAY, apart from the job blocks' MARKET_PER_DAY; a composter is a market job block. A
#                      WALK-IN trade (`_market_run ... walkin=1 zone=<id>`, a market_trip's) may stand INSIDE a village zone: counted
#                      (market_walkins), never zone -- presence is not a change; a run without walkin=1 there is still zone (TRIP).
# REPORTED, NOT A GATE: G7 placed_twice (a cell placed twice within 10 min: physically a re-placement after the cell was emptied
#   -- a repair or a survival dig -- never two blocks in one cell; read r1 both engines).
#   F2/F4 rows (owner 10-26: every replant skip a counted row): replants moved / deferred / skipped, cells deferred, STUCK ON CELL,
#   the stuck map unwritten, and STALLED POOLS -- a stuck_on_cell row with stalled=true (every open segment waits on the stuck
#   cell: an earth cell stops the whole workshop). A stalled pool is NOT "no effect": it is named per pool with the cells waiting,
#   and its build outcome is read as stuck, never as a null result (Claude r22 P2-2).
import sys, os, json, re, glob, hashlib, time
import datetime as dt
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from townplanread import (kv, pool_of, read_plans, zones_of, in_box, did_z, ck, MIN_CELL)  # noqa: E402

TOL = -0.20
RUNGS = ('wooden_pickaxe', 'stone_pickaxe', 'iron_pickaxe')
BUILDERS_MAX = 2
RECLAIM_MS = 60_000
PLACE_TWICE_MIN = 10
EXPOSE_TOWNS = 2
CONFORM_F1 = 0.98
CONFORM_GAP_MIN = 60
SAVE_LAG_MIN = 6
SURVEY_DIR = os.path.expanduser('~/shopconform/surveys')
# storehub-01: the hall clerk's rows and the hall's labels (SH1, G1 for signs); in BUILD_KINDS so the showcase tripwire loads them
# (showcase_trip.py shop_kinds), judged ahead of every build-row rule (never G6, never the arms line)
HUB_KINDS = ('_well_dispose', '_storehub_clerk', '_hub_sign', '_enchantshop_sign')   # enchantshop-01 EF1: the workshop's labels too
# market-01 MK1-MK3 (bots/src/market.mjs MARKET / GOODS / JOB_RECIPES, copied; the selftest asserts the copy)
MARKET_KINDS = ('_market_job', '_market_run', '_trade')
MARKET_BLOCKS = frozenset(['smithing_table', 'barrel', 'fletching_table', 'stonecutter', 'cartography_table', 'grindstone', 'composter'])
MARKET_BED = re.compile(r'^[a-z_]+_bed$')   # market-02: beds at a village's edge (both halves are rows: the head's says why=bed_head)
MARKET_GOODS = frozenset(['iron_ingot', 'coal', 'stone', 'stick', 'paper', 'glass_pane', 'wheat', 'potato', 'carrot', 'beetroot', 'pumpkin',
                          'melon', 'flint', 'clay_ball', 'andesite', 'granite', 'diorite', 'dried_kelp_block', 'sweet_berries', 'hay_block',
                          'water_bucket'])
MARKET_PAD = 3
MARKET_PER_DAY = 2
MARKET_BEDS_PER_DAY = 2                 # market-02 WALKIN.bedsPerDay (beds, by their FOOT rows)
MARKET_SPEND = 8
BUILD_KINDS = ('_build_mode', '_build', '_build_seg', '_build_visit', '_build_bag', '_structure_complete') + HUB_KINDS + MARKET_KINDS
ROW_KINDS = BUILD_KINDS + ('craft', 'gather')
# storehub-01 (bots/src/storehub.mjs HUB_THROWABLE, HUB_NEVER_THROW, copied; the selftest asserts the copy)
HUB_PLOT = 'storehub'
HUB_THROWABLE = frozenset(['egg', 'brown_egg', 'blue_egg', 'flint', 'clay_ball', 'ink_sac', 'glow_ink_sac', 'armadillo_scute', 'dead_bush',
                           'pointed_dripstone', 'rail', 'andesite', 'diorite', 'granite', 'mossy_cobblestone'])
HUB_NEVER_THROW = re.compile(r'(_(pickaxe|axe|shovel|hoe|sword)$|_ore$|^raw_|_ingot$|_nugget$|_log$|_wood$|_planks$|_stem$|^stick$|^(coal|charcoal|coal_block)$|^(cobblestone|cobbled_deepslate|stone|stone_bricks|smooth_stone|deepslate|tuff|blackstone|sandstone|red_sandstone|bricks?)$|glass|copper|_sapling$|_propagule$|^bamboo|^(diamond|emerald|lapis_lazuli|redstone|quartz)$|^(torch|chest|crafting_table|furnace|bucket|water_bucket|shears|flint_and_steel)$)')
NAMES_RE = re.compile(r'([a-z0-9_]+):(\d+)')


def hub_kept(name):
    """Is `name` a KEPT kind -- one the hall clerk never throws? (storehub.mjs hubKept: off the allowlist, or on the never-list.)"""
    return name not in HUB_THROWABLE or bool(HUB_NEVER_THROW.search(name))


def hub_names(field):
    """A row's `name:n,name:n` field -> {name: n}; '-' or empty -> {}."""
    return {m.group(1): int(m.group(2)) for m in NAMES_RE.finditer(field or '') if int(m.group(2)) > 0}


# enchantshop-01 EF1 (bots/src/enchantshop.mjs ENCHANT_PLOT, the record's `fit` and `signs`)
ENCHANT = 'enchantshop'


# townstyle-01 RO1: the blocks a click orients (bots/src/townstyle.mjs STAIRS SLAB AXIS TRAPDOOR LANTERN)
ORIENTED_RE = re.compile(r'^(minecraft:)?([a-z_]+_(stairs|slab|log|wood|stem|hyphae|trapdoor)|(soul_)?lantern)$')


def role_names(palette, role, seen=None):
    """A styled record's palette role -> its concrete names (a '$role' expanded, '$planks' kept as the planks wildcard). Pure."""
    seen = set() if seen is None else seen
    out = []
    for n in (palette or {}).get(role) or []:
        n = str(n)
        if n == '$planks' or not n.startswith('$'):
            out.append(n)
        elif n[1:] not in seen:
            seen.add(n[1:])
            out.extend(role_names(palette, n[1:], seen))
    return out


REROOF_DECOR_KIND = 'decor_post'


def reroof_decor_ok(rec, decor_chain, cell, block, plot=None):
    """reroof-02 RT2: is a retrofit's kind=decor_post dig listed in the head's clear (that cell, that kind, that block) AND a decor
    ornament cell this retrofit RETIRED (a generation's `retired`, by == 'reroof:<plot>': the bot retires before it lists -- Claude r1
    tightening: an ornament never handed over is never dug)? -> bool. Pure."""
    k = ck(cell)
    name = re.sub(r'^minecraft:', '', str(block or ''))
    if not any(isinstance(c, dict) and ck(c.get('at')) == k and c.get('kind') == REROOF_DECOR_KIND and c.get('name') == name for c in (rec or {}).get('clear') or []):
        return False
    return any(isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at') and ck(c['at']) == k and c.get('by') == 'reroof:%s' % plot for g in decor_chain or [] for c in g.get('retired') or [])


def reorient_ok(chain, cell, block):
    """townstyle-01 RO1: a `_build op=clear kind=reorient` -> bool. Pure. The cell is a STATE-BEARING record cell (`ws`, a non-empty
    object) of a generation of the plot's chain, the block dug is an oriented block, and it is one of that cell's role names in that
    generation's own palette."""
    b = re.sub(r'^minecraft:', '', str(block or ''))
    if not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 3 or not ORIENTED_RE.match(b):
        return False
    k = ck(cell)
    for rec in chain or []:
        pal = rec.get('palette')
        if not isinstance(pal, dict):
            continue
        for c in rec.get('cells') or []:
            if not (isinstance(c, dict) and isinstance(c.get('at'), list) and isinstance(c.get('ws'), dict) and c['ws'] and ck(c['at']) == k):
                continue
            names = role_names(pal, c.get('role'))
            if b in names or ('$planks' in names and b.endswith('_planks')):
                return True
    return False


def enchant_place_ok(rec, cell, block):
    """enchantshop-01 EF1: a `project=enchantshop op=place` -> bool. Pure. A record cell (the shell), or a `fit` slot's cell with THAT
    slot's own item (any other block on a slot, or a fit item off its slot, is off the record)."""
    k = ck(cell)
    if k in {ck(c['at']) for c in rec.get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')}:
        return True
    return any(isinstance(s, dict) and isinstance(s.get('at'), list) and ck(s['at']) == k and s.get('item') == block for s in rec.get('fit') or [])


def enchant_sign_ok(chain, cell):
    """enchantshop-01 EF1: is `cell` a label cell (`signs[].at`) of the workshop in ANY generation of its chain? Pure."""
    k = ck(cell)
    return any(isinstance(s, dict) and isinstance(s.get('at'), list) and ck(s['at']) == k for rec in chain or [] for s in rec.get('signs') or [])


def hub_sign_ok(chain, cell):
    """Is `cell` the label cell (the cell above a chest) of a hall chest in ANY generation of the storehub chain?"""
    k = ck(cell)
    return any(isinstance(c, dict) and isinstance(c.get('sign'), list) and ck(c['sign']) == k for rec in chain or [] for c in rec.get('chests') or [])
# EVERY PROJECT'S RECORD (2026-10-29, house-01 + monument-01: the town builds a workshop, a house and a ziggurat, each its own
# record chain `project-<home>.<plot>`; this read knew only `.shop`, so all 134 placements of the showcase's house read off_record
# and S6 stopped the bots 13:35Z). A `_build*` row names its project (`project=<plot>`); it is judged against THAT record.
REC_RE = re.compile(r'^(project-(-?\d+)_(-?\d+)_(-?\d+)\.([A-Za-z0-9]+))\.g(\d+)\.json$')
LEASE_RE = re.compile(r'^(project-(-?\d+)_(-?\d+)_(-?\d+)\.([A-Za-z0-9]+))\.(keeper|helper\d+|slot\d+|seg-[A-Za-z0-9-]+)\.lease\.g(\d+)\.json$')   # wall-F: upper case (Claude read r1 P2-1); gkhelpers-01: the crew's leases
SHOP = 'shop'
TIDY = 'tidy'                         # bots/src/tidy.mjs TIDY_PLOT
LAND = 'land'                         # bots/src/landplan.mjs LAND_PLOT (a reservation: no cells)
PATHS = 'paths'                       # bots/src/paths.mjs PATHS_PLOT (outbands-01: a replant never on a path)
# gkhelpers-01 (bots/src/groundskeeper.mjs GK.helpersMax / GK.segGap; park.mjs GATHER_NAMES)
CREW_HELPERS_MAX = 2
CREW_SEG_GAP = 2
CREW_YIELD_MS = 10_000
CREW_EPOCH_MS = 1793664000000          # 2026-11-03T00:00Z: before any helper existed (the showcase's tidy had one builder: slot1)
GATHER_BLOCKS = frozenset(['poppy', 'dandelion', 'cornflower', 'oxeye_daisy', 'azure_bluet', 'allium', 'blue_orchid', 'lily_of_the_valley',
                           'red_tulip', 'orange_tulip', 'white_tulip', 'pink_tulip', 'rose_bush', 'lilac', 'peony', 'sunflower', 'azalea',
                           'flowering_azalea', 'sugar_cane', 'bamboo', 'oak_leaves', 'birch_leaves', 'spruce_leaves', 'azalea_leaves',
                           'flowering_azalea_leaves', 'cherry_leaves', 'jungle_leaves', 'acacia_leaves', 'dark_oak_leaves', 'mangrove_leaves'])
# cropfarm-01 (bots/src/cropfarm.mjs CROPFARM_PLOT, CROP_BLOCKS, SEED_GRASS, CROP.gatherR)
CROPFARM = 'cropfarm'
CANEFARM = 'canefarm'                 # canefarm-01 CF1 (bots/src/canefarm.mjs CANEFARM_PLOT)
STEPWELL = 'stepwell'                 # stepwell-01 SW1 (bots/src/stepwell.mjs STEPWELL_PLOT)
FOREST = 'forest'                     # forest-01 FR1-FR4 (bots/src/forest.mjs FOREST_PLOT)
TREEFARM = 'treefarm'                 # forest-01 RF1 (bots/src/treefarm.mjs TREEFARM_PLOT, retired by the forest's keeper)
FOREST_TRUNK = 12                     # bots/src/forest.mjs FOREST.trunkMax: a plot column is the plot cell and this many above
LOG_RE = re.compile(r'^[a-z_]+_log$')
LEAVES_RE = re.compile(r'^[a-z_]+_leaves$')
CROP_BLOCKS = frozenset(['wheat', 'carrots', 'potatoes', 'beetroots'])
SEED_GRASS = frozenset(['short_grass', 'tall_grass', 'fern', 'large_fern'])
SEEDGRASS_R = 24
MARKERS_RE = re.compile(r'^expedition-.*\.markers\.jsonl$')
BRIDGES_RE = re.compile(r'^expedition-.*\.bridges\.jsonl$')        # bridgepost-01 (bots/src/bridge.mjs): the town's bridge sites
# what a build visit may lose to the pathfinder's own towers and bridges (bots/src/scaffold.mjs PATHFINDER_SCAFFOLD's stone and
# dirt family, and mineflayer-pathfinder's seeds dirt and cobblestone): the ziggurat places and digs these
SCAFFOLD_NAMES = {'cobblestone'} | {'%s_planks' % w for w in ('oak', 'spruce', 'birch', 'jungle', 'acacia', 'dark_oak', 'mangrove', 'cherry', 'pale_oak', 'bamboo', 'crimson', 'warped')} \
    | {'dirt', 'coarse_dirt', 'rooted_dirt', 'stone', 'andesite', 'diorite', 'granite', 'deepslate', 'cobbled_deepslate', 'tuff', 'netherrack', 'sandstone', 'red_sandstone', 'dripstone_block'}
BAG_RE = re.compile(r'(?:^| )([a-z_]+)=(-?\d+)/(-?\d+)/(-?\d+)/(-?\d+)/(-?\d+)/(-?\d+)')


def tidy_ok(rec, cell):
    """zigtidy-01: is a `kind=tidy` clear inside its record's tidy space? -> bool. Pure."""
    cells = rec.get('cells') or []
    box = rec.get('box')
    if not cells or not isinstance(box, list) or len(box) != 4 or not isinstance(rec.get('baseY'), int):
        return False
    if ck(cell) in {ck(c['at']) for c in cells}:
        return False
    top = max(c['at'][1] for c in cells)
    x, y, z = cell
    return box[0] - 1 <= x <= box[2] + 1 and box[1] - 1 <= z <= box[3] + 1 and rec['baseY'] <= y <= top + 2


def tidy_clear_ok(chain, cell, kind, t_ms, guard):
    """tidyread-01 (11-03): is a `project=tidy` clear one the tidy record planned? -> bool. Pure.
    chain: every generation of the tidy record's chain, root first (record_chain). A stage is one generation (tidy.mjs siteStage),
    so a cut planned in stage 0 stays legitimate after the head moves on to stage 1. Planned = a cell of any generation's `cut`
    list (kind=tidy: the pillar and grading cuts) or `clear` list (the road's leaves and trunks); kind=foreign: a cell of any
    generation's `cells` (the fill cells). guard: {cell key: ms it was first reserved} -- every OTHER project's record cell and every
    expedition marker block. A cell guarded at or before the clear (t_ms) is never the tidy's, planned or not (buildTidy's own dig
    guard: the projects' cells + the markers); a guard that came later (a house sited on a dug pillar) does not re-judge it."""
    k = ck(cell)
    if tidy_guarded(cell, t_ms, guard):
        return False
    lists = ('cells',) if kind in ('foreign', 'cover') else ('cut', 'clear')   # coverread-01: a cover is a foreign dig of an open cell
    return any(k == ck(c['at']) for rec in chain or [] for name in lists for c in rec.get(name) or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at'))


def gather_ok(chain, cell, block, t_ms, guard, park_box):
    """gkhelpers-01: is a `project=tidy op=clear kind=gather` (the park gather, bots/src/skills.mjs parkGather) a plant the gather may
    take? -> (bool, why). Pure. A GATHER_BLOCKS block; never guarded (another project's record cell or a marker when it was dug:
    tidy_guarded), never inside the park's box + 1 (park_box [x0, z0, x1, z1]: the land plan's), never a cell of any generation of the
    tidy chain (a cut or a clear-list leaf may be taken). The protected zones are G3's."""
    if block not in GATHER_BLOCKS:
        return False, ''
    guarded = tidy_guarded(cell, t_ms, guard)
    if guarded:
        return False, ' guarded'
    if isinstance(park_box, list) and len(park_box) == 4 and park_box[0] - 1 <= cell[0] <= park_box[2] + 1 and park_box[1] - 1 <= cell[2] <= park_box[3] + 1:
        return False, ' in_park'
    # a FILL cell of any generation (a park planting is one too) -- never the cut or clear lists: a head-room leaf the tidy itself
    # would clear is a plant the gather may take (Claude r1 BLOCKER 3)
    k = ck(cell)
    if any(k == ck(c['at']) for rec in chain or [] for c in rec.get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')):
        return False, ' tidy_cell'
    return True, ''


def crop_harvest_ok(chain, cell, block):
    """cropfarm-01: is a `project=cropfarm op=harvest` a crop block dug on a crop cell (role wheat) of any generation of the farm's
    record chain? -> bool. Pure."""
    if block not in CROP_BLOCKS:
        return False
    k = ck(cell)
    return any(c.get('role') == 'wheat' and ck(c['at']) == k for rec in chain or [] for c in rec.get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at'))


def cane_harvest_ok(chain, cell, block):
    """canefarm-01 CF1: is a `project=canefarm op=harvest` sugar cane cut at the SECOND block -- the cell directly above a cane cell
    (role cane) of any generation of the bed's chain? -> bool. Pure. The bottom block (the cane cell itself) is never the harvest's."""
    if block != 'sugar_cane' or not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 3:
        return False
    k = ck([cell[0], cell[1] - 1, cell[2]])
    return any(c.get('role') == 'cane' and ck(c['at']) == k for rec in chain or [] for c in rec.get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at'))


def plot_column_ok(chain, cell, top=FOREST_TRUNK):
    """forest-01: is `cell` in a PLOT COLUMN of a generation of the chain (a role-plot cell's column, from the cell up to `top`
    above it; a retired generation's `retired` cells too)? -> bool. Pure."""
    if not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 3:
        return False
    for rec in chain or []:
        for c in list(rec.get('cells') or []) + list(rec.get('retired') or []):
            if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('role') == 'plot' and isinstance(c.get('at'), list) and len(c['at']) == 3 \
                    and c['at'][0] == cell[0] and c['at'][2] == cell[2] and c['at'][1] <= cell[1] <= c['at'][1] + top:
                return True
    return False


def forest_harvest_ok(chain, cell, block, kind):
    """forest-01 FR1: is a `project=forest op=harvest` a log dug in a plot column of a generation (kind grown), or one of a generation's
    listed site-tree logs (kind site)? -> bool. Pure."""
    if not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 3 or not LOG_RE.match(str(block or '')):
        return False
    if kind == 'grown':
        return plot_column_ok(chain, cell)
    if kind == 'site':
        return any(isinstance(lg, list) and list(lg) == list(cell) for rec in chain or [] for t in rec.get('trees') or [] if isinstance(t, dict) for lg in t.get('logs') or [])
    return False


def forest_leaves_ok(chain, cell, block):
    """forest-01 FR2: is a `project=forest op=clear kind=stand|aisle` a leaves block inside a generation's box + 1, from its level - 3
    to its levelMax + 5 (forest-02: each strip at its own level; a forest-01 record has no levelMax: its level)? -> bool. Pure."""
    if not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 3 or not LEAVES_RE.match(str(block or '')):
        return False
    for rec in chain or []:
        b, w = rec.get('box'), rec.get('level')
        wm = rec.get('levelMax') if isinstance(rec.get('levelMax'), (int, float)) else w
        if isinstance(b, list) and len(b) == 4 and isinstance(w, (int, float)) and b[0] - 1 <= cell[0] <= b[2] + 1 and b[1] - 1 <= cell[2] <= b[3] + 1 \
                and w - 3 <= cell[1] <= wm + 5:
            return True
    return False


def forest_meal_ok(chain, cell):
    """forest-01 FR4: is a `project=forest op=use kind=bonemeal` on a plot cell of a generation? -> bool. Pure."""
    k = ck(cell) if isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) and len(cell) == 3 else None
    return k is not None and any(isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('role') == 'plot' and ck(c['at']) == k for rec in chain or [] for c in rec.get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at'))


def farm_retire_ok(chain, cell, block, kind):
    """forest-01 RF1: is a `project=treefarm` retirement dig on the farm's record -- kind retire: a *_log in a plot column of a
    generation, a *_sapling on a plot cell, a torch on a light cell; kind stand: leaves in a generation's box (or `was`) + 1, up to 3
    above its plots? -> bool. Pure."""
    if not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 3:
        return False
    name = str(block or '')
    k = ck(cell)
    cells = [c for rec in chain or [] for c in list(rec.get('cells') or []) + list(rec.get('retired') or []) if isinstance(c, dict) and isinstance(c.get('at'), list)]
    if kind == 'retire':
        if LOG_RE.match(name):
            return plot_column_ok(chain, cell)
        if re.match(r'^[a-z_]+_sapling$', name):
            return any(c.get('role') == 'plot' and ck(c['at']) == k for c in cells)
        if name == 'torch':
            return any(c.get('role') == 'light' and ck(c['at']) == k for c in cells)
        return False
    if kind == 'stand' and LEAVES_RE.match(name):
        tops = [c['at'][1] for c in cells if c.get('role') == 'plot']
        for rec in chain or []:
            b = rec.get('box') if isinstance(rec.get('box'), list) else rec.get('was')
            if isinstance(b, list) and len(b) == 4 and tops and b[0] - 1 <= cell[0] <= b[2] + 1 and b[1] - 1 <= cell[2] <= b[3] + 1 and min(tops) <= cell[1] <= max(tops) + 3:
                return True
    return False


def stepwell_place_ok(chain, cell, kind):
    """stepwell-01 SW1: is a `project=stepwell op=place` on the record -- a cell of any generation of the stepwell chain, or, for a
    kind=seal only, a CUT cell (its `clear` list, kind cut) of any generation? -> bool. Pure."""
    if not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 3:
        return False
    k = ck(cell)
    if any(ck(c['at']) == k for rec in chain or [] for c in rec.get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')):
        return True
    return kind == 'seal' and any(c.get('kind') == 'cut' and ck(c['at']) == k for rec in chain or [] for c in rec.get('clear') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at'))


def stepwell_clear_ok(chain, cell, kind):
    """stepwell-02 SW1: is a `project=stepwell op=clear` on the record -- kind=foreign on a cell of any generation of the chain, any
    other kind on a clear entry of any generation? -> bool. Pure. (The record grows: a portal cell was a cell, then a cut.)"""
    if not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 3:
        return False
    k = ck(cell)
    field = 'cells' if kind in ('foreign', 'cover') else 'clear'   # coverread-01: a cover is a foreign dig of an open cell
    return any(ck(c['at']) == k for rec in chain or [] for c in rec.get(field) or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at'))


def tidy_heads_at(chain, t_ms, skew_ms=60_000):
    """cropfarm-01: the tidy generations that may have been the HEAD when a dig happened at t_ms -> [gen]. Pure. The last one sited
    at or before t_ms (no `at`: in force) and every one sited within skew_ms after it (a clock skew: the head the bot read may be
    either -- Codex G9 r2 BLOCKER: the last in-force generation alone could be a FUTURE head). Their cells are judged together."""
    inf = sink_in_force(chain, t_ms, skew_ms)
    idx = max((i for i, g in enumerate(inf) if t_ms is None or not isinstance(g.get('at'), (int, float)) or g['at'] <= t_ms), default=None)
    return inf[idx:] if idx is not None else inf   # none sited before the dig: every one in the skew (Codex G9 r3)


def seedgrass_ok(chain, cell, block, t_ms, guard, park_box, tidy_chain=()):
    """cropfarm-01: is a `project=cropfarm op=clear kind=seedgrass` (the seed gather) a grass plant the gather may take? -> (bool, why).
    Pure. A SEED_GRASS block; never guarded (any project's record cell -- the farm's own too -- or a marker, when it was dug:
    tidy_guarded); never inside the park's box + 1; never a cell of the tidy generation in force when it was dug (`tidy_chain`: its
    fills -- the carried `prev` ones too --, cuts and clears, as the bot's gather guard reads the tidy head; tidy_guard leaves the
    tidy out -- Codex G9 r1 BLOCKER); within SEEDGRASS_R (Chebyshev) of the
    box of some generation of the farm's record. The protected zones are G3's."""
    if block not in SEED_GRASS:
        return False, ' not_grass'
    if tidy_guarded(cell, t_ms, guard):   # a record cell (the farm's own too) or a marker when it was dug
        return (False, ' guarded')
    if isinstance(park_box, list) and len(park_box) == 4 and park_box[0] - 1 <= cell[0] <= park_box[2] + 1 and park_box[1] - 1 <= cell[2] <= park_box[3] + 1:
        return (False, ' in_park')
    k = ck(cell)
    if any(k == ck(c['at']) for rec in tidy_chain or [] for name in ('cells', 'cut', 'clear') for c in rec.get(name) or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')):
        return (False, ' tidy_cell')
    boxes = [rec.get('box') for rec in chain or [] if isinstance(rec.get('box'), list) and len(rec['box']) == 4]
    x, z = cell[0], cell[2]
    if not any(max(b[0] - x, x - b[2], 0, b[1] - z, z - b[3]) <= SEEDGRASS_R for b in boxes):
        return False, ' far'
    return True, ''


def crew_overlaps(leases, chain, since_ms=None, helpers_max=CREW_HELPERS_MAX, gap=CREW_SEG_GAP, yield_ms=CREW_YIELD_MS, now_ms=None):
    """gkhelpers-01: THE GROUNDS CREW's lease rules over the tidy's leases -> [(name, gen, why)]. Pure.
    leases { name: [(gen, rec, released_ms)] } (read_projects); chain the tidy record's generations (a segment's columns are those of
    the generation in force when its lease was taken). Only leases taken at or after since_ms (the read's cut).
      - one holder on two crew leases at once (keeper and a helperN, or two helpers), each held [at, released or until]
      - a helperN with N > helpers_max
      - two holders on segments within `gap` columns (Chebyshev x, z) of each other, held together longer than yield_ms
    A lease still held is held up to NOW (now_ms, default the clock), never its future `until` (Codex r1 BLOCKER 2: a raced take
    read in the 50 ms before its yield measured 240 s of overlap)."""
    now_ms = int(time.time() * 1000) if now_ms is None else now_ms
    bad = []
    def spans(gens):
        """A lease's generations -> [(gen, rec, start, end)]: each held from its `at` to its release, else its `until` -- and never past
        the NEXT generation's `at` (a renewal supersedes it: only the newest generation is ever marked released; Claude r1 BLOCKERS
        1-2). A torn generation (no rec) is skipped."""
        gs = sorted(gens, key=lambda x: x[0])
        out_ = []
        for i, (g, r, rel) in enumerate(gs):
            if not r:
                continue
            end = int(rel) if rel is not None else int(r.get('until') or 0)
            nxt = next((int(r2.get('at') or 0) for _, r2, _ in gs[i + 1:] if r2), None)
            if nxt is not None:
                end = min(end, nxt)
            out_.append((g, r, int(r.get('at') or 0), min(end, now_ms)))
        return out_
    crew = []
    for name, gens in leases.items():
        m = re.match(r'^helper(\d+)$', name)
        if m and int(m.group(1)) > helpers_max:
            bad.append((name, None, 'helper beyond the crew cap %d' % helpers_max))
        if name == 'keeper' or m:
            for g, r, a, b in spans(gens):
                if since_ms is None or a >= since_ms:
                    crew.append((name, g, r.get('holder'), a, b))
    for i, (n0, g0, h0, a0, b0) in enumerate(crew):
        for n1, g1, h1, a1, b1 in crew[i + 1:]:
            if h0 == h1 and n0 != n1 and a0 < b1 and a1 < b0:
                bad.append((n1, g1, '%s held %s and %s at once' % (h1, n0, n1)))
    def cols_of(seg, at):
        rec = None
        for g in chain or []:
            if not isinstance(g.get('at'), (int, float)) or g['at'] <= at + 60_000:
                rec = g
        rec = rec or (chain[-1] if chain else None)
        return {(c['at'][0], c['at'][2]) for c in (rec or {}).get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('seg') == seg and not c.get('prev') and c.get('at')}
    segs = []
    for name, gens in leases.items():
        if not name.startswith('seg-') or name == 'seg-clear':
            continue
        for g, r, a, b in spans(gens):
            if since_ms is None or a >= since_ms:
                segs.append((name, g, r.get('holder'), a, b, cols_of(name[4:], a)))
    for i, (n0, g0, h0, a0, b0, c0) in enumerate(segs):
        for n1, g1, h1, a1, b1, c1 in segs[i + 1:]:
            if h0 == h1 or min(b0, b1) - max(a0, a1) <= yield_ms:
                continue
            if any(max(abs(x - u), abs(z - v)) <= gap for x, z in c0 for u, v in c1):
                bad.append((n1, g1, '%s on %s and %s on %s within %d columns for %d ms' % (h0, n0, h1, n1, gap, min(b0, b1) - max(a0, a1))))
    return bad


def tidy_place_ok(chain, cell, t_ms, skew_ms=60_000):
    """tidyread-02 (11-03, the terrace agent's replay): is a `project=tidy op=place` cell a fill of the tidy record IN FORCE when it
    was placed? -> bool. Pure. groundskeeper-01 publishes each land-plan stage as a NEW generation of the tidy record whose cells
    are that stage's only (it carries nothing over), so judged against the HEAD alone, stage 0's fills read off_record the moment
    stage 1 is sited while they are still in the read window. The row names no generation, so the generations in force are those
    sited at or before the placement (`at` <= t + skew: a visit that began under one stage may place after the next one is
    published); a generation with no `at` counts (the chain itself is validated by record_chain). A cell on none of them is still
    off_record. No time on the row: every generation of the chain."""
    for g in chain or []:
        at = g.get('at')
        if t_ms is not None and isinstance(at, (int, float)) and at > t_ms + skew_ms:
            continue
        if any(ck(c.get('at')) == ck(cell) for c in g.get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')):
            return True
    return False


# YARDREAD-01 (11-03, the showcase TRIP 07:35Z: H1 off_record 138, every one a cobblestone the yard clerk stocked on a PILE slot
# of the yard record -- piles are not record cells). The stock yard's geometry, as bots/src/yard.mjs pileSlots builds it from the
# blueprint's bays (bots/src/blueprints/yard@1.json, yard@2.json) and townplan.mjs rot; YARD_H = yard.mjs YARD_H.
# PICKER-01 (11-03): THE TOWN SINKS (bots/src/sinks.mjs SINK_PLOTS). Each plot's record GROWS: every sited instance (a road segment,
# a tower, a shrine) appends its cells, clears and zone to a new generation, and nothing is ever removed. A row is judged against the
# generations in force when it was written (sited at or before t + skew), so a placement or a dig ahead of its instance's siting is
# off the record -- Codex design 11-03: "validate H1/H2 against the generation authorizing the event"; never a blanket exemption.
# TOWNDRESS-01 (11-03): the fourth sink, `dress` (bots/src/towndress.mjs): batches of lamp posts, wall-top torches and signposts; each
# placed SIGN is logged `_build op=place project=dress kind=sign` on its record cell (the label writer, skills.mjs dressSignsVisit).
# DECORATOR-01 (11-04): the fifth sink, `decor` (bots/src/decorator.mjs), and its own rules DC1 (the nursery's meal), DC2 (its harvest),
# DC3 (a pot's fill). DECOR_SPREAD is decorator.mjs NURSERY.spread (bone meal's walk: at most 7 steps of one column); DECOR_BLOOMS its
# BLOOM_BLOCKS (the small flowers and the grass a meal leaves). --selftest asserts both against bots/src/decorator.mjs when it is beside.
SINK_PLOTS = ('road', 'tower', 'shrine', 'dress', 'decor')
DECOR = 'decor'
DECOR_SPREAD = 7
DECOR_BLOOMS = frozenset(['poppy', 'dandelion', 'cornflower', 'oxeye_daisy', 'azure_bluet', 'allium', 'blue_orchid', 'lily_of_the_valley',
                          'red_tulip', 'orange_tulip', 'white_tulip', 'pink_tulip', 'short_grass', 'tall_grass'])
DECOR_POT_ROLE = re.compile(r'^D\d+-pot$')
DECOR_FERN_R = 48   # decorator.mjs DECOR.fernR (decorator-02: the fern gather's reach from home)
# flowers-01 (DC6): the wild flower pick -- the small flowers (DECOR_BLOOMS less the grass), within decorator.mjs FLOWER.wildR of home
DECOR_WILD_R = 128
DECOR_SMALL = frozenset(DECOR_BLOOMS - {'short_grass', 'tall_grass'})
DECOR_FERNS = frozenset(['fern', 'large_fern'])
# decorator-03: the hedge's leaves (decorator.mjs HEDGE_LEAVES), cut by the same gather rule within DECOR_FERN_R of home (DC5)
DECOR_LEAVES = frozenset(['spruce_leaves', 'oak_leaves', 'birch_leaves', 'dark_oak_leaves', 'jungle_leaves', 'acacia_leaves', 'cherry_leaves', 'mangrove_leaves', 'azalea_leaves', 'flowering_azalea_leaves'])
# wallext-01 (WX1): the town wall and its extension are judged by the same wall rules (the footing, the cap over a sealed column)
WALL_PLOTS = ('wall', 'wallext')


def g5_pertree(chain, cell, t_ms):
    """PT1 (11-06): a felling under PER-TREE B4 (buildrun.mjs plotprep-03 `replantPerTree`: a tree waits only for ITS replants) -> None
    when not per-tree, else the most replants TAGGED to that tree (any state: a replant only ever becomes `skipped`, and one skipped
    after the felling was still owed when it fell -- Claude PT1 review BLOCKER 1) in ANY generation in force at t_ms that lists the
    cell as a tagged tree clear (never the newest alone: the bot may have held an older one -- BLOCKER 2). Pure. The row's
    saplings=a/b count the WHOLE record, so a < b there is no H5 on its own; 0 means the tree never owed a replant: clean."""
    k = ','.join(str(v) for v in cell)
    owed = None
    for g in sink_in_force(chain, t_ms):
        cl = [c for c in (g.get('clear') or []) + ((g.get('fell') or {}).get('clear') or []) if isinstance(c, dict)]
        # EVERY clear listing the cell (top-level and `fell`, Codex PT1 r2 BLOCKER: the first match alone could owe 0 while another owes one)
        hits = [c for c in cl if c.get('kind') == 'tree' and isinstance(c.get('at'), list) and ','.join(str(v) for v in c['at']) == k]
        if not hits:
            continue
        rp = [x for x in (g.get('replant') or []) + ((g.get('fell') or {}).get('replant') or []) if isinstance(x, dict)]
        if any(not isinstance(h.get('tree'), int) for h in hits) or not any(isinstance(x.get('tree'), int) for x in rp):
            return None   # a generation in force that lists the cell untagged is not per-tree: the old record-wide rule
        for hit in hits:
            n = len([x for x in rp if x.get('tree') == hit['tree'] and (x.get('plot') or None) == (hit.get('plot') or None)])
            owed = n if owed is None else max(owed, n)
    return owed


def g5_denominator_ok(chain, b, t_ms):
    """G5R: is a felling's sapling denominator `b` (saplings=a/b: the non-skipped replant entries of the record the bot held)
    the replant list of a generation in force at t_ms? Pure. A replant only ever becomes `skipped` (never back), so at felling
    time the non-skipped count lay between the generation's count of non-skipped entries NOW and its count of ALL entries."""
    for g in sink_in_force(chain, t_ms):
        rp = [x for x in g.get('replant') or [] if isinstance(x, dict)]
        if len([x for x in rp if x.get('state') != 'skipped']) <= b <= len(rp):
            return True
    return False


def sink_in_force(chain, t_ms, skew_ms=60_000):
    """picker-01: the generations of a sink chain in force at t_ms (sited at or before t + skew; no `at`: in force). Pure."""
    return [g for g in chain or [] if not (t_ms is not None and isinstance(g.get('at'), (int, float)) and g['at'] > t_ms + skew_ms)]


def sink_place_ok(chain, cell, t_ms):
    """picker-01: is a sink placement a cell of a generation in force? -> bool. Pure."""
    k = ck(cell)
    return any(ck(c.get('at')) == k for g in sink_in_force(chain, t_ms) for c in g.get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at'))


WALLDEMO = 'walldemo'
WALLDEMO_DEPTH = 3


DEMO_INWARD = {'N': 1, 'S': -1, 'W': 1, 'E': -1}


def demo_line_of(ext_chain):
    """wallext-02 WD1: the shared line, from the EXTENSION's record (never the demolition's own): its newest generation with a join
    and a side -> (axis, line, frm, to, inward) | None. Pure."""
    for g in reversed(ext_chain or []):
        j = g.get('join') or {}
        inward = DEMO_INWARD.get(g.get('side'))
        if j.get('axis') in ('x', 'z') and inward and all(isinstance(j.get(f), int) for f in ('line', 'from', 'to')):
            return j['axis'], j['line'], j['from'], j['to'], inward
    return None


def demo_clear_ok(demo_chain, wall_chain, ext_chain, cell, kind, t_ms, skew_ms=60_000):
    """wallext-02 WD1: is a walldemo dig a listed OLD WALL cell (never a foundation) of a built line column strictly inside the
    extension's join span, 0..WALLDEMO_DEPTH in from the line, dug after a generation of the extension stood complete? -> bool. Pure.
    The line, the span and the inward sign are the extension record's; the built line columns the old wall's own `cols`."""
    if kind != 'demolish' or not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 3 or t_ms is None:
        return False
    k = ck(cell)
    if not any(ck(c.get('at')) == k for g in demo_chain or [] for c in g.get('clear') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')):
        return False
    ln = demo_line_of(ext_chain)
    if ln is None:
        return False
    axis, line, frm, to, inward = ln
    along, across = (cell[0], cell[2]) if axis == 'z' else (cell[2], cell[0])
    if not (frm < along < to) or not (0 <= (across - line) * inward <= WALLDEMO_DEPTH):
        return False
    built = set()
    for g in wall_chain or []:
        for c in g.get('cols') or []:
            if not isinstance(c, dict):
                continue
            a, b = (c.get('x'), c.get('z')) if axis == 'z' else (c.get('z'), c.get('x'))
            if b == line and (c.get('s') in ('wall', 'post') or (c.get('s') == 'open' and c.get('why') == 'retired')):
                built.add(a)
    if along not in built:
        return False
    if not any(ck(c.get('at')) == k and c.get('role') != 'base' for g in wall_chain or [] for c in (g.get('cells') or []) + (g.get('retired') or []) if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')):
        return False
    return any(g.get('structure_complete') and isinstance(g.get('completeAt'), (int, float)) and g['completeAt'] <= t_ms + skew_ms for g in ext_chain or [])


def decor_nurseries(chain, t_ms):
    """decorator-01: every nursery centre a generation in force names (its `nursery`, and the `nurseries` it replaced) -> [[x, y, z]].
    Pure."""
    out = []
    for g in sink_in_force(chain, t_ms):
        for n in [g.get('nursery')] + list(g.get('nurseries') or []):
            if isinstance(n, dict) and isinstance(n.get('at'), list) and len(n['at']) == 3:
                out.append(n['at'])
    return out


def decor_lawns(chain, t_ms):
    """flowers-02 DC7: every lawn centre a generation in force names (its `lawns`) -> [[x, y, z]]. Pure."""
    return [n['at'] for g in sink_in_force(chain, t_ms) for n in g.get('lawns') or [] if isinstance(n, dict) and isinstance(n.get('at'), list) and len(n['at']) == 3]


def decor_lawn_meal_ok(chain, cell, t_ms):
    """flowers-02 DC7: is a lawn meal ON a lawn centre in force? -> bool. Pure."""
    return any(ck(a) == ck(cell) for a in decor_lawns(chain, t_ms))


def decor_meal_ok(chain, cell, t_ms):
    """decorator-01 DC1: is a meal ON a nursery centre in force? -> bool. Pure."""
    return any(ck(a) == ck(cell) for a in decor_nurseries(chain, t_ms))


def decor_bloom_ok(chain, cell, block, t_ms, guard, park_box=None, tidy_chain=()):
    """decorator-01 DC2: is a harvest a bloom (DECOR_BLOOMS) within DECOR_SPREAD columns (and DECOR_SPREAD + 1 blocks up or down: the
    walk climbs one a step, the plant stands one above its grass) of a nursery centre in force -- never a record cell or a marker that
    stood then (tidy_guarded), never in the park's box + 1, never a cell of the tidy generation in force (`tidy_chain`: tidy_heads_at;
    tidy_guard leaves the tidy out -- the seedgrass rule's lesson, Claude r1 BLOCKER)? -> (bool, why). Pure."""
    if block not in DECOR_BLOOMS or not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 3:
        return False, ' bloom'
    if tidy_guarded(cell, t_ms, guard):
        return False, ' guarded'
    if isinstance(park_box, list) and len(park_box) == 4 and park_box[0] - 1 <= cell[0] <= park_box[2] + 1 and park_box[1] - 1 <= cell[2] <= park_box[3] + 1:
        return False, ' in_park'
    k = ck(cell)
    if any(k == ck(c['at']) for rec in tidy_chain or [] for name in ('cells', 'cut', 'clear') for c in rec.get(name) or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')):
        return False, ' tidy_cell'
    ok = any(max(abs(cell[0] - a[0]), abs(cell[2] - a[2])) <= DECOR_SPREAD and abs(cell[1] - a[1]) <= DECOR_SPREAD + 1 for a in decor_nurseries(chain, t_ms))
    return (True, '') if ok else (False, ' bloom')


def decor_fern_ok(chain, cell, block, t_ms, guard, park_box=None, tidy_chain=(), names=DECOR_FERNS, tag=' fern_rule', r=DECOR_FERN_R):
    """decorator-02 DC4: is a fern gather's cut a fern or a large fern within DECOR_FERN_R columns of the decor record's home (a
    generation in force names it), never a record cell or marker that stood then, never in the park's box + 1, never a tidy head
    cell? -> (bool, why). Pure."""
    if block not in names or not isinstance(cell, (list, tuple)) or len(cell) != 3:
        return False, tag
    if tidy_guarded(cell, t_ms, guard):
        return False, ' guarded'
    if isinstance(park_box, list) and len(park_box) == 4 and park_box[0] - 1 <= cell[0] <= park_box[2] + 1 and park_box[1] - 1 <= cell[2] <= park_box[3] + 1:
        return False, ' in_park'
    k = ck(cell)
    if any(k == ck(c['at']) for rec in tidy_chain or [] for name in ('cells', 'cut', 'clear') for c in rec.get(name) or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')):
        return False, ' tidy_cell'
    # Claude r1 BLOCKER: the town's home from ANY generation (it is the town's config, constant) -- never only the ones in force (a
    # first visit's ferns may precede the record's first `at`); a home without numeric x and z is none (fail closed)
    homes = [g['home'] for g in chain or [] if isinstance(g.get('home'), dict) and all(isinstance(g['home'].get(a), (int, float)) for a in ('x', 'z'))]
    ok = any(max(abs(cell[0] - h['x']), abs(cell[2] - h['z'])) <= r for h in homes)
    return (True, '') if ok else (False, tag)


def decor_pot_ok(chain, cell, block, t_ms):
    """decorator-01 DC3: is a pot fill a potted block on a pot cell (role D<n>-pot) of a generation in force? -> bool. Pure."""
    if not str(block or '').startswith('potted_'):
        return False
    k = ck(cell)
    return any(ck(c.get('at')) == k and DECOR_POT_ROLE.match(str(c.get('role') or '')) for g in sink_in_force(chain, t_ms) for c in g.get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at'))


def sink_clear_ok(chain, cell, kind, t_ms):
    """picker-01: is a sink dig planned by a generation in force? -> bool. Pure. kind=foreign (a block in an open record cell):
    a cell; any other kind (tree, leaves, plant, cut): an entry of the clear list."""
    k = ck(cell)
    name = 'cells' if kind in ('foreign', 'cover') else 'clear'   # coverread-01: a cover is a foreign dig of an open cell
    return any(ck(c.get('at')) == k for g in sink_in_force(chain, t_ms) for c in g.get(name) or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at'))


YARD = 'yard'
YARD_H = 4
YARD_BAYS = {'yard@1': [((2, 3), (2, 12)), ((6, 7), (2, 12)), ((10, 11), (2, 12)), ((14, 15), (2, 12))],
             'yard@2': [((2, 3), (2, 23)), ((6, 7), (2, 23)), ((10, 11), (2, 23)), ((14, 15), (2, 23))]}
YARD_BULK = {'cobblestone', 'cobbled_deepslate', 'bamboo_block'}   # yard.mjs YARD_BULK


def yard_rot(orient, u, v):
    """townplan.mjs rot: (u, v) -> (dx, dz) for an orientation. Pure."""
    return {'E': (u, v), 'S': (-v, u), 'W': (-u, -v), 'N': (v, -u)}[orient]


def yard_slots(rec):
    """The yard record's pile slots (yard.mjs pileSlots) -> set of cell keys; empty when the record cannot say. Pure."""
    bays = YARD_BAYS.get(str(rec.get('blueprint') or ''))
    a, s0, o = rec.get('anchor'), rec.get('s'), rec.get('orient')
    if not bays or not isinstance(a, list) or len(a) < 2 or not isinstance(s0, int) or o not in ('E', 'S', 'W', 'N'):
        return set()
    out = set()
    for (u0, u1), (v0, v1) in bays:
        for k in range(1, YARD_H + 1):
            for v in range(v1, v0 + (k - 1) - 1, -1):
                for u in range(u0, u1 + 1):
                    dx, dz = yard_rot(o, u, v)
                    out.add(ck([a[0] + dx, s0 + k, a[1] + dz]))
    return out


def yard_place_ok(rec, cell, block):
    """yardread-01: a `project=yard op=place` -> bool. Pure. A record cell (floor, earthwork, torch), or a bulk block on one of
    the record's pile slots (the clerk's stock). Anything else is off_record."""
    k = ck(cell)
    if k in {ck(c['at']) for c in rec.get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')}:
        return True
    return block in YARD_BULK and k in yard_slots(rec)


def yard_tidy_ok(rec, cell):
    """yardread-01: a yard `kind=tidy` dig -> bool. Pure. A pile slot (a refill dug back off the top of a pile), or an AISLE cell
    one or two above a `base` floor cell (yard.mjs yardClutter). Never a record cell."""
    k = ck(cell)
    cells = [c for c in rec.get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')]
    if k in {ck(c['at']) for c in cells}:
        return False
    if k in yard_slots(rec):
        return True
    base = {ck(c['at']) for c in cells if c.get('role') == 'base'}
    return any(ck([cell[0], cell[1] - dy, cell[2]]) in base for dy in (1, 2))


def tidy_guarded(cell, t_ms, guard):
    """Was the cell another project's record cell or a marker block when it was dug? -> bool. Pure. (tidy_clear_ok's refusal; the
    read names such an item `guarded` so the tripwire's tree-debris split -- a log or leaves block cleared off a list is a WATCH --
    never softens it: Codex r1 BLOCKER, a log on another project's cell read as debris.)"""
    g = guard.get(ck(cell))
    u = guard.get('u|' + ck(cell))   # wallext-02: reserved only until its retirement
    return g is not None and (t_ms is None or g <= t_ms) and not (u is not None and t_ms is not None and t_ms >= u)


def overburden_ok(rec, cell, max_up=3, any_role=False):
    """pathsclose-01: is a `kind=overburden` clear a block set ON one of the record's PATH cells -- the same column, 1..max_up
    above it (bots/src/buildrun.mjs overburdenAbove, OVERBURDEN_MAX 3) -- and never a record cell itself? -> bool. Pure.
    wallstuck-01 (`any_role`, the wall): above ANY of the record's cells (buildrun.mjs sealedAbove: the cap over a sealed column)."""
    cells = rec.get('cells') or []
    keys = {ck(c['at']) for c in cells}
    if ck(cell) in keys:
        return False
    x, y, z = cell
    return any((any_role or c.get('role') == 'path') and c['at'][0] == x and c['at'][2] == z and 1 <= y - c['at'][1] <= max_up for c in cells)


def footing_ok(rec, cell, max_down=3):
    """wallstuck-01: is a `kind=footing` placement the floor given back under a record cell -- the same column, 1..max_down BELOW the
    column's lowest record cell (bots/src/buildrun.mjs footingBelow, FOOTING_MAX 3) -- and never a record cell itself? -> bool. Pure."""
    cells = rec.get('cells') or []
    if ck(cell) in {ck(c['at']) for c in cells}:
        return False
    x, y, z = cell
    col = [c['at'][1] for c in cells if c['at'][0] == x and c['at'][2] == z]
    return bool(col) and 1 <= min(col) - y <= max_down


def xyz_of(s):
    try:
        a = [int(v) for v in str(s).split(',')]
        return a if len(a) == 3 else None
    except ValueError:
        return None


def record_head(files):
    """files: [(gen, bytes)] -> the head record (buildproj.mjs recordHead, exactly: the root is the lowest gen with prev null;
    each later gen is the head only when its prev is the head and its prevSha the head's bytes' sha256) | None."""
    chain = record_chain(files)
    return chain[-1] if chain else None


def record_chain(files):
    """files: [(gen, bytes)] -> every generation of the valid chain, root first, each dict(rec, _gen, _sha) (record_head's walk:
    a generation joins only when its prev is the chain's last and its prevSha that one's bytes' sha256). Pure."""
    head, chain = None, []
    for gen, buf in sorted(files):
        try:
            rec = json.loads(buf.decode('utf-8'))
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not isinstance(rec, dict) or rec.get('gen') != gen:
            continue
        sha = hashlib.sha256(buf).hexdigest()
        if head is None:
            if rec.get('prev') is None:
                head = (gen, sha, rec)
                chain.append(dict(rec, _gen=gen, _sha=sha))
            continue
        if rec.get('prev') == head[0] and rec.get('prevSha') == head[1]:
            head = (gen, sha, rec)
            chain.append(dict(rec, _gen=gen, _sha=sha))
    return chain


def read_markers(d):
    """A town dir -> {cell key: ms placed} for every expedition marker block ever logged there (expedition-*.markers.jsonl: the
    two cobblestone and the torch, at .. at+2; skills.mjs exMarkers). A `gone` line does not lift it: a marker's blocks are never
    the tidy's to dig (fail closed). An unparseable line is skipped (the bot's exLines). Never raises."""
    out = {}
    try:
        names = [f for f in os.listdir(d) if MARKERS_RE.match(f)]
    except OSError:
        return out
    for f in names:
        try:
            lines = open(os.path.join(d, f), encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().split('\n')
        except OSError:
            continue
        for ln in lines:
            try:
                m = json.loads(ln) if ln.strip() else None
            except ValueError:
                continue
            at = m.get('at') if isinstance(m, dict) else None
            if m.get('gone') if isinstance(m, dict) else True:
                continue
            if not (isinstance(at, list) and len(at) == 3 and all(isinstance(v, int) for v in at)):
                continue
            t = m.get('t') if isinstance(m.get('t'), (int, float)) else 0
            for dy in (0, 1, 2):
                k = ck([at[0], at[1] + dy, at[2]])
                out[k] = min(out.get(k, t), t)
    return out


BRIDGE_TORCH_EVERY = 8                 # bots/src/bridge.mjs TORCH_EVERY
BRIDGE_MERGE_R = 8                     # bots/src/bridge.mjs SITE_MERGE_R


def _cell(q):
    return isinstance(q, list) and len(q) == 3 and all(isinstance(v, int) for v in q)


def bridge_cells(span):
    """A bridge site's span (bots/src/bridge.mjs bridgeSpan, as the index stores it) -> [[x, y, z], ...]: EXACTLY the blocks its plan
    (bridgePlan) places, the bot's own dig guard (bridgeCellKeys) -- each row's centre deck, both side decks, both rails, and on every
    BRIDGE_TORCH_EVERY-th row (i % 8 == 0) the torch above the side +1 rail. Pure; a malformed span gives []."""
    out = []
    rows = span.get('rows') if isinstance(span, dict) else None
    for r in rows if isinstance(rows, list) else []:
        if not isinstance(r, dict):
            continue
        c = r.get('centre')
        if _cell(c):
            out.append(c)
        for sd in r.get('sides') if isinstance(r.get('sides'), list) else []:
            if not isinstance(sd, dict):
                continue
            for k in ('deck', 'rail'):
                if _cell(sd.get(k)):
                    out.append(sd[k])
            if sd.get('side') == 1 and _cell(sd.get('rail')) and isinstance(r.get('i'), int) and r['i'] % BRIDGE_TORCH_EVERY == 0:
                q = sd['rail']
                out.append([q[0], q[1] + 1, q[2]])
    return out


def read_bridges(d):
    """A town dir -> {cell key: ms first sighted} for every block of every bridge site ever sighted there (expedition-*.bridges.jsonl
    `sight` lines; bridgepost-01). Built or not, complete or broken, a site's cells are never the tidy's to dig (the bot's own guard,
    skills.mjs bridgeCellKeys: fail closed). An unparseable line is skipped. Never raises."""
    out = {}
    try:
        names = sorted(f for f in os.listdir(d) if BRIDGES_RE.match(f))
    except OSError:
        return out
    # the bot's sites (bridge.mjs bridgeSites): a sighting on the same axis whose near end is within BRIDGE_MERGE_R of a site's is that
    # site, and the site keeps its FIRST span -- only those cells are planned and guarded
    sites = []
    for f in names:
        try:
            lines = open(os.path.join(d, f), encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().split('\n')
        except OSError:
            continue
        for ln in lines:
            try:
                m = json.loads(ln) if ln.strip() else None
            except ValueError:
                continue
            if not isinstance(m, dict) or m.get('op') != 'sight':
                continue
            sp = m.get('span') if isinstance(m.get('span'), dict) else None
            if not sp or not _cell(sp.get('near')) or not isinstance(sp.get('axis'), list):
                continue
            t = m.get('t') if isinstance(m.get('t'), (int, float)) else 0
            same = [q for q in sites if q['axis'] == sp['axis'] and ((q['near'][0] - sp['near'][0]) ** 2 + (q['near'][2] - sp['near'][2]) ** 2) ** 0.5 <= BRIDGE_MERGE_R]
            if same:
                same[0]['t'] = min(same[0]['t'], t)
                continue
            sites.append({'axis': sp['axis'], 'near': sp['near'], 'span': sp, 't': t})
    for q in sites:
        for c in bridge_cells(q['span']):
            k = ck(c)
            out[k] = min(out.get(k, q['t']), q['t'])
    return out


def merge_guards(a, b):
    """Two {cell key: ms} guard tables -> one, each cell at its EARLIEST time (a marker placed before a bridge was sighted over its
    cell guards from the marker's time). Pure."""
    out = dict(a or {})
    for k, t in (b or {}).items():
        out[k] = min(out.get(k, t), t)
    return out


def read_projects(root='/var/lib/mcai'):
    """-> { pool: { 'record': rec | None, 'leases': { name: [(gen, rec, released_ms|None)] }, 'dir': d, 'projects': { plot: {
    'record', 'records_files', 'leases' } } } } for every town. The top-level record and leases are the workshop's (`shop`), as
    before; `projects` holds every project's."""
    out = {}
    for d in glob.glob(os.path.join(root, '_pool-*')):
        name = os.path.basename(d)[len('_pool-'):]
        pool = pool_of(name)
        try:
            names = os.listdir(d)
        except OSError:
            continue
        recs, leases = defaultdict(list), defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
        for f in names:
            m = REC_RE.match(f)
            if m:
                try:
                    recs[m.group(5)].append((int(m.group(6)), open(os.path.join(d, f), 'rb').read()))
                except OSError:
                    pass
                continue
            m = LEASE_RE.match(f)
            if m:
                p = os.path.join(d, f)
                try:
                    rec = json.load(open(p))
                except Exception:
                    rec = None
                rel = None
                try:
                    rel = int(open(p + '.released').read().strip() or 0) or int(os.path.getmtime(p + '.released') * 1000)
                except (OSError, ValueError):
                    rel = None
                leases[m.group(5)][m.group(6)].append((int(m.group(7)), rec, rel))
        # the bot's own rule (skills.mjs townPlanZones): a MISSING file is known and empty; a present file that does not parse
        # to an object is unreadable -- the bot then uses its last good list or refuses, and this read cannot judge G3
        zf = os.path.join(d, 'protected-zones.json')
        if not os.path.exists(zf):
