            zok = True
        else:
            try:
                zok = zones_valid(json.load(open(zf)))
            except Exception:
                zok = False
        projects = {plot: {'record': record_head(recs[plot]) if recs.get(plot) else None, 'records_files': len(recs.get(plot) or []),
                           'leases': dict(leases.get(plot) or {}), 'chain': record_chain(recs[plot]) if recs.get(plot) else []}
                    for plot in set(recs) | set(leases)}
        shop = projects.get(SHOP) or {'record': None, 'records_files': 0, 'leases': {}}
        out[pool] = {'record': shop['record'], 'records_files': shop['records_files'], 'leases': shop['leases'], 'dir': d, 'zones_ok': zok,
                     'projects': projects, 'markers': merge_guards(read_markers(d), read_bridges(d))}
    return out


def replant_road_why(pr, cell):
    """outbands-01: is a tidy replant cell on a road? -> 'on_road' (in a land-plan corridor box [x0, y0, z0, x1, y1, z1], the land
    record's `corridors`: bots/src/landplan.mjs corridorAt -- columns only) | 'on_path' (a column of any generation of the paths
    record's cells) | ''. Pure."""
    lrec = project_of(pr, LAND).get('record') or {}
    for zb in lrec.get('corridors') or []:
        b = zb.get('box') if isinstance(zb, dict) else None
        if isinstance(b, list) and len(b) == 6 and b[0] <= cell[0] <= b[3] and b[2] <= cell[2] <= b[5]:
            return 'on_road'
    pj = project_of(pr, PATHS)
    for rec in pj.get('chain') or ([pj['record']] if pj.get('record') else []):
        if any(isinstance(c, dict) and isinstance(c.get('at'), list) and c['at'][0] == cell[0] and c['at'][2] == cell[2] for c in rec.get('cells') or []):
            return 'on_path'
    return ''


def project_of(pr, plot):
    """A town's entry for one project -> { record, records_files, leases }. A town read without `projects` (an older caller, the
    fixtures) holds the workshop's only: another project there has no record (oracle_missing, never judged against the
    workshop's)."""
    plot = plot or SHOP
    if isinstance((pr or {}).get('projects'), dict):
        return pr['projects'].get(plot) or {'record': None, 'records_files': 0, 'leases': {}}
    if plot == SHOP:
        return {'record': (pr or {}).get('record'), 'records_files': (pr or {}).get('records_files', 0), 'leases': (pr or {}).get('leases') or {}}
    return {'record': None, 'records_files': 0, 'leases': {}}


def tidy_guard(pr):
    """A town's entry -> {cell key: ms first reserved}: every cell of every OTHER project's record chain (not the tidy's, not the
    land plan's) at its earliest generation's time, and the expedition markers' blocks (read_projects). A record with no time is
    reserved from the start (0: fail closed). Pure."""
    out = dict((pr or {}).get('markers') or {})
    # wallext-02 hold (Codex batch-5 fold BLOCKER): a retired old wall cell stays reserved until the DEMOLITION is complete (its
    # earliest complete generation's completeAt), never from the retirement itself; with no complete demolition it stays reserved
    dj = (((pr or {}).get('projects') or {}).get(WALLDEMO) or {})
    dchain = dj.get('chain') or ([dj['record']] if dj.get('record') else [])
    done_at = min([g['completeAt'] for g in dchain if g.get('structure_complete') and isinstance(g.get('completeAt'), (int, float))] or [None], key=lambda v: float('inf') if v is None else v)
    for plot, pj in ((pr or {}).get('projects') or {}).items():
        if plot in (TIDY, LAND):
            continue
        chain = pj.get('chain') or ([pj['record']] if pj.get('record') else [])
        for rec in chain:
            t = rec.get('at') if isinstance(rec.get('at'), (int, float)) else (rec.get('sitedAt') if isinstance(rec.get('sitedAt'), (int, float)) else 0)
            for c in rec.get('cells') or []:
                if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at'):
                    k = ck(c['at'])
                    out[k] = min(out.get(k, t), t)
            # wallext-02 (Claude WD1 r1 BLOCKER 3; the hold, Codex batch-5 fold): a cell a later generation RETIRES (the old wall's
            # line, demolished) is no longer reserved once the demolition is complete: its ground is the groundskeeper's then (key
            # 'u|<cell>': reserved until)
            # forest-01 RF1: a RETIRED tree farm's cells are released at its own retiredAt (it has no demolition to wait for)
            ra = (rec['retiredAt'] if plot == TREEFARM else done_at) if isinstance(rec.get('retiredAt'), (int, float)) else None
            if isinstance(ra, (int, float)):
                for c in rec.get('retired') or []:
                    if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at'):
                        u = 'u|' + ck(c['at'])
                        out[u] = min(out.get(u, ra), ra)
    return out


def zones_valid(raw):
    """THE BOT'S OWN SCHEMA (protectzone.mjs parseZones; Codex read r2 P2-3): an object, version 1, a `zones` list of at most 64,
    every zone an id string and a 6-integer ordered box within the spans, floor_y (if any) an integer inside it. Anything less
    makes the bot keep its last good list -- which this read cannot see -- so G3 cannot be judged: False (fail closed)."""
    isint = lambda v: isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool)
    if not isinstance(raw, dict) or raw.get('version') != 1 or not isinstance(raw.get('zones'), list) or len(raw['zones']) > 64:
        return False
    for z in raw['zones']:
        b = z.get('box') if isinstance(z, dict) else None
        if not (isinstance(z, dict) and isinstance(z.get('id'), str) and z['id'] and isinstance(b, list) and len(b) == 6 and all(isint(v) for v in b)):
            return False
        x0, y0, z0, x1, y1, z1 = b
        if x0 > x1 or y0 > y1 or z0 > z1 or x1 - x0 > 1024 or z1 - z0 > 1024 or y1 - y0 > 448:
            return False
        fy = z.get('floor_y')
        if fy is not None and not (isint(fy) and y0 <= fy <= y1):
            return False
    return True


def lease_overlaps(leases, builders_max=BUILDERS_MAX):
    """{ name: [(gen, rec, released_ms)] } -> [(name, gen, why)]: a generation taken by ANOTHER holder while the one before it
    still blocked (unreleased and inside until + RECLAIM_MS), and any slot name beyond `builders_max` (the record's own: the
    ziggurat admits three)."""
    bad = []
    for name, gens in leases.items():
        m = re.match(r'^slot(\d+)$', name)
        if m and int(m.group(1)) > builders_max:
            bad.append((name, None, 'slot beyond builders_max'))
        gs = sorted(gens, key=lambda g: g[0])
        for (g0, r0, rel0), (g1, r1, _) in zip(gs, gs[1:]):
            if not r0 or not r1 or r0.get('holder') == r1.get('holder'):
                continue
            blocks_until = rel0 if rel0 is not None else (int(r0.get('until') or 0) + RECLAIM_MS)
            if int(r1.get('at') or 0) < blocks_until:
                bad.append((name, g1, '%s took it at %s, %s held it until %s' % (r1.get('holder'), r1.get('at'), r0.get('holder'), blocks_until)))
    return bad


def structcount_gens(root=os.path.expanduser('~/structcount/in')):
    """-> [(time, {town: day-doc})] oldest first, from the mirror's generation directories (gen-<YYYYmmddTHHMMSS.mmmZ>-<n>)."""
    out = []
    for d in glob.glob(os.path.join(root, 'gen-*')):
        m = re.match(r'^gen-(\d{8}T\d{6})', os.path.basename(d))
        if not m:
            continue
        try:
            doc = json.load(open(os.path.join(d, 'structcount-day.json')))
        except Exception:
            continue
        out.append((dt.datetime.strptime(m.group(1), '%Y%m%dT%H%M%S').replace(tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc), doc))
    return sorted(out, key=lambda g: g[0])


def load_surveys(root=SURVEY_DIR):
    """-> (present, {pool: [survey]}) from the conformance surveys' mirror (shopconform.py's JSON, one file each)."""
    if not os.path.isdir(root):
        return False, {}
    out = defaultdict(list)
    for f in glob.glob(os.path.join(root, '*.json')):
        try:
            d = json.load(open(f))
        except Exception:
            continue
        if isinstance(d, dict) and d.get('pool'):
            out[d['pool']].append(d)
    return True, dict(out)


def conformance(surveys, rec, since):
    """A completed town's CONFORMANCE from its surveys -> (ok, why). Pure. Counted: valid surveys of THIS record (gen and sha) whose
    observed time (`at` less SAVE_LAG_MIN) is after `since` (the completion). ok when two of them lie >= CONFORM_GAP_MIN apart with F1 >= CONFORM_F1
    each AND the newest counted survey has F1 >= CONFORM_F1 (damage after a good pair revokes it)."""
    if rec is None:
        return False, 'no record'
    # THE OBSERVED TIME is the survey's own time less SAVE_LAG_MIN: the copy holds the world as the server last SAVED it (a loaded,
    # changed chunk at its autosave, ~5 min; an unchanged or unloaded one exactly) -- never the region file's mtime, which a
    # quiet chunk never advances (a workshop that persists unchanged must still read as two observations an hour apart)
    def t(sv):
        # a time with no zone, or no time, is no observation (r6, Codex P2: a naive `at` raised TypeError)
        try:
            v = dt.datetime.fromisoformat(str(sv.get('at')).replace('Z', '+00:00'))
        except Exception:
            return None
        return v - dt.timedelta(minutes=SAVE_LAG_MIN) if v.tzinfo is not None else None

    def f1(sv):
        # a score that is not a finite number in [0, 1] is no score (r6, Codex P2: f1 "oops" raised ValueError)
        try:
            v = float(sv.get('f1'))
        except Exception:
            return None
        return v if v == v and 0.0 <= v <= 1.0 else None
    mine = sorted([sv for sv in surveys or [] if isinstance(sv, dict) and sv.get('valid') is True and sv.get('record_gen') == rec.get('_gen')
                   and sv.get('record_sha') == rec.get('_sha') and t(sv) is not None and t(sv) >= since and f1(sv) is not None], key=t)
    if not mine:
        return False, 'no valid survey of this record after the completion (%d surveys)' % len(surveys or [])
    newest = mine[-1]
    # the persistent pair lies AFTER the last bad survey (r6, Claude P3-4: good, damaged, repaired is not an hour of persistence);
    # a bad NEWEST survey leaves nothing after it (damage after a good pair revokes it)
    last_bad = max([i for i, sv in enumerate(mine) if f1(sv) < CONFORM_F1], default=-1)
    if last_bad == len(mine) - 1:
        return False, 'newest survey F1 %s at %s' % (newest.get('f1'), newest.get('at'))
    good = [sv for sv in mine[last_bad + 1:] if f1(sv) >= CONFORM_F1]
    if not good or (t(good[-1]) - t(good[0])).total_seconds() < CONFORM_GAP_MIN * 60:
        return False, '%d good survey(s), not %d min apart' % (len(good), CONFORM_GAP_MIN)
    return True, 'F1 %s .. %s over %d surveys, %s -> %s' % (good[0].get('f1'), newest.get('f1'), len(mine), good[0].get('at'), newest.get('at'))


def rooms_in_box(doc, box):
    """The structure count's lit rooms (B5 buildings: `building_rooms`, JSON strings with a `centre`) whose centre lies inside the
    record's cells' extent box = [x0, y0, z0, x1, y1, z1] (+-1) -> n, or None when the town's room data is missing or malformed
    (Codex read r3 P2-1: unknown is never zero). Record-SPECIFIC, height included (Codex read r3 P2-2: a room above or below
    does not count). Positive control, 10-25 on sandbox8 after SHOP-E2E: one lit room, centre 734.4,121,764, inside the record's
    extent; the base world: none."""
    rooms = (doc or {}).get('building_rooms') if isinstance(doc, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(rooms, list) or not box or len(box) != 6:
        return None
    n = 0
    for raw in rooms:
        try:
            r = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else raw
            cx, cy, cz = (float(v) for v in r.get('centre'))
        except Exception:
            return None
        if r.get('lit') and box[0] - 1 <= cx <= box[3] + 1 and box[1] - 1 <= cy <= box[4] + 1 and box[2] - 1 <= cz <= box[5] + 1:
            n += 1
    return n


def record_extent(rec):
    """The record's cells' extent -> [x0, y0, z0, x1, y1, z1] | None."""
    cells = [c.get('at') for c in (rec or {}).get('cells') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and isinstance(c.get('at'), list) and len(c['at']) == 3]
    if not cells:
        return None
    return [min(c[0] for c in cells), min(c[1] for c in cells), min(c[2] for c in cells), max(c[0] for c in cells), max(c[1] for c in cells), max(c[2] for c in cells)]


def world_evidence(gens, cut, complete_at, boxes=None):
    """gens: structcount_gens(); cut: the deploy time; complete_at: {pool: first completion time}; boxes: {pool: the record's box}.
    -> ({pool: why}, litter). A completed town is CONFIRMED when the first count after its completion shows a lit room INSIDE ITS
    RECORD'S BOX and the last count before the deploy showed none there. litter: {pool: (before, after)} for every town both
    counts cover (the no-harm line's cells)."""
    boxes = boxes or {}
    before = [g for g in gens if g[0] < cut]
    base = before[-1][1] if before else None
    world = {}
    for pool, t in complete_at.items():
        after = [g for g in gens if g[0] > t]
        if base is None:
            world[pool] = 'no count before the deploy'
        elif not after:
            world[pool] = 'no count after the completion yet'
        elif not boxes.get(pool):
            world[pool] = 'no record box'
        else:
            r0, r1 = rooms_in_box(base.get(pool), boxes[pool]), rooms_in_box(after[0][1].get(pool), boxes[pool])
            if r0 is None or r1 is None:
                world[pool] = 'room data missing or malformed (before %s, after %s): unknown' % (r0, r1)
            else:
                world[pool] = 'room' if r0 == 0 and r1 >= 1 else 'lit rooms in the record extent %d -> %d' % (r0, r1)
    litter = {}
    last = gens[-1][1] if gens and gens[-1][0] > cut else None
    if base is not None and last is not None:
        for pool in set(base) & set(last):
            a, b = (base.get(pool) or {}).get('litter_cells'), (last.get(pool) or {}).get('litter_cells')
            if isinstance(a, int) and isinstance(b, int):
                litter[pool] = (a, b)
    return world, litter


def pos_of(r):
    p = r.get('pos')
    if isinstance(p, dict) and all(k in p for k in 'xyz'):
        return [int(p['x'] // 1), int((p['y'] + 1e-6) // 1), int(p['z'] // 1)]
    if isinstance(p, (list, tuple)) and len(p) == 3:
        return [int(p[0] // 1), int((p[1] + 1e-6) // 1), int(p[2] // 1)]
    return None


def judge(rows, projects, zones_by_pool, cut=None, gens=(), surveys=None):
    """rows: [{t, per, arm, name, status, detail, bot, item, feet}]; projects: read_projects(); zones_by_pool: {pool: [box]};
    gens: structcount_gens() (the world evidence; empty: none)."""
    out = Counter()
    lists = defaultdict(list)
    rows = sorted(rows, key=lambda r: r['t'])
    placed = defaultdict(list)          # (pool, cell) -> [t]
    unknown_open = {}                   # (bot, cell) -> t
    complete_at = {}
    stalled_pools = {}                  # pool -> the cells waiting on its stuck cell (F4, a stalled project: reported)
    guards = {}                         # pool -> tidy_guard (tidyread-01)
    mutating, cans = set(), set()
    market_days = {}                    # pool -> [t] of market job placements (MK1's cap)
    for r in rows:
        f = kv(r['detail'])
        arm, per, name, pool, bot = r['arm'], r['per'], r['name'], pool_of(r['bot']), r['bot']
        if name in MARKET_KINDS:
            # market-01 MK1-MK3, judged on the canary arm after the cut (the showcase: every row), then done
            if arm != 'canary' or per != 'post':
                continue
            padded = [[b[0] - MARKET_PAD, b[1], b[2] - MARKET_PAD, b[3] + MARKET_PAD, b[4], b[5] + MARKET_PAD] for b in zones_by_pool.get(pool) or []]
            if name == '_market_job' and f.get('op') == 'placed':
                cell = xyz_of(f.get('at'))
                if cell is None:
                    lists['bag_unjudged'].append('MK1 %s placed with no at=' % bot); out['bag_unjudged'] += 1
                    continue
                out['market_places'] += 1
                bed = bool(MARKET_BED.match(f.get('block') or ''))
                if f.get('block') not in MARKET_BLOCKS and not bed:
                    lists['off_record'].append('%s %s %s market_block' % (bot, ck(cell), f.get('block'))); out['off_record'] += 1
                if any(in_box(b, cell) for b in padded):
                    lists['zone'].append('%s %s %s market_job' % (bot, ck(cell), f.get('block'))); out['zone'] += 1
                # the day caps: job blocks (MARKET_PER_DAY) and beds by their foot (MARKET_BEDS_PER_DAY); a bed's head row is its own half
                if not (bed and f.get('why') == 'bed_head'):
                    ts = market_days.setdefault((pool, 'bed' if bed else 'job'), [])
                    ts.append(r['t'])
                    if len([x for x in ts if (r['t'] - x).total_seconds() < 86400]) > (MARKET_BEDS_PER_DAY if bed else MARKET_PER_DAY):
                        lists['off_record'].append('%s %s %s market_cap' % (bot, ck(cell), f.get('block'))); out['off_record'] += 1
            elif name == '_market_run':
                out['market_runs'] += 1
                cell = xyz_of(f.get('at'))
                # market-02 (OWNER 11-07 D1): a WALK-IN trade (`walkin=1`, a market_trip's merchant) stands in the village by design --
                # PRESENCE, never a block change (every block change in a zone stays G3 / S4 / MK1's); a run without it never enters
                if cell is not None and any(in_box([b[0], -10 ** 6, b[2], b[3], 10 ** 6, b[5]], cell) for b in padded):
                    if f.get('walkin') == '1':
                        out['market_walkins'] += 1
                    else:
                        lists['zone'].append('%s %s %s market_run' % (bot, ck(cell), f.get('with'))); out['zone'] += 1
                try:
                    spent = int(f.get('spent') or 0)
                except ValueError:
                    spent = 0
                if spent > MARKET_SPEND:
                    lists['bag_loss'].append('MK2 market_spend %s spent=%d' % (bot, spent)); out['bag_loss'] += 1
            elif name == '_trade' and f.get('op') == 'sell':
                out['market_sales'] += 1
                if f.get('item') not in MARKET_GOODS:
                    lists['bag_loss'].append('MK3 market_sold %s %s:%s' % (bot, f.get('item'), f.get('n'))); out['bag_loss'] += 1
            continue
        if name in HUB_KINDS:
            # storehub-01 SH1 and the hall's signs, judged on the canary arm after the cut (the showcase: every row), then done
            if arm != 'canary' or per != 'post':
                continue
            if name == '_well_dispose' and f.get('via') == 'clerk':
                out['hub_well_rows'] += 1
                # lost= (the row's SECOND field: what went down, as the server confirmed) is judged; a row without it falls back to
                # items= (the LAST field), and a row the logger may have cut there (>= 299 characters) is unjudged as well as judged:
                # its last name may be cut short (Claude review of SH1, blocker 1)
                names = f.get('lost') if 'lost' in f else f.get('items')
                if 'lost' not in f and ('items' not in f or len(r['detail'] or '') >= 299):
                    lists['kept_unjudged'].append('%s %s' % (bot, (r['detail'] or '')[-40:])); out['kept_unjudged'] += 1
                    lists['bag_unjudged'].append('SH1 %s well via=clerk row cut' % bot); out['bag_unjudged'] += 1
                for n, c in sorted(hub_names(names).items()):
                    if hub_kept(n):
                        out['kept_thrown'] += 1; lists['kept_thrown'].append('%s well via=clerk %s:%d' % (bot, n, c))
                        lists['bag_loss'].append('SH1 kept_thrown %s %s:%d (well via=clerk)' % (bot, n, c)); out['bag_loss'] += 1
            elif name == '_storehub_clerk':
                out['hub_clerk_rows'] += 1
                for n, c in sorted(hub_names(f.get('junked')).items()):
                    if hub_kept(n):
                        out['kept_thrown'] += 1; lists['kept_thrown'].append('%s clerk junked %s:%d' % (bot, n, c))
                        lists['bag_loss'].append('SH1 kept_thrown %s %s:%d (clerk row)' % (bot, n, c)); out['bag_loss'] += 1
            elif name == '_hub_sign' and f.get('op') == 'place':
                cell = xyz_of(f.get('at'))
                if cell is None:
                    continue
                # a SIGN STANDS only after the writer's place (skills.mjs sortLabelOne): ok=1 (written), or why=no_editor /
                # text_not_read_back (placed, text not confirmed). Before it nothing was placed (out_of_reach, no_sign, no_wall,
                # not_placed): never judged (Codex review of SH1, blocker 1). An error mid-way is reported, not judged
                why = f.get('why') or ''
                if why.startswith('error'):
                    lists['hub_sign_unjudged'].append('%s %s %s' % (bot, ck(cell), why[:30])); out['hub_sign_unjudged'] += 1
                if not (f.get('ok') == '1' or why in ('no_editor', 'text_not_read_back')):
                    continue
                out['hub_sign_places'] += 1
                if any(in_box(b, cell) for b in zones_by_pool.get(pool) or []):
                    lists['zone'].append('%s sign %s' % (bot, ck(cell))); out['zone'] += 1
                pj = project_of(projects.get(pool), HUB_PLOT)
                chain = pj.get('chain') or ([pj['record']] if pj.get('record') else [])
                if not hub_sign_ok(chain, cell):
                    lists['off_record'].append('%s %s hub_sign' % (bot, ck(cell))); out['off_record'] += 1
            elif name == '_enchantshop_sign' and f.get('op') == 'place':
                # enchantshop-01 EF1: the workshop's labels, by the hall signs' rule (judged only once a sign stands)
                cell = xyz_of(f.get('at'))
                if cell is None:
                    continue
                why = f.get('why') or ''
                if why.startswith('error'):
                    lists['enchant_sign_unjudged'].append('%s %s %s' % (bot, ck(cell), why[:30])); out['enchant_sign_unjudged'] += 1
                if not (why in ('no_editor', 'text_not_read_back') or f.get('ok') == '1'):
                    continue
                out['enchant_sign_places'] += 1
                if any(in_box(b, cell) for b in zones_by_pool.get(pool) or []):
                    lists['zone'].append('%s enchant sign %s' % (bot, ck(cell))); out['zone'] += 1
                pj = project_of(projects.get(pool), ENCHANT)
                chain = pj.get('chain') or ([pj['record']] if pj.get('record') else [])
                if not enchant_sign_ok(chain, cell):
                    lists['off_record'].append('%s %s enchantshop_sign' % (bot, ck(cell))); out['off_record'] += 1
            continue
        if name in BUILD_KINDS and arm == 'control' and per == 'post':
            out['arms_rows_control_post'] += 1
            if name == '_build_visit':
                lists['control_rows'].append('%s %s' % (bot, f.get('outcome')))
        if name == 'craft':
            out['craft_%s_%s' % (per, arm)] += 1
            if r.get('item') in RUNGS:
                out['rung_%s_%s' % (per, arm)] += 1
                out['rung_ok_%s_%s' % (per, arm)] += int(r['status'] == 'success')
        if name == 'gather':
            out['gather_%s_%s' % (per, arm)] += 1
            out['gather_ok_%s_%s' % (per, arm)] += int(r['status'] == 'success')
        if arm == 'canary':
            cans.add(pool)
        if arm != 'canary' or per != 'post':
            continue
        if name == '_build_mode':
            for k in [k for k in unknown_open if k[0] == bot]:      # a new connection: its unknown set starts empty -- FIRST
                del unknown_open[k]
        # G6 on the bot's OWN build rows only (their position is the builder's stand): feet IN the cell or ON it
        if name in BUILD_KINDS and r.get('feet') and unknown_open:
            p = r['feet']
            for cand in (ck(p), ck([p[0], p[1] - 1, p[2]])):
                if (bot, cand) in unknown_open and r['t'] > unknown_open[(bot, cand)]:
                    out['unknown_stood'] += 1; lists['unknown_stood'].append('%s at %s' % (bot, cand))
                    del unknown_open[(bot, cand)]
        if name in BUILD_KINDS and r.get('feet'):
            out['build_rows_with_pos'] += 1
        if name == '_build_mode':
            out['licence_canary'] += 1
        elif name == '_build_visit':
            out['visits_canary'] += 1
            out['visit_%s' % f.get('stop', '?')] += 1
            for k in ('placed', 'refused', 'silent', 'cleared', 'planted', 'unknown'):
                try:
                    out['sum_%s' % k] += int(f.get(k) or 0)
                except ValueError:
                    pass
            if f.get('stop') not in ('complete', None):
                lists['why'].append('%s %s:%s' % (bot, f.get('stop'), (f.get('why') or '-')[:50]))
        elif name == '_build_bag':
            out['bag_rows'] += 1
            # G8 judges only a CLEAN exit with no silent click (Claude read r2 P2: a late-acked silent click placed its block
            # untallied; a death or an abort leaves the bag mid-visit) -- the rest are reported, never a loss
            if f.get('exit') in (None, '-', 'aborted', 'error', 'died', 'off') or f.get('silent') != '0':
                out['bag_unjudged'] += 1; lists['bag_unjudged'].append('%s exit=%s silent=%s' % (bot, f.get('exit'), f.get('silent')))
                continue
            for m in BAG_RE.finditer(r['detail'] or ''):
                n = m.group(1)
                b0, took, crafted, pl, back, b1 = (int(m.group(i)) for i in range(2, 8))
                unexplained = b1 - (b0 + took + crafted - pl - back)
                out['bag_names'] += 1
                if unexplained < 0 and n not in SCAFFOLD_NAMES:
                    out['bag_loss'] += 1; lists['bag_loss'].append('%s %s %d' % (bot, n, unexplained))
                elif unexplained < 0:
                    out['bag_scaffold'] += -unexplained
        elif name == '_structure_complete':
            # the WORKSHOP's completion drives the world evidence and H8's conformance (both are the workshop's); another
            # project's is counted
            if (f.get('project') or SHOP) == SHOP:
                complete_at.setdefault(pool, r['t'])
            else:
                out['projects_complete'] += 1; lists['projects_complete'].append('%s %s' % (pool, f.get('project')))
        elif name == '_build':
            op, cell = f.get('op'), xyz_of(f.get('cell'))
            plot = f.get('project') or SHOP
            rec = project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('record')
            # wallretired-01 (11-05 showcase TRIP: 113 off_record + 3 clear_outside, every one the old wall's own row of 10-30/31 on a
            # cell wallext-02's demolition RETIRED): a later generation moves those cells from `cells` to `retired`. A wall row made
            # BEFORE retiredAt is ALSO judged with them still its cells (rec_pre), and the more lenient verdict stands, so this only
            # removes defects (Claude r1 BLOCKER: overburden_ok / footing_ok refuse a record cell). At or after retiredAt: the head alone
            rec_pre = None
            if rec is not None and plot in WALL_PLOTS + (TREEFARM,) and rec.get('retired') and isinstance(rec.get('retiredAt'), (int, float)) \
                    and hasattr(r['t'], 'timestamp') and int(r['t'].timestamp() * 1000) < rec['retiredAt']:
                # forest-01 RF1: a retired tree farm's earlier rows, judged with its cells and its clears as they were
                rec_pre = dict(rec, cells=list(rec.get('cells') or []) + [c for c in rec['retired'] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')],
                               clear=list(rec.get('clear') or []) + [c for c in rec.get('retiredClear') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')])
            if op == 'sited':
                out['sited'] += 1
                continue
            if cell is None:
                continue
            k = ck(cell)
            # decorator-01 DC1: the nursery's meal is a mutation too (G3 judges it)
            if op in ('place', 'clear', 'replant', 'meal'):
                mutating.add((pool, plot))
                out['mutations'] += 1
                if any(in_box(b, cell) for b in zones_by_pool.get(pool) or []):
                    out['zone'] += 1; lists['zone'].append('%s %s %s' % (bot, op, k))
            # forest-01 FR1/FR4/RF1: the forest's felling and bone meal and the retired farm's felling are mutations (G3 judges them);
            # a `*_failed` harvest row digs nothing
            if (op == 'harvest' and ((plot == FOREST and not str(f.get('kind') or '').endswith('_failed')) or (plot == TREEFARM and f.get('kind') == 'retire'))) \
                    or (op == 'use' and plot == FOREST):
                mutating.add((pool, FOREST if plot == FOREST else TREEFARM))
                out['mutations'] += 1
                if any(in_box(b, cell) for b in zones_by_pool.get(pool) or []):
                    lists['zone'].append('%s %s %s' % (bot, op, k)); out['zone'] += 1
            if op == 'harvest' and plot in (CROPFARM, CANEFARM):
                # cropfarm-01: a crop farm's harvest is a mutation too, and G3 judges it (canefarm-01: the cane bed's too)
                mutating.add((pool, CANEFARM if plot == CANEFARM else CROPFARM))
                out['mutations'] += 1
                if any(in_box(b, cell) for b in zones_by_pool.get(pool) or []):
                    lists['zone'].append('%s harvest %s' % (bot, k)); out['zone'] += 1
            if op == 'place':
                out['placements'] += 1
                pwhy = ''
                if plot == TIDY and rec is not None:
                    # tidyread-02: a tidy fill is judged against the generations in force when it was placed (tidy_place_ok)
                    t_ms = int(r['t'].timestamp() * 1000) if hasattr(r['t'], 'timestamp') else None
                    off = not tidy_place_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, t_ms)
                    # tidyplaceguard-01 (access-01 review, Claude: the groundskeeper's stairs put tidy blocks ABOVE ground beside builds): a
                    # tidy placement onto another project's record cell or a marker that stood then is never the tidy's, listed or not
                    # (the clear side's tidy_guarded, now on the place side too); its item ends `guarded`
                    if not off:
                        if pool not in guards:
                            guards[pool] = tidy_guard(projects.get(pool))
                        if tidy_guarded(cell, t_ms, guards[pool]):
                            off = True
                            pwhy = ' guarded'
                elif plot in SINK_PLOTS and rec is not None:
                    # picker-01: the generations of the sink's growing chain in force when it was placed (sink_place_ok)
                    t_ms = int(r['t'].timestamp() * 1000) if hasattr(r['t'], 'timestamp') else None
                    off = not sink_place_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, t_ms)
                    # decorator-01 DC3: a pot's fill is a potted block on a pot cell in force
                    if not off and plot == DECOR and f.get('kind') == 'pot_fill' and not decor_pot_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('block'), t_ms):
                        off = True
                        pwhy = ' pot'
                elif plot == YARD and rec is not None:
                    # any generation of the yard's chain (a re-sited yard's earlier stock stays the yard's -- Claude r1 note)
                    off = not any(yard_place_ok(g, cell, f.get('block')) for g in (project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec]))
                elif plot == STEPWELL and rec is not None:
                    # stepwell-01 SW1: a cell of any generation of the stepwell's chain; a seal also in a cut cell of one
                    off = not stepwell_place_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('kind'))
                    pwhy = ' seal' if off and f.get('kind') == 'seal' else ''
                elif plot == ENCHANT and rec is not None:
                    # enchantshop-01 EF1: a shell cell, or a fit slot with its own item, of any generation of the workshop's chain
                    off = not any(enchant_place_ok(g, cell, f.get('block')) for g in (project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec]))
                else:
                    off_of = lambda rr: k not in {ck(c['at']) for c in rr.get('cells') or []} and not (f.get('kind') == 'footing' and plot in WALL_PLOTS and footing_ok(rr, cell))
                    off = rec is not None and off_of(rec) and (rec_pre is None or off_of(rec_pre))
                if off:
                    out['off_record'] += 1; lists['off_record'].append('%s %s %s%s' % (bot, k, f.get('block'), pwhy))
                if [t for t in placed[(pool, plot, k)] if (r['t'] - t).total_seconds() < PLACE_TWICE_MIN * 60]:
                    out['placed_twice'] += 1; lists['placed_twice'].append('%s %s' % (pool, k))
                placed[(pool, plot, k)].append(r['t'])
            elif op == 'meal':
                # decorator-01 DC1: bone meal used ON a nursery centre of a generation in force, nothing else (any other project: off)
                out['decor_meals'] += 1
                t_ms = int(r['t'].timestamp() * 1000) if hasattr(r['t'], 'timestamp') else None
                lawn = f.get('kind') == 'lawn'   # flowers-02 DC7: a lawn's one meal, on a lawn centre in force only
                meal_ok = decor_lawn_meal_ok if lawn else decor_meal_ok
                if rec is not None and not (plot == DECOR and meal_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, t_ms)):
                    out['off_record'] += 1; lists['off_record'].append('%s %s %s %s' % (bot, k, f.get('block'), 'lawn_meal' if lawn else 'meal'))
            elif op == 'harvest' and plot == CROPFARM:
                # cropfarm-01 G9: a crop dug on a crop cell of the farm's record, nothing else (counted with G2's clear_outside)
                out['harvests'] += 1
                if rec is not None and not crop_harvest_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('block')):
                    lists['clear_outside'].append('%s %s harvest %s' % (bot, k, f.get('block'))); out['clear_outside'] += 1
            elif op == 'harvest' and plot == CANEFARM:
                # canefarm-01 CF1: sugar cane cut at the second block of a cane cell of the bed's record, nothing else
                out['harvests'] += 1
                if rec is not None and not cane_harvest_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('block')):
                    lists['clear_outside'].append('%s %s cane harvest %s' % (bot, k, f.get('block'))); out['clear_outside'] += 1
            elif op == 'harvest' and plot == FOREST and not str(f.get('kind') or '').endswith('_failed'):
                # forest-01 FR1: a log in a plot column (grown) or a listed site-tree log (site), nothing else
                out['harvests'] += 1
                if rec is not None and not forest_harvest_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('block'), f.get('kind')):
                    lists['clear_outside'].append('%s %s %s %s forest_fell' % (bot, k, f.get('kind'), f.get('block'))); out['clear_outside'] += 1
            elif op == 'harvest' and plot == TREEFARM and f.get('kind') == 'retire':
                # forest-01 RF1: the retirement's felling, a log in a plot column of the farm's chain
                out['harvests'] += 1
                if rec is not None and not farm_retire_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('block'), 'retire'):
                    lists['clear_outside'].append('%s %s retire %s farm_retire' % (bot, k, f.get('block'))); out['clear_outside'] += 1
            elif op == 'use' and plot == FOREST:
                # forest-01 FR4: bone meal on a plot cell of a generation, nothing else
                out['forest_meals'] = out.get('forest_meals', 0) + 1
                if rec is not None and not forest_meal_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell):
                    out['off_record'] += 1; lists['off_record'].append('%s %s %s forest_meal' % (bot, k, f.get('block')))
            elif op == 'replant':
                out['replants'] += 1
                # outbands-01 (owner 11-04, groundskeeper piece 5: "cut down trees and replant in better spots" -- never on a road or a
                # path): a tidy replant on a land-plan road corridor or a laid path's cell is off the record (`on_road` / `on_path`)
                if plot == TIDY:
                    rwhy = replant_road_why(projects.get(pool), cell)
                    if rwhy:
                        lists['off_record'].append('%s %s %s %s' % (bot, k, f.get('block'), rwhy))
                        out['off_record'] += 1
            elif op in ('replant_moved', 'replant_deferred', 'replant_skipped', 'deferred', 'stuck_on_cell', 'stuck_unwritten'):
                out[op] = out.get(op, 0) + 1; lists.setdefault(op, []).append('%s %s %s' % (bot, k, f.get('to') or ''))
                if op == 'stuck_on_cell' and f.get('stalled') == 'true':
                    stalled_pools.setdefault(pool, f.get('waiting') or '?')
            elif op == 'clear':
                out['clears'] += 1
                why = ''
                if rec is not None:
                    if plot == TIDY:
                        # tidyread-01 (11-03, showcase 62e2aff0 TRIP): the tidy record has no box; its cuts are judged against
                        # its own planned lists across every stage generation, never the ziggurat's zigtidy box rule
                        if pool not in guards:
                            guards[pool] = tidy_guard(projects.get(pool))
                        t_ms = int(r['t'].timestamp() * 1000) if hasattr(r['t'], 'timestamp') else None
                        chain_t = project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec]
                        if f.get('kind') == 'gather':
                            # gkhelpers-01: the park gather -- a plant, off every reservation, outside the park (gather_ok)
                            lrec = project_of(projects.get(pool), LAND).get('record') or {}
                            pbox = (lrec.get('park') or {}).get('box') or next((g.get('park', {}).get('box') for g in reversed(chain_t) if isinstance(g.get('park'), dict) and g['park'].get('box')), None)
                            ok, why = gather_ok(chain_t, cell, f.get('block'), t_ms, guards[pool], pbox)
                        else:
                            ok = tidy_clear_ok(chain_t, cell, f.get('kind'), t_ms, guards[pool])
                            why = ' guarded' if tidy_guarded(cell, t_ms, guards[pool]) else ''
                    elif plot == CROPFARM and f.get('kind') == 'seedgrass':
                        # cropfarm-01 G9: the seed gather -- a grass plant, off every record and marker, outside the park, near the farm
                        if pool not in guards:
                            guards[pool] = tidy_guard(projects.get(pool))
                        t_ms = int(r['t'].timestamp() * 1000) if hasattr(r['t'], 'timestamp') else None
                        lrec = project_of(projects.get(pool), LAND).get('record') or {}
                        ok, why = seedgrass_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('block'), t_ms, guards[pool], (lrec.get('park') or {}).get('box'), tidy_heads_at(project_of(projects.get(pool), TIDY).get('chain') or [], t_ms))
                    elif plot == FOREST and f.get('kind') in ('stand', 'aisle'):
                        # forest-01 FR2: the leaves in a stand's or an aisle's feet and head, inside the forest's box + 1 and its band
                        ok = forest_leaves_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('block'))
                        why = '' if ok else ' forest_leafrule'
                    elif plot == TREEFARM and f.get('kind') in ('stand', 'retire'):
                        # forest-01 RF1: the retirement's saplings, torches and stand leaves
                        ok = farm_retire_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('block'), f.get('kind'))
                        why = '' if ok else ' farm_retire'
                    elif plot == WALLDEMO:
                        # wallext-02 WD1: an old wall cell of the shared line, listed, after the extension stood (demo_clear_ok)
                        t_ms = int(r['t'].timestamp() * 1000) if hasattr(r['t'], 'timestamp') else None
                        pr = projects.get(pool)
                        chain_of = lambda pl: project_of(pr, pl).get('chain') or ([project_of(pr, pl)['record']] if project_of(pr, pl).get('record') else [])
                        ok = demo_clear_ok(chain_of(plot) or [rec], chain_of('wall'), chain_of('wallext'), cell, f.get('kind'), t_ms)
                        why = '' if ok else ' demo'
                    elif plot == STEPWELL:
                        # stepwell-02 SW1: its digs over the chain (a portal cell was a wall cell, then a cut)
                        ok = stepwell_clear_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('kind'))
                        why = '' if ok else ' stepwell'
                    elif plot == DECOR and f.get('kind') in ('fern', 'leaves', 'wild'):
                        # decorator-02 DC4: the fern gather -- a fern near home, off every record cell, marker, the park and the tidy head
                        if pool not in guards:
                            guards[pool] = tidy_guard(projects.get(pool))
                        t_ms = int(r['t'].timestamp() * 1000) if hasattr(r['t'], 'timestamp') else None
                        lrec = project_of(projects.get(pool), LAND).get('record') or {}
                        lv = f.get('kind') == 'leaves'   # decorator-03 DC5: the hedge's leaves, the fern rule over DECOR_LEAVES
                        wd = f.get('kind') == 'wild'     # flowers-01 DC6: the wild flower pick, the fern rule over DECOR_SMALL within DECOR_WILD_R
                        ok, why = decor_fern_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('block'), t_ms, guards[pool],
                                                (lrec.get('park') or {}).get('box'), tidy_heads_at(project_of(projects.get(pool), TIDY).get('chain') or [], t_ms),
                                                names=DECOR_LEAVES if lv else DECOR_SMALL if wd else DECOR_FERNS, tag=' leaves_rule' if lv else ' wild_rule' if wd else ' fern_rule',
                                                r=DECOR_WILD_R if wd else DECOR_FERN_R)
                    elif plot == DECOR and f.get('kind') == 'bloom':
                        # decorator-01 DC2: the nursery's harvest -- a bloom near a nursery centre in force, off every record cell and marker
                        if pool not in guards:
                            guards[pool] = tidy_guard(projects.get(pool))
                        t_ms = int(r['t'].timestamp() * 1000) if hasattr(r['t'], 'timestamp') else None
                        lrec = project_of(projects.get(pool), LAND).get('record') or {}
                        ok, why = decor_bloom_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('block'), t_ms, guards[pool],
                                                 (lrec.get('park') or {}).get('box'), tidy_heads_at(project_of(projects.get(pool), TIDY).get('chain') or [], t_ms))
                    elif plot in SINK_PLOTS:
                        # picker-01: the clear lists (kind=foreign: the cells) of the generations in force when it was dug
                        t_ms = int(r['t'].timestamp() * 1000) if hasattr(r['t'], 'timestamp') else None
                        ok = sink_clear_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('kind'), t_ms)
                    elif f.get('kind') == REROOF_DECOR_KIND:
                        # reroof-02 RT2: a retrofit's dig of a decorator ornament standing in its new roof (listed, a decor cell)
                        pr = projects.get(pool)
                        dchain = project_of(pr, DECOR).get('chain') or ([project_of(pr, DECOR)['record']] if project_of(pr, DECOR).get('record') else [])
                        ok = reroof_decor_ok(rec, dchain, cell, f.get('block'), plot)
                        why = '' if ok else ' decor_post'
                    elif f.get('kind') == 'reorient':
                        # townstyle-01 RO1: the builder's own block in the wrong state, on a state-bearing cell of the chain
                        ok = reorient_ok(project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec], cell, f.get('block'))
                        why = '' if ok else ' reorient'
                    elif f.get('kind') in ('foreign', 'cover'):
                        # coverread-01 (11-05 23:39Z showcase TRIP, batch 11's coverdig-01 005e2932): kind=cover is the bot's own
                        # scaffold-class block dug out of an OPEN cell of its record over an unreached cell (buildrun.mjs coverAbove)
                        # -- a foreign dig of its own cell, judged the same
                        ok = any(k in {ck(c['at']) for c in rr.get('cells') or []} for rr in (rec, rec_pre) if rr is not None)
                    elif f.get('kind') == 'tidy' and plot == YARD:
                        # yardread-01: the clerk's refill digs (pile tops) and its aisle clutter
                        ok = any(yard_tidy_ok(g, cell) for g in (project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or [rec]))
                    elif f.get('kind') == 'tidy':
                        # zigtidy-01 (10-30): a climbing pillar dug off a finished ziggurat -- NOT a record cell, inside the
                        # record's box + 1, from its base up to its top + 2 (bots/src/ziggurat.mjs zigTidy)
                        ok = tidy_ok(rec, cell)
                    elif f.get('kind') == 'overburden':
                        # pathsclose-01 (10-30): dirt or grass SET ON a path cell after siting, dug so the shovel can lay it
                        ok = any(overburden_ok(rr, cell, any_role=(plot in WALL_PLOTS)) for rr in (rec, rec_pre) if rr is not None)
                    else:
                        # forest-01 RF1: a retired farm's earlier clears against its clears as they were (rec_pre)
                        ok = any(k in {ck(c['at']) for c in rr.get('clear') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')} for rr in (rec, rec_pre) if rr is not None)
                    if not ok:
                        out['clear_outside'] += 1; lists['clear_outside'].append('%s %s %s %s%s' % (bot, k, f.get('kind'), f.get('block'), why))
                if f.get('kind') == 'tree':
                    out['tree_clears'] += 1
                    m = re.match(r'^(\d+)/(\d+)$', f.get('saplings') or '')
                    # a SKIPPED replant (shop-01 F2: no plantable cell in the band from where the builder stood) is no obligation
                    need = len([x for x in rec.get('replant') or [] if isinstance(x, dict) and x.get('state') != 'skipped']) if rec is not None else None
                    # G5R (phase 1 item 4, 11-06): the bot's denominator is the replant list of the record IT ran when it felled
                    # (buildrun.mjs: the non-skipped entries then). Judging every past felling against the CURRENT record made
                    # 47 of 52 live items g5_unjudged (a later generation, or a replant skipped since): judge it against the
                    # generations in force at the row's time instead (g5_denominator_ok)
                    chain_g5 = project_of(projects.get(pool), plot).get('chain') or ([rec] if rec is not None else [])
                    t_g5 = int(r['t'].timestamp() * 1000) if hasattr(r['t'], 'timestamp') else None
                    if not m or int(m.group(1)) > int(m.group(2)) or (rec is not None and not g5_denominator_ok(chain_g5, int(m.group(2)), t_g5)):
                        out['g5_unjudged'] += 1; lists['g5_unjudged'].append('%s %s saplings=%s record=%s' % (bot, k, f.get('saplings'), need))
                    elif int(m.group(1)) < int(m.group(2)) and f.get('first') == '1':
                        # FF1 (fellfirst-01, 11-06; bots/src/buildrun.mjs fellFirstOk): a tree felled FIRST because no sapling of its kind
                        # existed in any bag or town chest (the stepwell's 'felling complete, 17 logs left' loop) -- its own drops replant it.
                        # Counted (a watch line), never H5; any other short felling is still H5
                        out['felled_first'] += 1; lists['felled_first'].append('%s %s saplings=%s' % (bot, k, f.get('saplings')))
                    elif int(m.group(1)) < int(m.group(2)) and g5_pertree(chain_g5, cell, t_g5) is not None:
                        # PT1 (11-06, the showcase's H5 27 on the stepwell's felling): under per-tree B4 the record-wide a < b is no H5 --
                        # a tree that owed no replant itself is clean; one that owed some cannot be told from this row (it carries the
                        # record's count, not the tree's): unjudged, never H5
                        if g5_pertree(chain_g5, cell, t_g5) > 0:
                            out['g5_unjudged'] += 1
                            lists['g5_unjudged'].append('%s %s saplings=%s pertree' % (bot, k, f.get('saplings')))
                    elif int(m.group(1)) < int(m.group(2)):
                        out['felled_unplanted'] += 1; lists['felled_unplanted'].append('%s %s saplings=%s' % (bot, k, f.get('saplings')))
                unknown_open.pop((bot, k), None)
            elif op == 'unknown':
                out['unknown_rows'] += 1
                unknown_open[(bot, k)] = r['t']
            elif op == 'known':
                unknown_open.pop((bot, k), None)
    for pool, plot in sorted(mutating):
        pj = project_of(projects.get(pool), plot)
        if pj.get('record') is None:
            out['oracle_missing'] += 1; lists['oracle_missing'].append('%s (record files %s)' % (pool if plot == SHOP else '%s %s' % (pool, plot), pj.get('records_files', 0)))
    for pool in sorted({p for p, _ in mutating}):
        if not (projects.get(pool) or {}).get('zones_ok'):
            out['zone_unknown'] += 1; lists['zone_unknown'].append(pool)
    out['towns_mutating'] = len({p for p, _ in mutating})
    for pool, pr in projects.items():
        plots = sorted(pr['projects']) if isinstance(pr.get('projects'), dict) else [SHOP]
        for plot in plots:
            pj = project_of(pr, plot)
            leases = pj.get('leases') or {}
            out['lease_files'] += sum(len(v) for v in leases.values())
            out['lease_names'] += len(leases)
            bmax = (pj.get('record') or {}).get('builders_max')
            bmax = bmax if isinstance(bmax, int) and not isinstance(bmax, bool) and bmax > 0 else BUILDERS_MAX
            for name, gen, why in lease_overlaps(leases, bmax):
                # the workshop's items keep their old words: the tripwire's acknowledgements (ACK.json) match an item by its text
                where = pool if plot == SHOP else '%s %s' % (pool, plot)
                out['lease_overlap'] += 1; lists['lease_overlap'].append('%s %s g%s: %s' % (where, name, gen, why))
            if plot == TIDY:
                # gkhelpers-01: the grounds crew (keeper + helpers), judged on the leases taken since the read's cut
                cut_ms = max(CREW_EPOCH_MS, int(cut.timestamp() * 1000) if hasattr(cut, 'timestamp') else 0)
                for name, gen, why in crew_overlaps(leases, pj.get('chain') or ([pj['record']] if pj.get('record') else []), since_ms=cut_ms):
                    lists['lease_overlap'].append('%s %s %s g%s: crew %s' % (pool, plot, name, gen, why)); out['lease_overlap'] += 1
    out['complete_towns'] = len(complete_at)
    lists['complete_towns'] = sorted(complete_at)
    boxes = {p: record_extent(pr.get('record')) for p, pr in projects.items()}
    world, litter = world_evidence(list(gens), cut, complete_at, boxes) if cut is not None else ({}, {})
    out['complete_towns_room'] = sum(1 for v in world.values() if v == 'room')
    lists['world'] = ['%s: %s' % kv_ for kv_ in sorted(world.items())]
    present, by_pool = surveys if surveys is not None else (False, {})
    out['conformance_source'] = int(bool(present))
    conf = {p: conformance(by_pool.get(p), (projects.get(p) or {}).get('record'), t) for p, t in complete_at.items()}
    out['complete_towns_conform'] = sum(1 for ok, _ in conf.values() if ok)
    lists['conformance'] = ['%s: %s' % (p, why) for p, (ok, why) in sorted(conf.items())]
    lists['_litter'] = litter
    lists['_cans'] = sorted(cans)
    out['stalled_pools'] = stalled_pools
    return out, lists


def combine(out, lists):
    g = lambda k: out.get(k, 0)
    res = {k: v for k, v in out.items()}
    for k in ('off_record', 'clear_outside', 'zone', 'lease_overlap', 'felled_unplanted', 'g5_unjudged', 'unknown_stood', 'bag_loss',
              'bag_unjudged', 'oracle_missing', 'zone_unknown', 'placed_twice', 'kept_thrown', 'kept_unjudged'):
        res[k] = g(k)
    res['arms_breach'] = g('arms_rows_control_post')
    res['liveness'] = int(g('licence_canary') >= 1 and g('visits_canary') >= 1)
    # EXPOSURE IS RECORD CONFORMANCE AND PERSISTENCE (Codex read r3; owner 10-25): two surveys >= 1 h apart with F1 >= 0.98 and the
    # newest still >= 0.98, per town; no survey mirror -> never ready (fail closed). The room check stays PRELIMINARY, reported.
    res['conformance_source'] = g('conformance_source')
    res['exposure_ready'] = int(res['conformance_source'] == 1 and g('complete_towns_conform') >= EXPOSE_TOWNS)
    res['instrument_control'] = int(g('craft_post_control') >= 1 and g('gather_post_control') >= 1)
    for name, key in (('gather_ok', 'gather'), ('rung_ok', 'rung')):
        cells = [(g('%s_ok_pre_canary' % key), g('%s_pre_canary' % key)), (g('%s_ok_post_canary' % key), g('%s_post_canary' % key)),
                 (g('%s_ok_pre_control' % key), g('%s_pre_control' % key)), (g('%s_ok_post_control' % key), g('%s_post_control' % key))]
        d, z = did_z(cells)
        res['%s_did' % name] = d; res['%s_z' % name] = z
        res[name] = 1 if d is None or z is None or not (d < TOL and z < -2) else 0
    lit = lists.get('_litter') or {}
    can = [b - a for p, (a, b) in lit.items() if p in set(lists.get('_cans') or [])]
    con = [b - a for p, (a, b) in lit.items() if p not in set(lists.get('_cans') or [])]
    res['litter_did'] = (sum(can) / len(can) - sum(con) / len(con)) if can and con else None
    res['_lists'] = dict(lists)
    return res


def verdict_of(res, name):
    if res.get('%s_did' % name) is None or res.get('%s_z' % name) is None:
        return 'UNEXPOSED'
    return 'ok' if res[name] else 'DOWN'


def selftest():
    t0 = dt.datetime(2026, 10, 26, tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc)
    CUT = t0 - dt.timedelta(hours=1)
    R = lambda name, detail, arm='canary', per='post', s=0, status='success', bot='board-a-Alpha', item=None, feet=None: {
        't': t0 + dt.timedelta(seconds=s), 'per': per, 'arm': arm, 'name': name, 'detail': detail, 'status': status, 'bot': bot, 'item': item, 'feet': feet}
    rec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'box': [9, 9, 13, 12], 'cells': [{'at': [10, 70, 10], 'role': 'base', 'seg': 'pad'}, {'at': [11, 70, 10], 'role': 'base', 'seg': 'pad'}],
           'clear': [{'at': [12, 71, 10], 'kind': 'tree', 'name': 'oak_log'}], 'replant': [{'cell': [20, 70, 20], 'sapling': 'oak_sapling'}]}
    PR = lambda **kw: {'record': rec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'zones_ok': True, **kw}
    proj = {'board-a': PR(), 'board-b': PR()}
    room = lambda x, z, lit=True: json.dumps({'centre': [x, 71, z], 'lit': lit})
    town = lambda b, lit_c: {'buildings': b, 'building_rooms': [room(11, 10)] * b if lit_c else ([room(60, 60)] * b), 'litter_cells': 100}
    def day(b_a, b_b, la=100, lb=100, lc=100, where_a=True):
        return {'board-a': {**town(b_a, where_a), 'litter_cells': la}, 'board-b': {**town(b_b, True), 'litter_cells': lb}, 'hive-b': {'buildings': 0, 'building_rooms': [], 'litter_cells': lc}}
    gens = [(CUT - dt.timedelta(hours=2), day(0, 0)), (t0 + dt.timedelta(hours=3), day(1, 1))]
    base = [R('_build_mode', 'phase=mode v=1 shop=on'), R('_build', 'project=shop op=sited cell=10,10 gen=1', s=5),
            R('_build', 'project=shop op=replant cell=20,70,20 block=oak_sapling', s=8),
            R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=1/1', s=9),
            R('_build', 'project=shop op=place seg=pad cell=10,70,10 block=stone_bricks', s=10),
            R('_build', 'project=shop op=place seg=pad cell=11,70,10 block=stone_bricks', s=11),
            R('_build_visit', 'project=shop outcome=complete stop=complete placed=2 refused=0', s=12),
            R('_build_bag', 'project=shop exit=complete silent=0 stone_bricks=0/2/0/2/0/0 cobblestone=64/0/0/0/0/60', s=12),
            R('_structure_complete', 'project=shop op=complete kind=workshop@1 gen=2', s=12),
            R('_build', 'project=shop op=place seg=pad cell=10,70,10 block=stone_bricks', s=15, bot='board-b-Alpha'),
            R('_structure_complete', 'project=shop op=complete', s=20, bot='board-b-Alpha'),
            R('gather', '', arm='control', bot='hive-b-A'), R('craft', '', arm='control', bot='hive-b-A')]
    J = lambda rows, projects=proj, zones=None, g=gens: combine(*judge(rows, projects, zones or {}, cut=CUT, gens=g))
    res = J(base)
    for k in ('off_record', 'clear_outside', 'zone', 'lease_overlap', 'felled_unplanted', 'g5_unjudged', 'unknown_stood', 'bag_loss', 'oracle_missing', 'zone_unknown', 'arms_breach'):
        assert res[k] == 0, (k, res)
    assert res['liveness'] == 1 and res['instrument_control'] == 1, res
    assert res['placements'] == 3 and res['complete_towns'] == 2 and res['complete_towns_room'] == 2, res
    assert res['exposure_ready'] == 0 and res['conformance_source'] == 0, 'Codex read r3: never READY without a conformance source'
    # CONFORMANCE (owner 10-25): two valid surveys of THIS record >= 60 min apart, F1 >= 0.98, the newest too
    hd = {'_gen': 1, '_sha': 'abc'}
    SV = lambda pool, m, f1, valid=True, gen=1, sha='abc': {'pool': pool, 'record_gen': gen, 'record_sha': sha, 'valid': valid, 'f1': f1,
                                                         'at': (t0 + dt.timedelta(minutes=m + SAVE_LAG_MIN)).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ'),
                                                         'region_mtime': (t0 - dt.timedelta(hours=5)).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')}
    since = t0
    assert conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0), SV('a', 70, 1.0)], hd, since)[0]
    assert not conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0), SV('a', 40, 1.0)], hd, since)[0], 'not 60 min apart'
    assert not conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0)], hd, since)[0], 'one survey'
    assert not conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0), SV('a', 70, 0.979)], hd, since)[0], 'the newest below 0.98 (damage, or 7 cells missing)'
    assert not conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0), SV('a', 70, 1.0), SV('a', 130, 0.97)], hd, since)[0], 'damage after a good pair revokes'
    assert not conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0), SV('a', 70, 1.0, valid=False)], hd, since)[0], 'an invalid survey never counts'
    assert not conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0, gen=2), SV('a', 70, 1.0, gen=2)], hd, since)[0], 'another record generation'
    assert not conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0, sha='x'), SV('a', 70, 1.0, sha='x')], hd, since)[0], 'another record body'
    assert not conformance([SV('a', -90, 1.0), SV('a', -10, 1.0)], hd, since)[0], 'world states before the completion'
    assert not conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0), SV('a', 70, 1.0)], None, since)[0]
    # r6: a bad survey between two good ones breaks the pair; the pair after it counts (Claude P3-4)
    assert not conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0), SV('a', 40, 0.9), SV('a', 70, 1.0)], hd, since)[0], 'good, damaged, repaired: no hour after the damage'
    assert conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0), SV('a', 40, 0.9), SV('a', 50, 1.0), SV('a', 115, 1.0)], hd, since)[0], 'an hour of good surveys after the damage'
    # r6: malformed fields are no observation, never a crash (Codex P2)
    naive = dict(SV('a', 70, 1.0), at=(t0 + dt.timedelta(minutes=76)).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S'))
    for bad in (naive, dict(SV('a', 70, 1.0), f1='oops'), dict(SV('a', 70, 1.0), f1=float('nan')), dict(SV('a', 70, 1.0), f1=1.5), dict(SV('a', 70, 1.0), at=None), 'junk'):
        assert not conformance([SV('a', 5, 1.0), bad], hd, since)[0], bad
    # through judge: both towns conformant -> READY; one -> not; no mirror -> never
    projc = {p: dict(proj[p], record=dict(rec, _gen=1, _sha='abc')) for p in proj}
    sv_both = (True, {'board-a': [SV('board-a', 30, 1.0), SV('board-a', 100, 1.0)], 'board-b': [SV('board-b', 30, 1.0), SV('board-b', 100, 0.99)]})
    rc = combine(*judge(base, projc, {}, cut=CUT, gens=gens, surveys=sv_both))
    assert rc['conformance_source'] == 1 and rc['complete_towns_conform'] == 2 and rc['exposure_ready'] == 1, rc
    sv_one = (True, {'board-a': sv_both[1]['board-a'], 'board-b': [SV('board-b', 30, 1.0)]})
    assert combine(*judge(base, projc, {}, cut=CUT, gens=gens, surveys=sv_one))['exposure_ready'] == 0
    assert combine(*judge(base, projc, {}, cut=CUT, gens=gens, surveys=(False, sv_both[1])))['exposure_ready'] == 0, 'no mirror: fail closed'
    assert res['bag_scaffold'] == 4, 'cobblestone short by 4: scaffold, reported, not a loss'
    # G1 off the record
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=place seg=pad cell=30,70,30 block=stone_bricks', s=13)])['off_record'] == 1
    # G2 a clear outside the clear list; a foreign clear outside the record's cells; a foreign clear INSIDE them is clean
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=40,70,40 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=1/1', s=14)])['clear_outside'] == 1
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear seg=pad cell=50,70,50 block=dirt kind=foreign', s=14)])['clear_outside'] == 1
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear seg=pad cell=10,70,10 block=dirt kind=foreign', s=9.5)])['clear_outside'] == 0
    # coverread-01: a cover dug from a record cell is clean; one off the record trips (positive control)
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear seg=pad cell=10,70,10 block=grass_block kind=cover', s=9.5)])['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a cover in its own record cell'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear seg=pad cell=50,70,50 block=grass_block kind=cover', s=9.5)])['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a cover off the record'
    # G3 zone: a placement, and a REPLANT (Codex read r1 P2-6)
    assert J(base, zones={'board-a': [[10, 0, 10, 10, 200, 10]]})['zone'] == 1
    assert J(base, zones={'board-a': [[20, 0, 20, 20, 200, 20]]})['zone'] == 1, 'the replant cell'
    # G4: B took seg-pad while A's unreleased lease still blocked; a renewal by the SAME holder is fine; released -> fine; and the
    # WALL segments' names (upper case, Claude read r1 P2-1) are read
    L = lambda holder, at, until: {'holder': holder, 'at': at, 'until': until}
    p4 = {**proj, 'board-a': PR(leases={'seg-pad': [(1, L('A', 0, 240_000), None), (2, L('B', 100_000, 340_000), None)]})}
    assert J(base, p4)['lease_overlap'] == 1
    p4b = {**proj, 'board-a': PR(leases={'seg-pad': [(1, L('A', 0, 240_000), None), (2, L('A', 100_000, 340_000), None), (3, L('B', 340_000 + RECLAIM_MS + 1, 1e9), None)]})}
    assert J(base, p4b)['lease_overlap'] == 0, 'a renewal and a take after the reclaim are clean'
    p4c = {**proj, 'board-a': PR(leases={'seg-pad': [(1, L('A', 0, 240_000), 50_000), (2, L('B', 60_000, 300_000), None)]})}
    assert J(base, p4c)['lease_overlap'] == 0, 'released before the take'
    assert J(base, {**proj, 'board-a': PR(leases={'slot3': [(1, L('C', 0, 1), None)]})})['lease_overlap'] == 1
    assert LEASE_RE.match('project-0_70_0.shop.seg-wall-F.lease.g3.json'), 'the wall segments are read'
    assert LEASE_RE.match('project-0_70_0.shop.slot2.lease.g12.json') and LEASE_RE.match('project--5_64_17.shop.seg-roof-edge.lease.g1.json')
    assert not LEASE_RE.match('project-0_70_0.shop.seg-pad.lease.g3.json.released')
    # EVERY PROJECT'S RECORD (house-01 + monument-01, 10-29): a row is judged against ITS project's record
    assert REC_RE.match('project-0_70_0.house1.g2.json').group(5) == 'house1' and REC_RE.match('project-0_70_0.shop.g3.json').group(5) == 'shop'
    assert not REC_RE.match('project-0_70_0.shop.barrier.g1.json') and not REC_RE.match('project-0_70_0.shop.mutate.g213.json')
    assert LEASE_RE.match('project-0_70_0.monument.seg-L03-N.lease.g4.json').group(6) == 'seg-L03-N'
    hrec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'cells': [{'at': [30, 71, 20], 'role': 'base', 'seg': 'pad'}], 'clear': [], 'replant': []}
    mrec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'cells': [{'at': [-30, 70, -30], 'role': 'core', 'seg': 'L00-N'}], 'builders_max': 3,
            'clear': [{'at': [-31, 71, -30], 'kind': 'leaves', 'name': 'spruce_leaves'}, {'at': [-31, 72, -30], 'kind': 'tree', 'name': 'spruce_log'}], 'replant': []}
    PP = lambda **more: {'board-a': {**PR(), 'projects': {'shop': {'record': rec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}}, **more}}, 'board-b': PR()}
    both = PP(house1={'record': hrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}}, monument={'record': mrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}})
    hp = R('_build', 'project=house1 op=place seg=pad cell=30,71,20 block=stone_bricks', s=13)
    mp = R('_build', 'project=monument op=place seg=L00-N cell=-30,70,-30 block=cobblestone', s=13)
    rp = J(base + [hp, mp], both)
    assert rp['off_record'] == 0 and rp['oracle_missing'] == 0, ('each against its own record', rp['_lists'].get('off_record'))
    assert J(base + [hp], PP())['off_record'] == 0 and J(base + [hp], PP())['oracle_missing'] == 1, 'a house with no record: not judged against the workshop, named'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=house1 op=place seg=pad cell=31,71,20 block=stone_bricks', s=13)], both)['off_record'] == 1, 'positive control: off the HOUSE record'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=house1 op=place seg=pad cell=10,70,10 block=stone_bricks', s=13)], both)['off_record'] == 1, 'a workshop cell is not a house cell'
    assert J(base + [hp])['off_record'] == 0 and J(base + [hp])['oracle_missing'] == 1, 'a caller without `projects` (the old shape): a house row has no record there -- named, never judged against the workshop'
    mc = [R('_build', 'project=monument op=clear cell=-31,71,-30 block=spruce_leaves kind=leaves', s=14),
          R('_build', 'project=monument op=clear cell=-31,72,-30 block=spruce_log kind=tree saplings=0/0', s=14)]
    rm = J(base + mc, both)
    assert rm['clear_outside'] == 0 and rm['felled_unplanted'] == 0 and rm['g5_unjudged'] == 0, ('the ziggurat\'s clears, no replant owed', rm['_lists'].get('clear_outside'), rm['_lists'].get('g5_unjudged'))
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=monument op=clear cell=-40,71,-30 block=dirt kind=cut', s=14)], both)['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: a cut off the list'
    # zigtidy-01: a pillar dug off the finished ziggurat is its own record's tidy -- inside box + 1, never a record cell, never above top + 2
    trec = {**mrec, 'box': [-31, -31, -29, -29], 'baseY': 70}
    tb = PP(monument={'record': trec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}})
    tidy = lambda c: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=monument op=clear cell=%s block=dirt kind=tidy' % c, s=14)], tb)['clear_outside']
    assert tidy('-30,71,-30') == 0 and tidy('-32,70,-28') == 0, 'on the build and in its apron: tidy'
    assert tidy('-30,70,-30') == 1, 'a record cell is never a tidy'
    assert tidy('-34,71,-30') == 1 and tidy('-30,73,-30') == 1 and tidy('-30,69,-30') == 1, 'off the box + 1, above the top + 2, below the base: outside'
    # tidyread-01 (11-03, the showcase TRIP on 62e2aff0): a project=tidy clear is judged against the TIDY record's own lists across
    # every stage generation -- the record has no box, so the zigtidy rule read its 4 stage-0 cuts as clear_outside
    T0MS = int(t0.timestamp() * 1000)
    tg1 = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'tidy', 'stage': 0, 'at': T0MS - 60_000, 'cells': [{'at': [17, 64, -2], 'role': 'core', 'seg': 'T-00-0'}],
           'cut': [{'at': [3, 73, -10], 'kind': 'tidy', 'name': 'dirt'}, {'at': [-8, 72, 16], 'kind': 'tidy', 'name': 'dirt'}, {'at': [-30, 71, -30], 'kind': 'tidy', 'name': 'dirt'}], 'clear': [], 'replant': []}
    tg2 = {'v': 1, 'gen': 2, 'prev': 1, 'plot': 'tidy', 'stage': 1, 'at': T0MS - 30_000, 'cells': [],
           'cut': [{'at': [30, 72, 0], 'kind': 'tidy', 'name': 'dirt', 'pillar': 'road'}], 'clear': [{'at': [31, 74, 0], 'kind': 'leaves', 'name': 'oak_leaves'}], 'replant': []}
    files = [(1, json.dumps(tg1).encode())]
    files.append((2, json.dumps(dict(tg2, prevSha=hashlib.sha256(files[0][1]).hexdigest())).encode()))
    chain = record_chain(files)
    assert [c['_gen'] for c in chain] == [1, 2] and record_head(files)['_gen'] == 2, 'the chain walks both stages; the head is stage 1'
    assert [c['_gen'] for c in record_chain([files[0], (2, json.dumps(dict(tg2, prevSha='bad')).encode())])] == [1], 'a broken link never joins'
    mk = {ck([40, 70 + dy, 40]): T0MS - 90_000 for dy in (0, 1, 2)}
    TP_ = lambda chain_=chain, markers=mk, mon=mrec: {'board-a': {**PR(), 'markers': markers, 'projects': {
        'shop': {'record': rec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [rec]},
        'monument': {'record': mon, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [mon]},
        'tidy': {'record': chain_[-1] if chain_ else None, 'records_files': len(chain_), 'leases': {}, 'chain': chain_}}}, 'board-b': PR()}
    tc = lambda c, kind='tidy', pp=None, s_=14, block='dirt': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=tidy op=clear seg=T-00-0 cell=%s block=%s kind=%s' % (c, block, kind), s=s_)], pp or TP_())
    assert tc('3,73,-10')['clear_outside'] == 0 and tc('-8,72,16')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'stage 0 cuts, read after the head moved to stage 1'
    assert tc('30,72,0')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a stage 1 (road) cut'
    assert tc('31,74,0', kind='leaves', block='oak_leaves')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a stage 1 road clear'
    assert tc('3,74,-10')['clear_outside'] == 1 and tc('5,70,5')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: a tidy cut off every list trips'
    assert tc('17,64,-2')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a fill cell cut as a tidy is off the cut lists'
    # tidyread-02 (11-03, the terrace agent's replay): a stage-0 FILL placed in the read window after stage 1 is the head (stage 1
    # carries no cells over) is judged against the generations in force when it was placed, never the head alone
    tpl = lambda c, s_=14, pp=None: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=tidy op=place seg=T-00-0 cell=%s block=cobblestone' % c, s=s_)], pp or TP_())
    assert tpl('17,64,-2')['off_record'] == 0, 'a stage-0 fill read after the head moved to stage 1'
    assert tpl('5,70,5')['off_record'] == 1 and tpl('30,72,0')['off_record'] == 1, 'positive control: a planted off-record fill (and a stage-1 CUT cell) trips'
    assert tpl('17,64,-2', pp=TP_(chain_=chain[-1:]))['off_record'] == 1, 'control: judged against the head alone (the old rule), it trips'
    # tidyplaceguard-01: a listed tidy fill on ANOTHER project's record cell (the monument's, sited before) trips, named `guarded`
    mg = dict(mrec, cells=mrec['cells'] + [{'at': [17, 64, -2], 'role': 'core', 'seg': 'L01-N'}], at=T0MS - 120_000)
    gp = tpl('17,64,-2', pp=TP_(mon=mg))
    assert gp['off_record'] == 1 and gp['_lists']['off_record'] == ['board-a-Alpha 17,64,-2 cobblestone guarded'], gp['_lists']['off_record']
    assert tpl('17,64,-2', pp=TP_(mon=dict(mg, at=T0MS + 3_600_000)))['off_record'] == 0, 'a project sited on the cell later does not re-judge it'
    assert tpl('5,70,5')['_lists']['off_record'] == ['board-a-Alpha 5,70,5 cobblestone'], 'an off-record item keeps its old words'
    # outbands-01: a tidy replant on a land corridor or a path's column trips (off_record, `on_road` / `on_path`); a wild cell does not
    lrec_ = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'land', 'cells': [], 'corridors': [{'box': [20, 50, -1, 40, 90, 1], 'why': 'land:road'}]}
    prec_ = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'paths', 'cells': [{'at': [6, 69, 5], 'role': 'path', 'seg': 'P-d-0'}]}
    def RP_(c):
        pp = TP_()
        pp['board-a']['projects'] = dict(pp['board-a']['projects'], land={'record': lrec_, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [lrec_]},
                                         paths={'record': prec_, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [prec_]})
        return J(base + [R('_build', 'project=tidy op=replant cell=%s block=oak_sapling' % c, s=14)], pp)
    assert RP_('30,70,0')['off_record'] == 1 and RP_('30,70,0')['_lists']['off_record'] == ['board-a-Alpha 30,70,0 oak_sapling on_road'], RP_('30,70,0')['_lists']['off_record']
    assert RP_('6,70,5')['_lists']['off_record'] == ['board-a-Alpha 6,70,5 oak_sapling on_path']
    assert RP_('30,70,5')['off_record'] == 0 and RP_('-30,70,-30')['off_record'] == 0, 'a wild cell beside the corridor is clean'
    assert replant_road_why({}, [30, 70, 0]) == '' and replant_road_why({'projects': {'land': {'record': {'corridors': [{'box': [1, 2]}]}}}}, [1, 70, 2]) == '', 'no land record or a bad box: nothing'
    c3 = record_chain(files + [(3, json.dumps(dict(tg2, gen=3, prev=2, stage=2, at=T0MS + 3_600_000, cells=[{'at': [50, 70, 50], 'role': 'core', 'seg': 'T-02-0'}], prevSha=hashlib.sha256(files[1][1]).hexdigest())).encode())])
    assert [c['_gen'] for c in c3] == [1, 2, 3]
    assert tpl('50,70,50', pp=TP_(chain_=c3))['off_record'] == 1, 'a cell of a stage sited AFTER the placement (beyond the skew) is not in force'
    assert tpl('50,70,50', s_=3_700, pp=TP_(chain_=c3))['off_record'] == 0 and tpl('17,64,-2', s_=3_700, pp=TP_(chain_=c3))['off_record'] == 0, 'after it: both stages in force'
    assert tidy_place_ok(chain, [17, 64, -2], None) and not tidy_place_ok(chain, [5, 70, 5], None) and not tidy_place_ok([], [17, 64, -2], 0)
    # picker-01 (11-03): a sink plot's growing chain -- T1 sited before the window, completed, T2 sited an hour in; every row judged
    # against the generations in force when it was written
    sg1 = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'tower', 'blueprint': 'tower@1', 'builders_max': 3, 'at': T0MS - 60_000, 'structure_complete': False,
           'instances': [{'id': 'T1'}], 'cells': [{'at': [40, 70, 40], 'role': 'T1-b0', 'seg': 'T1-L00-N'}, {'at': [40, 71, 40], 'role': 'T1-core', 'seg': 'T1-L01-N'}],
           'clear': [{'at': [41, 72, 40], 'kind': 'leaves', 'name': 'spruce_leaves'}, {'at': [42, 71, 40], 'kind': 'tree', 'name': 'spruce_log'}], 'replant': []}
    sg2 = dict(sg1, gen=2, prev=1, at=T0MS - 30_000, structure_complete=True)
    sg3 = dict(sg2, gen=3, prev=2, at=T0MS + 3_600_000, structure_complete=False, instances=[{'id': 'T1'}, {'id': 'T2'}],
               cells=sg1['cells'] + [{'at': [60, 70, 60], 'role': 'T2-b0', 'seg': 'T2-L00-N'}], clear=sg1['clear'] + [{'at': [61, 71, 60], 'kind': 'cut', 'name': 'dirt'}])
    sf = [(1, json.dumps(sg1).encode())]
    sf.append((2, json.dumps(dict(sg2, prevSha=hashlib.sha256(sf[0][1]).hexdigest())).encode()))
    sf.append((3, json.dumps(dict(sg3, prevSha=hashlib.sha256(sf[1][1]).hexdigest())).encode()))
    schain = record_chain(sf)
    assert [c['_gen'] for c in schain] == [1, 2, 3], 'the sink chain walks every generation'
    SP_ = lambda chain_=schain: {'board-a': {**PR(), 'projects': {'shop': {'record': rec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [rec]},
                                                                 'tower': {'record': chain_[-1] if chain_ else None, 'records_files': len(chain_), 'leases': {}, 'chain': chain_}}}, 'board-b': PR()}
    spl = lambda c, s_=14, pp=None, block='cobblestone': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=tower op=place seg=T1-L00-N cell=%s block=%s' % (c, block), s=s_)], pp or SP_())['off_record']
    assert spl('40,70,40') == 0 and spl('40,71,40', block='andesite') == 0, 'an instance\'s cells, read with the head two generations on'
    assert spl('60,70,60') == 1, 'a cell of an instance sited AFTER the placement (beyond the skew) is off the record'
    assert spl('60,70,60', s_=3_700) == 0 and spl('40,70,40', s_=3_700) == 0, 'after its siting: both instances in force'
    assert spl('44,70,40') == 1 and spl('40,72,40') == 1, 'positive control: a block off every generation trips'
    assert ck([60, 70, 60]) in {ck(c['at']) for c in schain[-1]['cells']}, 'control: the HEAD holds that cell -- the head-only (generic) rule would have passed it'
    scl = lambda c, kind, s_=14, block='spruce_leaves': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=tower op=clear seg=T1-L00-N cell=%s block=%s kind=%s' % (c, block, kind), s=s_)], SP_())['clear_outside']
    assert scl('41,72,40', 'leaves') == 0 and scl('42,71,40', 'tree', block='spruce_log') == 0, 'the listed canopy and trunk'
    assert scl('40,71,40', 'foreign', block='dirt') == 0, 'a foreign block dug out of an open cell'
    assert scl('40,71,40', 'cover', block='dirt') == 0 and scl('45,72,40', 'cover', block='dirt') == 1, 'coverread-01: a sink\'s cover in its own cell; off it, a trip'
    assert scl('61,71,60', 'cut', block='dirt') == 1 and scl('61,71,60', 'cut', s_=3_700, block='dirt') == 0, 'T2\'s cut: only once T2 is sited'
    assert scl('45,72,40', 'leaves') == 1 and scl('41,72,40', 'foreign', block='dirt') == 1, 'positive control: off the list, or a clear dug as foreign'
    assert lease_overlaps({'slot3': [], 'slot4': []}, 3) == [('slot4', None, 'slot beyond builders_max')], 'three builder slots, never a fourth'
    assert sink_place_ok(schain, [40, 70, 40], None) and not sink_place_ok([], [40, 70, 40], 0) and sink_in_force(schain, T0MS) == schain[:2]
    # towndress-01 (11-03): the dress sink -- L1 (a signpost: post, torch, sign) sited before the window, L2 (a wall-top torch) an hour in
    dg1 = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'dress', 'blueprint': 'dress@1', 'builders_max': 3, 'at': T0MS - 60_000, 'structure_complete': False,
           'instances': [{'id': 'L1'}], 'cells': [{'at': [5, 70, 1], 'role': 'L1-post', 'seg': 'L1-P0'}, {'at': [5, 71, 1], 'role': 'L1-post', 'seg': 'L1-P0'},
                                                  {'at': [5, 72, 1], 'role': 'L1-light', 'seg': 'L1-P0'}, {'at': [5, 71, 0], 'role': 'L1-sign', 'seg': 'L1-P0'}], 'clear': [], 'replant': []}
    dg2 = dict(dg1, gen=2, prev=1, at=T0MS + 3_600_000, instances=[{'id': 'L1'}, {'id': 'L2'}], cells=dg1['cells'] + [{'at': [17, 73, -20], 'role': 'L2-light', 'seg': 'L2-P0'}])
    df = [(1, json.dumps(dg1).encode())]
    df.append((2, json.dumps(dict(dg2, prevSha=hashlib.sha256(df[0][1]).hexdigest())).encode()))
    dchain = record_chain(df)
    assert [c['_gen'] for c in dchain] == [1, 2], 'the dress chain walks every generation'
    DP_ = {'board-a': {**PR(), 'projects': {'shop': {'record': rec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [rec]},
