                                            'dress': {'record': dchain[-1], 'records_files': 2, 'leases': {}, 'chain': dchain}}}, 'board-b': PR()}
    dpl = lambda c, s_=14, block='torch', kind='': J(base + [R('_build', ('project=dress op=place seg=L1-P0 cell=%s block=%s' % (c, block)) + (' kind=%s' % kind if kind else ''), s=s_)], DP_)['off_record']
    assert dpl('5,70,1', block='stone_bricks') == 0 and dpl('5,72,1') == 0, 'a lamp post and its torch'
    assert dpl('5,71,0', block='spruce_wall_sign', kind='sign') == 0, 'a sign the label writer placed on its record cell'
    assert dpl('6,71,0', block='spruce_wall_sign', kind='sign') == 1 and dpl('5,73,1') == 1, 'positive control: a sign or a torch off the record trips'
    assert dpl('17,73,-20') == 1 and dpl('17,73,-20', s_=3_700) == 0, 'L2\'s wall-top torch: off the record before L2 is sited, on it after'
    # decorator-01 (11-04): the decor sink -- a nursery-only generation (centre 30,70,30) before the window, D1 (two border flowers and a
    # pot) and a moved nursery (centre 60,70,60; the first kept in `nurseries`) an hour in. DC1 meal, DC2 bloom, DC3 pot fill.
    cg1 = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'decor', 'blueprint': 'decor@1', 'builders_max': 3, 'at': T0MS - 60_000, 'structure_complete': True, 'home': {'x': 0, 'y': 70, 'z': 0},
           'instances': [], 'cells': [], 'clear': [], 'replant': [], 'nursery': {'at': [30, 70, 30], 'box': [23, 23, 37, 37]}}
    cg2 = dict(cg1, gen=2, prev=1, at=T0MS + 3_600_000, structure_complete=False, instances=[{'id': 'D1'}],
               cells=[{'at': [5, 71, 1], 'role': 'D1-f0', 'seg': 'D1-B0'}, {'at': [6, 71, 1], 'role': 'D1-f1', 'seg': 'D1-B0'}, {'at': [3, 71, 3], 'role': 'D1-pot', 'seg': 'D1-P'},
                      {'at': [31, 71, 31], 'role': 'D1-f2', 'seg': 'D1-B1'}],
               nursery={'at': [60, 70, 60], 'box': [53, 53, 67, 67]}, nurseries=[{'at': [30, 70, 30], 'box': [23, 23, 37, 37]}],
               lawns=[{'at': [-20, 70, 15], 'box': [-27, 8, -13, 22], 'sitedAt': T0MS + 3_600_000}])
    cf = [(1, json.dumps(cg1).encode())]
    cf.append((2, json.dumps(dict(cg2, prevSha=hashlib.sha256(cf[0][1]).hexdigest())).encode()))
    cchain = record_chain(cf)
    assert [c['_gen'] for c in cchain] == [1, 2], 'the decor chain walks every generation'
    CP_ = {'board-a': {**PR(), 'projects': {'shop': {'record': rec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [rec]},
                                            'decor': {'record': cchain[-1], 'records_files': 2, 'leases': {}, 'chain': cchain}}}, 'board-b': PR()}
    CJ = lambda d, s_=14, zones=None: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=decor ' + d, s=s_)], CP_, zones=zones)
    # G1: the border flowers and the pot are record cells once D1 is sited
    assert CJ('op=place seg=D1-B0 cell=5,71,1 block=poppy', s_=3_700)['off_record'] == 0 and CJ('op=place seg=D1-P cell=3,71,3 block=flower_pot', s_=3_700)['off_record'] == 0, 'the batch\'s cells'
    assert CJ('op=place seg=D1-B0 cell=5,71,1 block=poppy')['off_record'] == 1, 'positive control: before D1 is sited a flower is off the record'
    # DC3 the pot's fill: a potted block on the pot cell, nothing else
    assert CJ('op=place seg=D1-P cell=3,71,3 block=potted_spruce_sapling kind=pot_fill', s_=3_700)['off_record'] == 0, 'a sapling potted'
    pf = CJ('op=place seg=D1-B0 cell=5,71,1 block=potted_poppy kind=pot_fill', s_=3_700)
    assert pf['off_record'] == 1 and pf['_lists'].get('off_record') == ['board-a-Alpha 5,71,1 potted_poppy pot'], ('a fill on a flower cell', pf['_lists'].get('off_record'))
    assert CJ('op=place seg=D1-P cell=3,71,3 block=poppy kind=pot_fill', s_=3_700)['off_record'] == 1, 'a fill that is no potted block'
    # DC1 the meal: on a nursery centre in force, nothing else
    assert CJ('op=meal cell=30,70,30 block=bone_meal kind=use')['off_record'] == 0, 'the first nursery'
    assert CJ('op=meal cell=60,70,60 block=bone_meal kind=use', s_=3_700)['off_record'] == 0 and CJ('op=meal cell=30,70,30 block=bone_meal kind=use', s_=3_700)['off_record'] == 0, 'the moved nursery, and the old one it keeps'
    mo = CJ('op=meal cell=60,70,60 block=bone_meal kind=use')
    assert mo['off_record'] == 1 and mo['_lists'].get('off_record') == ['board-a-Alpha 60,70,60 bone_meal meal'], ('positive control: the second nursery before it was sited', mo['_lists'].get('off_record'))
    assert CJ('op=meal cell=31,70,30 block=bone_meal kind=use')['off_record'] == 1, 'beside the centre'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=meal cell=30,70,30 block=bone_meal', s=14)], CP_)['off_record'] == 1, 'another project\'s meal'
    assert CJ('op=meal cell=30,70,30 block=bone_meal kind=use', zones={'board-a': [[30, 0, 30, 30, 200, 30]]})['zone'] == 1, 'G3 judges a meal'
    # flowers-02 DC7: a lawn's meal on a lawn centre in force, nothing else -- never a nursery centre, never before the lawn is claimed
    assert CJ('op=meal cell=-20,70,15 block=bone_meal kind=lawn', s_=3_700)['off_record'] == 0, 'the claimed lawn'
    lm = CJ('op=meal cell=-20,70,15 block=bone_meal kind=lawn')
    assert lm['off_record'] == 1 and lm['_lists'].get('off_record') == ['board-a-Alpha -20,70,15 bone_meal lawn_meal'], ('before its claim', lm['_lists'].get('off_record'))
    assert CJ('op=meal cell=30,70,30 block=bone_meal kind=lawn', s_=3_700)['off_record'] == 1, 'a nursery centre is no lawn'
    assert CJ('op=meal cell=-20,70,15 block=bone_meal kind=use', s_=3_700)['off_record'] == 1, 'a lawn centre is no nursery'
    assert CJ('op=meal cell=-19,70,15 block=bone_meal kind=lawn', s_=3_700)['off_record'] == 1, 'beside the lawn centre'
    # DC2 the harvest: a bloom within 7 of a nursery centre in force, never a record cell
    assert CJ('op=clear cell=33,71,28 block=poppy kind=bloom')['clear_outside'] == 0 and CJ('op=clear cell=37,71,30 block=short_grass kind=bloom')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a flower and the grass at 7'
    bo = CJ('op=clear cell=38,71,30 block=poppy kind=bloom')
    assert bo['clear_outside'] == 1 and bo['_lists'].get('clear_outside') == ['board-a-Alpha 38,71,30 bloom poppy bloom'], ('8 from the centre', bo['_lists'].get('clear_outside'))
    assert CJ('op=clear cell=33,71,28 block=stone kind=bloom')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: no bloom'
    assert CJ('op=clear cell=31,71,31 block=poppy kind=bloom', s_=3_700)['clear_outside'] == 1 and CJ('op=clear cell=31,71,31 block=poppy kind=bloom')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'the decor\'s own border flower once sited: never the harvest\'s'
    assert CJ('op=clear cell=62,71,60 block=dandelion kind=bloom')['clear_outside'] == 1 and CJ('op=clear cell=62,71,60 block=dandelion kind=bloom', s_=3_700)['clear_outside'] == 0, 'the moved nursery from its siting on'
    # the selftest's own mutants (anchors present and unique in this file)
    global decor_bloom_ok, decor_meal_ok, decor_pot_ok, decor_fern_ok
    # Claude r1 BLOCKER: the park (+1) and the tidy head's cells are refused too (tidy_guard leaves the tidy out)
    ltrec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'land', 'at': T0MS - 120_000, 'park': {'box': [32, 28, 34, 29]}}
    ttrec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'tidy', 'blueprint': 'tidy@1', 'at': T0MS - 120_000, 'cells': [{'at': [28, 71, 33], 'role': 'flower0', 'seg': 'T-P'}], 'cut': [], 'clear': []}
    CPT_ = {'board-a': {**CP_['board-a'], 'projects': {**CP_['board-a']['projects'], 'land': {'record': ltrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [ltrec]},
                                                       'tidy': {'record': ttrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [ttrec]}}}, 'board-b': PR()}
    CT = lambda d: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=decor ' + d, s=14)], CPT_)
    ip = CT('op=clear cell=35,71,30 block=poppy kind=bloom')
    assert ip['clear_outside'] == 1 and ip['_lists'].get('clear_outside') == ['board-a-Alpha 35,71,30 bloom poppy in_park'], ip['_lists'].get('clear_outside')
    tcl = CT('op=clear cell=28,71,33 block=poppy kind=bloom')
    assert tcl['clear_outside'] == 1 and tcl['_lists'].get('clear_outside') == ['board-a-Alpha 28,71,33 bloom poppy tidy_cell'], tcl['_lists'].get('clear_outside')
    assert CT('op=clear cell=27,71,33 block=poppy kind=bloom')['clear_outside'] == 0 and CT('op=clear cell=36,71,30 block=poppy kind=bloom')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'positive control: beside the tidy cell, 2 past the park'
    # decorator-02 DC4: the fern gather -- a fern near home, never beyond DECOR_FERN_R, a record cell, the park or a tidy cell
    assert CT('op=clear cell=40,71,-40 block=fern kind=fern n=1')['clear_outside'] == 0 and CT('op=clear cell=-48,71,10 block=large_fern kind=fern n=2')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a fern and a large fern near home'
    fo = CT('op=clear cell=49,71,0 block=fern kind=fern n=1')
    assert fo['clear_outside'] == 1 and fo['_lists'].get('clear_outside') == ['board-a-Alpha 49,71,0 fern fern fern_rule'], ('past fernR', fo['_lists'].get('clear_outside'))
    assert CT('op=clear cell=40,71,-40 block=oak_sapling kind=fern n=1')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: no fern'
    assert CT('op=clear cell=33,71,28 block=fern kind=fern n=1')['clear_outside'] == 1 and CT('op=clear cell=28,71,33 block=fern kind=fern n=1')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'the park and a tidy cell'
    assert CJ('op=clear cell=31,71,31 block=fern kind=fern n=1', s_=3_700)['clear_outside'] == 1, 'the decor\'s own border cell once sited'
    assert CJ('op=clear cell=40,71,-40 block=fern kind=fern n=1', s_=-600)['clear_outside'] == 0, 'Claude r1 BLOCKER: a fern cut before the record\'s first generation (home from any generation)'
    assert not decor_fern_ok([{'home': {'y': 70}}], [1, 71, 1], 'fern', 0, {})[0], 'a home without x/z is none'
    # decorator-03 DC5: the hedge's leaves under the fern rule
    assert CT('op=clear cell=40,74,-40 block=spruce_leaves kind=leaves')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a leaf near home'
    lo = CT('op=clear cell=40,74,-40 block=fern kind=leaves')
    assert lo['clear_outside'] == 1 and lo['_lists'].get('clear_outside') == ['board-a-Alpha 40,74,-40 leaves fern leaves_rule'], ('a fern under the leaves rule', lo['_lists'].get('clear_outside'))
    assert CT('op=clear cell=49,74,0 block=spruce_leaves kind=leaves')['clear_outside'] == 1 and CT('op=clear cell=33,74,28 block=spruce_leaves kind=leaves')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'past 48; the park'
    assert CT('op=clear cell=40,74,-40 block=spruce_leaves kind=fern')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'leaves under the fern rule'
    # flowers-01 DC6: the wild flower pick -- a small flower within DECOR_WILD_R of home, never a record cell, the park or a tidy cell
    assert CT('op=clear cell=120,71,-90 block=poppy kind=wild')['clear_outside'] == 0 and CT('op=clear cell=-128,71,5 block=dandelion kind=wild')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'wild flowers in reach'
    wo = CT('op=clear cell=129,71,0 block=poppy kind=wild')
    assert wo['clear_outside'] == 1 and wo['_lists'].get('clear_outside') == ['board-a-Alpha 129,71,0 wild poppy wild_rule'], ('past wildR', wo['_lists'].get('clear_outside'))
    assert CT('op=clear cell=40,71,-40 block=fern kind=wild')['clear_outside'] == 1 and CT('op=clear cell=40,71,-40 block=short_grass kind=wild')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: a fern or grass is no wild flower'
    assert CT('op=clear cell=33,71,28 block=poppy kind=wild')['clear_outside'] == 1 and CT('op=clear cell=28,71,33 block=poppy kind=wild')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'the park and a tidy cell'
    assert CJ('op=clear cell=31,71,31 block=poppy kind=wild', s_=3_700)['clear_outside'] == 1, 'the decor\'s own border flower once sited'
    assert CT('op=clear cell=60,71,0 block=poppy kind=fern n=1')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a flower under the fern rule: still fern_rule'
    assert CJ('op=clear cell=120,71,-90 block=poppy kind=wild', zones={'board-a': [[120, 0, -90, 120, 200, -90]]})['zone'] == 1, 'G3 judges a pick in a protected zone (the villages)'
    for name, mut, scen in (('decor_fern_ok', lambda chain, cell, block, t_ms, guard, park_box=None, tidy_chain=(), names=DECOR_FERNS, tag=' fern_rule', r=DECOR_FERN_R: (block in names, ''), lambda: CT('op=clear cell=49,71,0 block=fern kind=fern n=1')['clear_outside'] == 1),
                            ('decor_fern_ok', lambda chain, cell, block, t_ms, guard, park_box=None, tidy_chain=(), names=DECOR_FERNS, tag=' fern_rule', r=DECOR_FERN_R: (block in names, ''), lambda: CT('op=clear cell=129,71,0 block=poppy kind=wild')['clear_outside'] == 1),
                            ('decor_bloom_ok', lambda chain, cell, block, t_ms, guard, park_box=None, tidy_chain=(): (block in DECOR_BLOOMS, ''), lambda: CJ('op=clear cell=38,71,30 block=poppy kind=bloom')['clear_outside'] == 1),
                            ('decor_meal_ok', lambda chain, cell, t_ms: True, lambda: CJ('op=meal cell=31,70,30 block=bone_meal kind=use')['off_record'] == 1),
                            ('decor_pot_ok', lambda chain, cell, block, t_ms: True, lambda: CJ('op=place seg=D1-P cell=3,71,3 block=poppy kind=pot_fill', s_=3_700)['off_record'] == 1)):
        saved_fn = globals()[name]
        assert scen(), name
        globals()[name] = mut
        try:
            assert not scen(), 'MUTANT SURVIVED: %s' % name
        finally:
            globals()[name] = saved_fn
    # yardread-01 (11-03 TRIP 07:35Z): the clerk's stock on the yard record's pile slots is the yard's own; a planted block is not
    yrec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 5, 'plot': 'yard', 'blueprint': 'yard@1', 'anchor': [-50, 5], 's': 68, 'orient': 'S', 'box': [-64, 5, -50, 22],
            'cells': [{'at': [-52, 68, 7], 'role': 'base', 'seg': 'floor'}, {'at': [-51, 69, 6], 'role': 'light', 'seg': 'L'}], 'clear': [], 'replant': []}
    sl = yard_slots(yrec)
    assert len(sl) == 304, len(sl)
    assert ck([-52, 69, 7]) in sl and ck([-62, 72, 16]) in sl, 'the live items 07:14-07:35Z: first and last'
    YP_ = lambda: {'board-a': {**PR(), 'projects': {'shop': {'record': rec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [rec]}, 'yard': {'record': yrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [yrec]}}}, 'board-b': PR()}
    yp = lambda c, block='cobblestone': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=yard op=place cell=%s block=%s' % (c, block), s=14)], YP_())['off_record']
    assert yp('-52,69,7') == 0 and yp('-62,72,16') == 0, 'stock on pile slots'
    assert yp('-51,69,6', 'torch') == 0 and yp('-52,68,7') == 0, 'record cells as before'
    assert yp('-40,69,7') == 1 and yp('-52,75,7') == 1, 'positive control: a planted cobblestone off every slot trips'
    assert yp('-52,69,7', 'dirt') == 1, 'a non-bulk block on a slot trips'
    yc = lambda c: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=yard op=clear cell=%s block=cobblestone kind=tidy' % c, s=14)], YP_())['clear_outside']
    assert yc('-62,72,16') == 0 and yc('-52,70,7') == 0, 'a refill dug off a pile; an aisle cell above a base floor cell'
    assert yc('-52,68,7') == 1 and yc('-30,70,30') == 1, 'positive control: a record cell, and a dig off the yard'
    assert tc('17,64,-2', kind='foreign')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a foreign block dug out of a fill cell is its own'
    assert tc('17,64,-2', kind='cover')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'coverread-01: a tidy cover dug out of a fill cell'
    assert tc('-30,71,-30')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'listed, and no project holds it'
    m2 = dict(mrec, cells=mrec['cells'] + [{'at': [-30, 71, -30], 'role': 'core', 'seg': 'L01-N'}], at=T0MS - 120_000)
    assert tc('-30,71,-30', pp=TP_(mon=m2))['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a listed cut ON another project\'s record cell trips'
    lg = J(base + [R('_build', 'project=tidy op=clear seg=T-00-0 cell=-30,71,-30 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=0/0', s=14)], TP_(mon=m2))
    assert lg['_lists']['clear_outside'] == ['board-a-Alpha -30,71,-30 tree oak_log guarded'], ('Codex r1 BLOCKER: a guarded log is named guarded, never debris', lg['_lists']['clear_outside'])
    assert tc('3,74,-10')['_lists']['clear_outside'] == ['board-a-Alpha 3,74,-10 tidy dirt'], 'an off-list cut keeps the old words (ACK matches by text)'
    assert tc('-30,71,-30', pp=TP_(mon=dict(m2, at=T0MS + 3_600_000)))['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a project sited on the dug cell later does not re-judge it'
    cl1 = dict(tg1, cut=tg1['cut'] + [{'at': [40, 71, 40], 'kind': 'tidy', 'name': 'cobblestone'}])
    f1_ = [(1, json.dumps(cl1).encode())]
    assert tc('40,71,40', pp=TP_(chain_=record_chain(f1_)), block='cobblestone')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a listed cut of a marker block trips'
    assert tc('40,71,40', pp=TP_(chain_=record_chain(f1_), markers={}), block='cobblestone')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'the control: no marker there'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=tidy op=clear seg=T-00-0 cell=3,73,-10 block=dirt kind=tidy', s=14)], TP_(), zones={'board-a': [[3, 0, -10, 3, 200, -10]]})['zone'] == 1, 'a listed cut in a protected zone is H3'
    assert tc('3,73,-10', pp=TP_(chain_=[]))['clear_outside'] == 0 and tc('3,73,-10', pp=TP_(chain_=[]))['oracle_missing'] == 1, 'no tidy record: named oracle_missing, as before'
    assert tidy('-30,71,-30') == 0, 'the ziggurat\'s own zigtidy rule is unchanged'
    # gkhelpers-01: THE PARK GATHER (kind=gather) -- a plant off every reservation and outside the park; anything else trips
    lrec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'land', 'park': {'box': [60, 60, 70, 70]}}
    GP_ = lambda: dict(TP_(mon=m2), **{'board-a': dict(TP_(mon=m2)['board-a'], projects=dict(TP_(mon=m2)['board-a']['projects'], land={'record': lrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [lrec]}))})
    gt = lambda c, block='poppy': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=tidy op=clear cell=%s block=%s kind=gather n=1' % (c, block), s=14)], GP_())
    assert gt('80,71,80')['clear_outside'] == 0 and gt('80,72,80', 'sugar_cane')['clear_outside'] == 0 and gt('81,72,80', 'spruce_leaves')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'plants off every list: the gather\'s'
    assert gt('80,71,80', 'stone')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: a stone gathered trips'
    assert gt('80,71,80', 'sweet_berry_bush')['clear_outside'] == 1 and gt('80,71,80', 'oak_log')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'never berries, never a log'
    g2 = gt('-30,71,-30')
    assert g2['clear_outside'] == 1 and g2['_lists']['clear_outside'] == ['board-a-Alpha -30,71,-30 gather poppy guarded'], g2['_lists']['clear_outside']
    assert gt('65,71,65')['clear_outside'] == 1 and gt('71,71,59')['clear_outside'] == 1 and gt('72,71,65')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'the park and its edge, never; past the edge, fine'
    assert gt('3,73,-10', 'dirt')['clear_outside'] == 1 and gt('17,64,-2')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a dirt cut is no plant; a tidy fill cell is never a gather'
    assert gt('31,74,0', 'oak_leaves')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'Claude r1 BLOCKER 3: a head-room leaf on the tidy\'s clear list is a plant the gather may take'
    assert gather_ok([], [1, 2, 3], 'poppy', None, {}, None) == (True, '')
    # cropfarm-01 G9: THE CROP FARM's harvest (a crop on a crop cell) and its seed gather (a grass plant near the farm, off every
    # record and the park); anything else trips as clear_outside; a harvest in a protected zone is G3's
    cf = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'cropfarm', 'at': T0MS - 60_000, 'box': [100, 100, 108, 108], 'clear': [{'at': [101, 71, 101], 'kind': 'plant', 'name': 'short_grass'}],
          'cells': [{'at': [101, 70, 101], 'role': 'till', 'seg': 'R-1'}, {'at': [101, 71, 101], 'role': 'wheat', 'seg': 'R-1'}]}
    CF_ = lambda: dict(GP_(), **{'board-a': dict(GP_()['board-a'], projects=dict(GP_()['board-a']['projects'], cropfarm={'record': cf, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [cf]}))})
    hv = lambda c, block='wheat': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=cropfarm op=harvest cell=%s block=%s kind=crop n=7' % (c, block), s=14)], CF_())
    assert hv('101,71,101')['clear_outside'] == 0 and hv('101,71,101')['harvests'] == 1, 'a wheat on a crop cell: the harvest'
    assert hv('101,70,101', 'farmland')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: the till cell dug trips'
    assert hv('101,70,101', 'wheat')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a crop name on the TILL cell is no harvest (the role is judged)'
    assert hv('101,71,101', 'cobblestone')['clear_outside'] == 1 and hv('102,71,101')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'not a crop; not a crop cell'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=cropfarm op=harvest cell=101,71,101 block=wheat kind=crop', s=14)], CF_(), zones={'board-a': [[101, 0, 101, 101, 200, 101]]})['zone'] == 1, 'a harvest in a protected zone is G3'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=treefarm op=harvest cell=1,71,1 block=oak_log kind=grown', s=14)], CF_())['clear_outside'] == 0, 'the tree farm\'s harvest is unchanged (unjudged)'
    sgr = lambda c, block='short_grass': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=cropfarm op=clear cell=%s block=%s kind=seedgrass' % (c, block), s=14)], CF_())
    assert sgr('110,71,110')['clear_outside'] == 0 and sgr('130,71,104', 'tall_grass')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'grass near the farm: the gather\'s'
    assert sgr('110,71,110', 'poppy')['clear_outside'] == 1 and sgr('110,71,110', 'dirt')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: not grass trips'
    assert sgr('133,71,104')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'past SEEDGRASS_R of the farm'
    sg2 = sgr('-30,71,-30')
    assert sg2['clear_outside'] == 1 and sg2['_lists']['clear_outside'] == ['board-a-Alpha -30,71,-30 seedgrass short_grass guarded'], sg2['_lists']['clear_outside']
    assert sgr('101,71,101')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'the farm\'s own crop cell is never the gather\'s (its bed plant is a listed clear)'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=cropfarm op=clear cell=101,71,101 block=short_grass kind=plant', s=14)], CF_())['clear_outside'] == 0, 'the listed bed plant: the record\'s clear list'
    cfp = dict(cf, box=[60, 72, 68, 80])
    CFP_ = lambda: dict(GP_(), **{'board-a': dict(GP_()['board-a'], projects=dict(GP_()['board-a']['projects'], cropfarm={'record': cfp, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [cfp]}))})
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=cropfarm op=clear cell=65,71,71 block=short_grass kind=seedgrass', s=14)], CFP_())['clear_outside'] == 1, 'the park\'s edge, never'
    assert seedgrass_ok([cf], [109, 71, 109], 'fern', None, {}, None) == (True, '') and seedgrass_ok([], [109, 71, 109], 'fern', None, {}, None) == (False, ' far')
    # Codex G9 r1 BLOCKER: a tidy fill or cut cell is never the seed gather's (the bot's guard holds the tidy's plan)
    cft = dict(cf, box=[20, -10, 28, -2])
    CFT_ = lambda: dict(GP_(), **{'board-a': dict(GP_()['board-a'], projects=dict(GP_()['board-a']['projects'], cropfarm={'record': cft, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [cft]}))})
    sgt = lambda c: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=cropfarm op=clear cell=%s block=short_grass kind=seedgrass' % c, s=14)], CFT_())
    assert sgt('30,72,0')['clear_outside'] == 1 and sgt('31,74,0')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'the tidy head\'s cut and clear cells'
    assert sgt('30,72,1')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'the control: grass beside them'
    assert sgt('17,64,-2')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a cell only an OLDER tidy generation listed (not carried): the bot\'s guard reads the head, so does the read'
    # Codex G9 r2 BLOCKER: a head sited 30 s AFTER the dig never hides the head the bot read
    hA = {'at': 1000, 'cells': [{'at': [5, 70, 5]}], 'cut': [], 'clear': []}
    hB = {'at': 31_000, 'cells': [], 'cut': [], 'clear': []}
    assert tidy_heads_at([hA, hB], 1_000) == [hA, hB] and tidy_heads_at([hA, hB], 100_000) == [hB] and tidy_heads_at([hA], 0) == [hA] and tidy_heads_at([hA, hB], 0) == [hA, hB]
    assert seedgrass_ok([cf], [101, 71, 120], 'short_grass', 1_000, {}, None, tidy_heads_at([dict(hA, cells=[{'at': [101, 71, 120]}]), hB], 1_000)) == (False, ' tidy_cell')
    # gkhelpers-01: THE GROUNDS CREW -- one bot on two crew leases, a third helper, two bots on neighbouring segments past the yield
    cg = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'tidy', 'at': T0MS - 60_000, 'cut': [], 'clear': [], 'replant': [],
          'cells': [{'at': [10, 70, 10], 'seg': 'T-00-0'}, {'at': [11, 70, 10], 'seg': 'T-00-0'}, {'at': [13, 70, 10], 'seg': 'T-01-0'}, {'at': [40, 70, 40], 'seg': 'T-02-0'}, {'at': [12, 70, 10], 'seg': 'T-PREV', 'prev': True}]}
    E0 = CREW_EPOCH_MS + 3_600_000   # an hour into the crew's epoch
    LL = lambda holder, at, until: {'holder': holder, 'at': E0 + at, 'until': E0 + until}
    CP_ = lambda leases: {'board-a': {**PR(), 'projects': {'shop': {'record': rec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [rec]}, 'tidy': {'record': cg, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': leases, 'chain': [cg]}}}, 'board-b': PR()}
    cr = lambda leases: J(base, CP_(leases))
    kh = {'keeper': [(1, LL('K', 0, 1_800_000), None)], 'helper1': [(1, LL('H', 1_000, 700_000), None)]}
    assert cr(dict(kh, **{'seg-T-00-0': [(1, LL('K', 2_000, 242_000), E0 + 100_000)], 'seg-T-02-0': [(1, LL('H', 2_000, 242_000), E0 + 100_000)]}))['lease_overlap'] == 0, 'a keeper and a helper far apart: clean'
    near = cr(dict(kh, **{'seg-T-00-0': [(1, LL('K', 2_000, 242_000), E0 + 100_000)], 'seg-T-01-0': [(1, LL('H', 2_000, 242_000), E0 + 100_000)]}))
    assert near['lease_overlap'] == 1 and 'within 2 columns' in near['_lists']['lease_overlap'][0], near['_lists']['lease_overlap']
    assert cr(dict(kh, **{'seg-T-00-0': [(1, LL('K', 2_000, 242_000), E0 + 100_000)], 'seg-T-01-0': [(1, LL('H', 2_000, 242_000), E0 + 2_050)]}))['lease_overlap'] == 0, 'a raced take yielded in 50 ms'
    assert cr(dict(kh, **{'seg-T-00-0': [(1, LL('K', 2_000, 242_000), E0 + 100_000)], 'seg-T-01-0': [(1, LL('H', 200_000, 440_000), None)]}))['lease_overlap'] == 0, 'one after the other: clean'
    assert cr({'keeper': [(1, LL('K', 0, 1_800_000), None)], 'helper1': [(1, LL('K', 1_000, 700_000), None)]})['lease_overlap'] == 1, 'the keeper on a helper lease too'
    assert cr({'keeper': [(1, LL('K', 0, 1_800_000), E0 + 5_000)], 'helper1': [(1, LL('K', 5_000, 700_000), None)]})['lease_overlap'] == 0, 'released first, then taken: clean'
    assert cr({'helper1': [(1, LL('H', 0, 700_000), None)], 'helper2': [(1, LL('H', 1_000, 700_000), None)]})['lease_overlap'] == 1, 'one bot on two helper leases'
    # Claude r1 BLOCKERS 1-2: a renewed generation is superseded by the next one (only the newest is ever marked released)
    ren = {'helper1': [(1, LL('H', 0, 720_000), None), (2, LL('H', 180_000, 900_000), E0 + 300_000)], 'keeper': [(1, LL('H', 300_001, 2_100_000), None)]}
    assert cr(ren)['lease_overlap'] == 0, 'a renewed helper lease released, then the keeper lease: clean'
    assert cr(dict(ren, helper1=[(1, LL('H', 0, 720_000), None), (2, LL('H', 180_000, 900_000), None)]))['lease_overlap'] == 1, 'control: the newest never released -> two at once'
    rs = dict(kh, **{'seg-T-00-0': [(1, LL('K', 0, 240_000), None), (2, LL('K', 130_000, 370_000), E0 + 150_000)], 'seg-T-01-0': [(1, LL('H', 160_000, 400_000), E0 + 300_000)]})
    assert cr(rs)['lease_overlap'] == 0, 'a renewed segment released before the neighbour was taken: clean'
    assert cr(dict(rs, **{'seg-T-00-0': [(1, LL('K', 0, 240_000), None), (2, LL('K', 130_000, 370_000), None)]}))['lease_overlap'] == 1, 'control: still held'
    # Codex r1 BLOCKER 2: read mid-race (20 ms after both takes, before the yield) -- held only up to now: no 240 s of future overlap
    race = {'seg-T-00-0': [(1, LL('K', 1_000, 241_000), None)], 'seg-T-01-0': [(1, LL('H', 1_000, 241_000), None)]}
    assert crew_overlaps(race, [cg], now_ms=E0 + 1_020) == [], 'mid-race: clean'
    assert len(crew_overlaps(race, [cg], now_ms=E0 + 30_000)) == 1, 'control: both still held 29 s on: a real overlap'
    assert cr({'helper3': [(1, LL('X', 0, 700_000), None)]})['lease_overlap'] == 1, 'a third helper'
    old = {'seg-T-00-0': [(1, {'holder': 'K', 'at': CREW_EPOCH_MS - 600_000, 'until': CREW_EPOCH_MS - 300_000}, None)], 'seg-T-01-0': [(1, {'holder': 'H', 'at': CREW_EPOCH_MS - 600_000, 'until': CREW_EPOCH_MS - 300_000}, None)]}
    assert crew_overlaps(old, [cg], since_ms=CREW_EPOCH_MS) == [] and len(crew_overlaps(old, [cg])) == 1, 'leases before the crew epoch are not judged (control: they would be)'
    assert cr(dict(old, keeper=kh['keeper'], helper1=kh['helper1']))['lease_overlap'] == 0, 'through judge: the epoch holds'
    assert LEASE_RE.match('project-0_70_0.tidy.keeper.lease.g63.json').group(6) == 'keeper' and LEASE_RE.match('project-0_70_0.tidy.helper2.lease.g1.json').group(6) == 'helper2'
    import tempfile
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        with open(os.path.join(td, 'expedition-0_70_0.markers.jsonl'), 'w') as fh:
            fh.write('{"at":[12,71,52],"t":5}\nnot json\n{"at":[12,71,52],"gone":true}\n{"at":[1,2]}\n')
        assert read_markers(td) == {'12,71,52': 5, '12,72,52': 5, '12,73,52': 5}, read_markers(td)
        # read_projects carries the chain and the markers (the wiring the live read uses), and the stage-0 cut reads clean through it
        pd = os.path.join(td, '_pool-board-a'); os.mkdir(pd)
        for g, b in files:
            open(os.path.join(pd, 'project-0_70_0.tidy.g%d.json' % g), 'wb').write(b)
        os.replace(os.path.join(td, 'expedition-0_70_0.markers.jsonl'), os.path.join(pd, 'expedition-0_70_0.markers.jsonl'))
        rp_ = read_projects(td)
        assert [c['_gen'] for c in rp_['board-a']['projects']['tidy']['chain']] == [1, 2] and '12,72,52' in rp_['board-a']['markers'], rp_['board-a'].get('markers')
        assert tc('3,73,-10', pp=rp_)['clear_outside'] == 0 and tc('3,74,-10', pp=rp_)['clear_outside'] == 1, 'through read_projects'
        assert tc('12,72,52', pp={'board-a': dict(rp_['board-a'], projects=dict(rp_['board-a']['projects'], tidy=dict(rp_['board-a']['projects']['tidy'], chain=[dict(tg1, cut=[{'at': [12, 72, 52]}])])))})['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a marker read from disk guards'
        # bridgepost-01: a bridge site's planned blocks guard as a marker's do (sighted, built or not), read from the bridge index
        row = lambda i, x: {'i': i, 'centre': [x, 69, 0], 'sides': [{'side': -1, 'deck': [x, 69, -1], 'rail': [x, 70, -1]}, {'side': 1, 'deck': [x, 69, 1], 'rail': [x, 70, 1]}]}
        span = {'near': [19, 70, 0], 'axis': [1, 0], 'rows': [row(0, 20), row(1, 21)]}
        # the bot's own plan for this span (bridge.mjs bridgePlan): 5 a row + ONE torch, above the side +1 rail of row 0 only
        assert sorted(ck(c) for c in bridge_cells(span)) == sorted(['20,69,0', '20,69,-1', '20,70,-1', '20,69,1', '20,70,1', '20,71,1',
                                                                    '21,69,0', '21,69,-1', '21,70,-1', '21,69,1', '21,70,1']), bridge_cells(span)
        assert bridge_cells({'rows': [{'centre': [1, 2]}, 'x']}) == [] and bridge_cells(None) == [], 'a malformed span gives nothing'
        assert [bridge_cells(x) for x in (True, {'rows': 1}, {'rows': [{'sides': 1}]}, {'rows': [{'sides': [1, None]}]})] == [[], [], [], []], 'nor raises (Codex r1)'
        with open(os.path.join(pd, 'expedition-0_70_0.bridges.jsonl'), 'w') as fh:
            # a second sighting 3 off on the same axis is the same site (its own span is never planned); another axis is a new site
            moved = dict(span, near=[19, 70, 3], rows=[row(0, 30)])
            other = dict(span, axis=[0, 1], near=[50, 70, 0], rows=[row(0, 50)])
            fh.write(json.dumps({'op': 'sight', 'id': 's', 'span': span, 't': 7}) + '\nnot json\n' + json.dumps({'op': 'sight', 'span': moved, 't': 8}) + '\n' +
                     json.dumps({'op': 'state', 'id': 's', 'complete': True, 't': 9}) + '\n' + json.dumps({'op': 'sight', 'span': other, 't': 11}) + '\n')
        rbd = read_bridges(pd)
        assert rbd['20,70,1'] == 7 and '30,69,0' not in rbd and rbd['50,69,0'] == 11 and len(rbd) == 11 + 6, rbd
        assert merge_guards({'20,70,1': 5}, {'20,70,1': 100, '9,9,9': 3}) == {'20,70,1': 5, '9,9,9': 3}, 'the earliest time wins'
        rb_ = read_projects(td)
        assert '20,69,0' in rb_['board-a']['markers'] and '12,72,52' in rb_['board-a']['markers'], 'the bridge cells join the markers'
        cut_b = lambda c: tc(c, pp={'board-a': dict(rb_['board-a'], projects=dict(rb_['board-a']['projects'], tidy=dict(rb_['board-a']['projects']['tidy'], chain=[dict(tg1, cut=[{'at': [int(v) for v in c.split(',')]}])])))})['clear_outside']
        assert cut_b('20,70,1') == 1, 'a listed cut of a bridge rail trips'
        assert cut_b('22,70,1') == 0, 'the control: a cell past the bridge does not'
        assert cut_b('20,71,-1') == 0 and cut_b('21,71,1') == 0, 'the control: above a rail with no torch planned is not guarded (the bot digs it)'
    # pathsclose-01: a block set on a path cell, dug (kind=overburden) -- the same column, 1..3 above a PATH cell, never a record cell
    prec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'cells': [{'at': [5, 69, 5], 'role': 'path', 'seg': 'P-d-0'}, {'at': [6, 68, 5], 'role': 'fill', 'seg': 'P-d-0'},
                                                      {'at': [6, 69, 5], 'role': 'path', 'seg': 'P-d-0'}, {'at': [6, 71, 5], 'role': 'light', 'seg': 'P-d-0'}], 'clear': [], 'replant': []}
    pb = PP(paths={'record': prec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}})
    ob = lambda c: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=paths op=clear seg=P-d-0 cell=%s block=dirt kind=overburden' % c, s=14)], pb)['clear_outside']
    assert ob('5,70,5') == 0 and ob('5,72,5') == 0, 'one and three above a path cell: overburden'
    assert ob('5,73,5') == 1 and ob('5,69,5') == 1 and ob('6,69,5') == 1 and ob('7,70,5') == 1, 'four above, the path cell itself, a record cell, another column: outside'
    assert ob('6,70,5') == 0, 'above the path cell over a fill'
    assert ob('6,71,5') == 1, 'a record cell above a path cell is never overburden'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=paths op=clear seg=P-d-0 cell=5,70,5 block=dirt kind=cut', s=14)], pb)['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: the same cell as a cut off the list'
    # wallstuck-01: the wall's footing (1..3 below a column's lowest record cell, never a record cell) and the cap over a sealed column
    wrec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'cells': [{'at': [5, 68, 5], 'role': 'wall', 'seg': 'W-03'}, {'at': [5, 69, 5], 'role': 'wall', 'seg': 'W-03'},
                                                      {'at': [6, 69, 5], 'role': 'step', 'seg': 'W-03'}], 'clear': [], 'replant': []}
    wb = PP(wall={'record': wrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}})
    ft = lambda c, kind='footing', proj='wall', pp=None: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=%s op=place seg=W-03 cell=%s block=cobblestone kind=%s' % (proj, c, kind), s=14)], pp or wb)['off_record']
    assert ft('5,67,5') == 0 and ft('5,65,5') == 0 and ft('6,68,5') == 0, 'one and three below the lowest record cell: footing'
    assert ft('5,64,5') == 1 and ft('5,70,5') == 1 and ft('7,67,5') == 1, 'four below, above the column, another column: off the record'
    assert ft('5,67,5', kind='access') == 1, 'positive control: the same cell placed as anything but a footing'
    assert ft('5,67,5', proj='paths', pp=pb) == 1, 'a footing is the wall\'s only'
    wob = lambda c: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=wall op=clear seg=W-03 cell=%s block=granite kind=overburden' % c, s=14)], wb)['clear_outside']
    assert wob('6,70,5') == 0 and wob('5,72,5') == 0, 'the cap over a step, three over a wall cell: overburden'
    assert wob('5,73,5') == 1 and wob('6,69,5') == 1 and wob('7,70,5') == 1, 'four above, a record cell, another column: outside'
    # wallext-01 WX1: the extension judged as the wall (footing, cap); the OLD wall's cells are never the extension's (place or dig)
    xrec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'blueprint': 'wallext@1', 'plot': 'wallext', 'cells': [{'at': [5, 68, -25], 'role': 'wall', 'seg': 'W-00'}, {'at': [5, 69, -25], 'role': 'wall', 'seg': 'W-00'},
                                                      {'at': [6, 69, -25], 'role': 'step', 'seg': 'W-00'}], 'clear': [{'at': [5, 72, -25], 'kind': 'leaves', 'name': 'oak_leaves'}], 'replant': []}
    xb = PP(wall={'record': wrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}}, wallext={'record': xrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}})
    xp = lambda c, kind='footing': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=wallext op=place seg=W-00 cell=%s block=cobblestone kind=%s' % (c, kind), s=14)], xb)['off_record']
    assert xp('5,68,-25', kind='wall') == 0 and xp('5,67,-25') == 0 and xp('6,66,-25') == 0, 'its own cell, its footings: on the record'
    assert xp('5,64,-25') == 1 and xp('5,67,-25', kind='access') == 1, 'four below; positive control: the same cell as no footing'
    assert xp('5,68,5', kind='wall') == 1 and xp('5,67,5') == 1, 'an OLD wall cell (and a footing under one) is never the extension\'s'
    xc = lambda c, kind: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=wallext op=clear seg=W-00 cell=%s block=cobblestone kind=%s' % (c, kind), s=14)], xb)['clear_outside']
    assert xc('5,72,-25', 'leaves') == 0 and xc('5,69,-25', 'foreign') == 0 and xc('6,70,-25', 'overburden') == 0, 'its clear list, its own cell, the cap over a step'
    assert xc('5,69,5', 'foreign') == 1 and xc('5,68,5', 'leaves') == 1 and xc('6,70,5', 'overburden') == 1, 'a dig of the OLD wall (any kind) is outside its record'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=wall op=clear seg=W-03 cell=5,69,5 block=cobblestone kind=foreign', s=14)], xb)['clear_outside'] == 0, 'positive control: the old wall\'s own foreign dig'
    # wallext-02 WD1: the old line's demolition digs only listed old wall cells of a built line column strictly inside the extension's
    # span, 0..3 in (the extension's own line and side), never a foundation, after the extension stood
    T0MS_ = int(t0.timestamp() * 1000)
    W_ = lambda x, y, z, role='wall': {'at': [x, y, z], 'role': role, 'seg': 'W-03'}
    dwall = {'v': 1, 'gen': 2, 'prev': 1, 'cols': [{'x': 5, 'z': 5, 's': 'open', 'why': 'retired'}, {'x': 6, 'z': 5, 's': 'open'}, {'x': 0, 'z': 5, 's': 'wall'}, {'x': 7, 'z': 5, 's': 'wall'}],
             'cells': [W_(5, 68, 5), W_(6, 69, 5), W_(5, 69, 9, 'step'), W_(5, 71, 6, 'step'), W_(5, 67, 5, 'base'), W_(0, 69, 5), W_(7, 69, 4)],
             'retired': [W_(5, 69, 5), W_(5, 70, 6, 'step')], 'retiredAt': T0MS_ - 5000, 'clear': [], 'replant': []}
    L_ = lambda *xyz: {'at': list(xyz), 'kind': 'demolish', 'name': 'cobblestone'}
    drec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'blueprint': 'walldemo@1', 'plot': 'walldemo', 'cells': [], 'replant': [], 'cols': [[5, 5]],
            'clear': [L_(5, 69, 5), L_(5, 68, 5), L_(6, 69, 5), L_(5, 69, 9), L_(5, 70, 5), L_(5, 70, 6), L_(5, 67, 5), L_(0, 69, 5), L_(7, 69, 4)]}
    xdone = dict(xrec, side='N', join={'axis': 'z', 'line': 5, 'from': 0, 'to': 10}, structure_complete=True, completeAt=T0MS_ - 1000)
    db = lambda ext=xdone: PP(wall={'record': dwall, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}}, wallext={'record': ext, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}}, walldemo={'record': drec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}})
    dc = lambda c, kind='demolish', pp=None: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=walldemo op=clear seg=D-00 cell=%s block=cobblestone kind=%s' % (c, kind), s=14)], pp or db())['clear_outside']
    assert dc('5,69,5') == 0 and dc('5,68,5') == 0 and dc('5,70,6') == 0, 'a retired / still-listed old wall cell of a built line column, 0..1 in, after the extension stood'
    assert dc('5,69,5', kind='leaves') == 1, 'only kind=demolish'
    assert dc('6,69,5') == 1, 'an old wall cell of a line column that never stood as wall'
    assert dc('5,69,9') == 1, 'four in from the line'
    assert dc('5,70,5') == 1, 'listed, but no old wall cell'
    assert dc('5,71,6') == 1, 'an old wall cell of its line column 1 in, but not listed'
    assert dc('5,67,5') == 1, 'a foundation (base) is ground, never demolished'
    assert dc('0,69,5') == 1, 'the span\'s end (a corner where the extension meets the old line)'
    assert dc('7,69,4') == 1, 'the wrong side of the line (outward)'
    assert dc('5,70,6', pp=db(dict(xdone, side='S'))) == 1, 'positive control: the same cell with the line\'s inward flipped'
    assert dc('5,69,5', pp=db(dict(xrec, structure_complete=True, completeAt=T0MS_ + 600_000, side='N', join=xdone['join']))) == 1, 'before the extension stood'
    assert dc('5,69,5', pp=db(dict(xrec, side='N', join=xdone['join']))) == 1, 'the extension never complete'
    assert dc('5,69,5', pp=db(dict(xdone, structure_complete=False))) == 1, 'a completeAt on a generation that is not complete'
    assert dc('5,69,5', pp=db(dict(xdone, join=None))) == 1, 'an extension with no join'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=walldemo op=place seg=D-00 cell=5,69,5 block=cobblestone', s=14)], db())['off_record'] == 1, 'the demolition places nothing'
    # wallretired-01: the old wall's own rows on a cell a later generation retired (retiredAt T0-5 s) -- on the record before the
    # retirement, off it after; a cell neither listed nor retired stays off either way; never a NEW defect (the lenient verdict)
    wj = lambda d, s_: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=wall %s' % d, s=s_)], db())
    wp = lambda c, s_: wj('op=place seg=W-03 cell=%s block=cobblestone' % c, s_)['off_record']
    wf = lambda c, s_, kind='foreign': wj('op=clear seg=W-03 cell=%s block=granite kind=%s' % (c, kind), s_)['clear_outside']
    assert wp('5,69,5', -10) == 0 and wp('5,70,6', -10) == 0, 'a retired cell placed before the retirement is the old wall\'s'
    assert wf('5,69,5', -10) == 0, 'a foreign block dug from a retired cell before the retirement'
    assert wp('5,69,5', 14) == 1 and wf('5,69,5', 14) == 1, 'positive control: the same retired cell after the retirement'
    assert wp('9,69,9', -10) == 1 and wf('9,69,9', -10) == 1, 'positive control: a cell neither listed nor retired'
    assert wp('5,68,5', -10) == 0 and wp('5,68,5', 14) == 0, 'a listed cell (control), either side'
    assert wf('5,69,5', -10, 'overburden') == wf('5,69,5', 14, 'overburden') == 0, 'the overburden over a listed cell stays clean before the retirement too (Claude r1 BLOCKER)'
    fwall = dict(dwall, cells=[W_(8, 73, 8)], retired=[W_(8, 69, 8)])
    fp = J(base + [R('_build', 'project=wall op=place seg=W-03 cell=8,71,8 block=cobblestone kind=footing', s=-10)], PP(wall={'record': fwall, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}}))['off_record']
    assert fp == 0, 'a footing 2 under the head\'s lowest cell stays clean though a retired cell lies below it (the lenient verdict)'
    rr = J(base + [R('_build', 'project=wall op=retired cell=0,70,0 kind=cells114/cols15 gen=5', s=14), R('_build', 'project=walldemo op=sited cell=0,70,0 kind=clear114/cols15/skipped0 gen=1', s=15)], db())
    assert rr['off_record'] == 0 and rr['clear_outside'] == 0 and rr['zone'] == 0, ('the retirement and the siting rows judge nothing', rr)
    # the tidy guard: a retired cell is reserved until the DEMOLITION completes, then it is the groundskeeper's ground (Claude WD1 r1
    # BLOCKER 3; the hold: Codex batch-5 fold BLOCKER -- never from the retirement itself)
    dwall1 = dict(dwall, gen=1, prev=None, cells=dwall['cells'] + dwall['retired'], retired=[], at=T0MS_ - 60_000)
    dwall1.pop('retiredAt')
    wch = {'record': dwall, 'chain': [dwall1, dict(dwall, at=T0MS_ - 5000)]}
    ddone = dict(drec, gen=2, structure_complete=True, completeAt=T0MS_ - 2000)
    tg = tidy_guard({'projects': {'wall': wch, 'walldemo': {'record': ddone, 'chain': [drec, ddone]}}})
    assert tidy_guarded([5, 70, 6], T0MS_, tg) is False and tidy_guarded([5, 70, 6], T0MS_ - 3000, tg) is True, 'demolished at T0-2 s (retired at T0-5 s: still held between)'
    assert tidy_guarded([5, 71, 6], T0MS_, tg) is True, 'positive control: a cell still on the wall record stays reserved'
    tg2 = tidy_guard({'projects': {'wall': wch, 'walldemo': {'record': drec, 'chain': [drec]}}})
    assert tidy_guarded([5, 70, 6], T0MS_ + 3_600_000, tg2) is True, 'the demolition not complete: held, whatever the time'
    tg3 = tidy_guard({'projects': {'wall': wch}})
    assert tidy_guarded([5, 70, 6], T0MS_ + 3_600_000, tg3) is True, 'no demolition record: held'
    tg4 = tidy_guard({'projects': {'wall': wch, 'walldemo': {'record': dict(drec, completeAt=T0MS_ - 2000), 'chain': [dict(drec, completeAt=T0MS_ - 2000)]}}})
    assert tidy_guarded([5, 70, 6], T0MS_, tg4) is True, 'a completeAt on a demolition that is not complete: held'
    # stepwell-01 SW1: the stepwell's stone on its cells, its cut on its clear list, a seal also in a cut cell -- nothing else
    srec1 = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'blueprint': 'stepwell@1', 'plot': 'stepwell', 'cells': [{'at': [60, 66, 60], 'role': 'floor', 't': 1}, {'at': [61, 67, 60], 'role': 'post', 't': 0}],
             'clear': [{'at': [60, 67, 60], 'kind': 'cut', 't': 1}, {'at': [62, 75, 60], 'kind': 'tree', 'name': 'spruce_log', 'tree': 0}], 'replant': []}
    srec2 = dict(srec1, gen=2, prev=1, tiers_built=[1])
    sb = PP(stepwell={'record': srec2, 'records_files': 2, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [srec1, srec2]})
    sp = lambda c, kind, block='cobblestone': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=stepwell op=place cell=%s block=%s kind=%s' % (c, block, kind), s=14)], sb)['off_record']
    sc = lambda c, kind, block='dirt': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=stepwell op=clear cell=%s block=%s kind=%s' % (c, block, kind), s=14)], sb)['clear_outside']
    assert sp('60,66,60', 'floor', 'stone_bricks') == 0 and sp('61,67,60', 'post', 'cobblestone_wall') == 0, 'stone on its record cells'
    assert sp('60,67,60', 'seal') == 0, 'a seal in a cut cell (the liquid met in the volume)'
    assert sp('60,67,60', 'floor') == 1, 'no other placement in a cut cell'
    assert sp('70,67,60', 'seal') == 1 and sp('70,67,60', 'floor') == 1, 'off the record either way'
    assert sp('60,66,60', 'seal') == 0, 'a seal in a record cell (a wet face)'
    assert sp('62,75,60', 'seal') == 1, 'a felling clear is no cut cell: no seal there'
    assert sc('60,67,60', 'cut') == 0 and sc('62,75,60', 'tree', 'spruce_log') == 0, 'its cut and its felling on the clear list'
    assert sc('60,66,60', 'foreign') == 0, 'a bad block dug from its own record cell'
    assert sc('60,66,60', 'cover') == 0 and sc('70,66,60', 'cover') == 1, 'coverread-01: the stepwell\'s cover in its own cell; off it, a trip'
    assert sc('70,67,60', 'cut') == 1 and sc('70,66,60', 'foreign') == 1, 'a dig off the record either way'
    assert sc('60,66,60', 'cut') == 1, 'a record cell is never cut'
    # stepwell-02: the descent's generation moves a wall cell (the portal) from cells to clear -- an earlier foreign dig of it and a later
    # cut of it are both on the record; a cut of a cell no generation lists still trips
    srec3 = dict(srec2, gen=3, prev=2, cells=[c for c in srec2['cells'] if c['at'] != [61, 67, 60]] + [{'at': [61, 60, 60], 'role': 'tread', 't': 3, 'd': 1}],
                 clear=srec2['clear'] + [{'at': [61, 67, 60], 'kind': 'cut', 't': 3, 'd': 1}])
    sb3 = PP(stepwell={'record': srec3, 'records_files': 3, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [srec1, srec2, srec3]})
    sc3 = lambda c, kind, block='cobblestone': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=stepwell op=clear cell=%s block=%s kind=%s' % (c, block, kind), s=14)], sb3)['clear_outside']
    assert sc3('61,67,60', 'foreign') == 0, 'a foreign dig of a cell an earlier generation listed'
    assert sc3('61,67,60', 'cut') == 0, 'the portal cut (the head lists it)'
    assert sc3('61,60,60', 'foreign') == 0 and sc3('61,60,60', 'cut') == 1, 'the new tread: foreign yes, cut no'
    assert sc3('71,67,60', 'cut') == 1 and sc3('71,67,60', 'foreign') == 1, 'off every generation either way'
    # canefarm-01 CF1: the cane bed's harvest is the second block of a cane cell, sugar cane only; its planting a record cell
    crec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'blueprint': 'canefarm@1', 'plot': 'canefarm', 'cells': [{'at': [40, 66, 40], 'role': 'cane', 'seg': 'C'}, {'at': [42, 66, 40], 'role': 'floor', 'seg': 'C'}], 'clear': [], 'replant': []}
    cb = PP(canefarm={'record': crec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}})
    ch = lambda c, block='sugar_cane', proj='canefarm': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=%s op=harvest cell=%s block=%s kind=cane' % (proj, c, block), s=14)], cb)['clear_outside']
    assert ch('40,67,40') == 0, 'the second block of a cane cell'
    assert ch('40,66,40') == 1, 'the bottom block is never the harvest\'s'
    assert ch('40,68,40') == 1 and ch('41,67,40') == 1, 'the third block, another column'
    assert ch('40,67,40', block='dirt') == 1, 'not sugar cane'
    assert ch('42,67,40') == 1, 'above a record cell that is not a cane cell'
    m0 = J(base, cb)['mutations']
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=canefarm op=harvest cell=40,67,40 block=sugar_cane kind=cane', s=14)], cb)['mutations'] == m0 + 1, 'a cane harvest is a mutation (G3)'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=canefarm op=place seg=C cell=40,66,40 block=sugar_cane', s=14)], cb)['off_record'] == 0, 'the planting is a record cell'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=canefarm op=place seg=C cell=40,67,40 block=sugar_cane', s=14)], cb)['off_record'] == 1, 'a planting off the record'
    # townstyle-01 RO1: a reorient dig of a state-bearing cell's own oriented block; anything else is clear_outside
    cpal = {'roof': ['dark_oak_stairs'], 'ridge': ['dark_oak_slab'], 'post': ['spruce_log'], 'sill': ['spruce_trapdoor'], 'lamp': ['$light'], 'light': ['lantern'],
            'wall': ['spruce_planks', '$planks']}
    crec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'blueprint': 'cottage@1s', 'plot': 'cottage1', 'clear': [], 'replant': [], 'palette': cpal,
            'cells': [{'at': [50, 71, 50], 'role': 'roof', 'seg': 'roof-edge', 'ws': {'facing': 'north', 'half': 'bottom'}}, {'at': [51, 71, 50], 'role': 'wall', 'seg': 'wall-F'},
                      {'at': [54, 71, 50], 'role': 'ridge', 'seg': 'roof-inner', 'ws': {'type': 'bottom'}}, {'at': [55, 71, 50], 'role': 'sill', 'seg': 'fit-out', 'ws': {'facing': 'north', 'half': 'top', 'open': False}},
                      {'at': [56, 71, 50], 'role': 'lamp', 'seg': 'fit-out', 'ws': {'hanging': False}},
                      {'at': [57, 71, 50], 'role': 'beam', 'seg': 'gable'}, {'at': [58, 71, 50], 'role': 'wall', 'seg': 'wall-F', 'ws': {'axis': 'y'}}]}
    crec2 = dict(crec, gen=2, prev=1, cells=[{'at': [52, 71, 50], 'role': 'post', 'seg': 'wall-F', 'ws': {'axis': 'y'}}])
    cbk = PP(cottage1={'record': crec2, 'records_files': 2, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [crec, crec2]})
    ro = lambda c, block, kind='reorient', pp=cbk: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=cottage1 op=clear seg=roof-edge cell=%s block=%s kind=%s' % (c, block, kind), s=14)], pp)
    assert ro('50,71,50', 'dark_oak_stairs')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a stair on its state cell (an earlier generation of the chain)'
    assert ro('52,71,50', 'spruce_log')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a log on its post cell (the head generation)'
    assert ro('54,71,50', 'minecraft:dark_oak_slab')['clear_outside'] == 0 and ro('55,71,50', 'spruce_trapdoor')['clear_outside'] == 0 and ro('56,71,50', 'lantern')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'each its own role\'s block ($role expanded)'
    assert ro('52,71,50', 'oak_trapdoor')['clear_outside'] == 1 and ro('52,71,50', 'dark_oak_stairs')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'Codex r1 BLOCKER: a post cell authorizes no other family'
    assert ro('50,71,50', 'spruce_stairs')['clear_outside'] == 1 and ro('52,71,50', 'oak_log')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'Codex r1 BLOCKER: nor another species'
    nopal = PP(cottage1={'record': dict(crec2, palette=None), 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [dict(crec2, palette=None)]})
    assert ro('52,71,50', 'spruce_log', pp=nopal)['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a generation with no palette authorizes nothing'
    assert ro('51,71,50', 'spruce_stairs')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a stateless cell is never reoriented'
    assert ro('57,71,50', 'spruce_log', pp=PP(cottage1={'record': dict(crec, palette=dict(cpal, beam=['spruce_log'])), 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [dict(crec, palette=dict(cpal, beam=['spruce_log']))]}))['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a stateless cell whose own block is a log: still never reoriented'
    assert ro('58,71,50', 'spruce_planks')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'never a block a click does not orient, even on a state cell whose role names it'
    assert ro('50,71,50', 'chest')['clear_outside'] == 1 and ro('50,71,50', 'cobblestone')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'not an oriented block'
    assert ro('53,71,50', 'spruce_stairs')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'off the record'
    assert ro('52,71,50', 'cobblestone', kind='foreign')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'the control: a foreign dig of a head-record cell is its own (G2 as before)'
    assert ro('50,71,50', 'dark_oak_stairs')['_lists'].get('clear_outside', []) == [] and ro('51,71,50', 'spruce_stairs')['_lists']['clear_outside'] == ['board-a-Alpha 51,71,50 reorient spruce_stairs reorient'], ro('51,71,50', 'spruce_stairs')['_lists']['clear_outside']
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=cottage1 op=place seg=roof-edge cell=50,71,50 block=dark_oak_stairs st=north/bottom', s=14)], cbk)['off_record'] == 1, 'positive control: a place is judged on the HEAD record (G1 as before): gen 1\'s cell is off it'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=cottage1 op=place seg=wall-F cell=52,71,50 block=spruce_log st=y', s=14)], cbk)['off_record'] == 0
    # townstyle-03 RT1: a retrofit generation (old cells kept + the new roof) judged by the existing rules
    hold = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'blueprint': 'house@1', 'plot': 'house1', 'clear': [], 'replant': [], 'structure_complete': True,
            'cells': [{'at': [60, 73, 60], 'role': 'roof', 'seg': 'roof-inner'}, {'at': [60, 72, 60], 'role': 'wall', 'seg': 'wall-F'}]}
    hnew = dict(hold, gen=2, prev=1, structure_complete=False, style={'wood': 'spruce', 'roof': 'spruce'}, restyle={'id': 'house@1r'},
                palette={'roof': ['$planks'], 'wall': ['$planks'], 'roofstair': ['spruce_stairs'], 'ridge': ['spruce_slab']},
                cells=hold['cells'] + [{'at': [60, 74, 60], 'role': 'roofstair', 'seg': 'reroof-roof-edge', 'ws': {'facing': 'south', 'half': 'bottom'}},
                                       {'at': [60, 75, 61], 'role': 'ridge', 'seg': 'reroof-roof-inner', 'ws': {'type': 'bottom'}}])
    rbk = PP(house1={'record': hnew, 'records_files': 2, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [hold, hnew]})
    rp = lambda c, block, op='place', kind=None: J(base + [R('_build', 'project=house1 op=%s seg=reroof-roof-edge cell=%s block=%s%s' % (op, c, block, (' kind=%s' % kind) if kind else ''), s=14)], rbk)
    assert rp('60,74,60', 'spruce_stairs')['off_record'] == 0 and rp('60,75,61', 'spruce_slab')['off_record'] == 0, 'the new roof: cells of the head generation'
    assert rp('60,73,60', 'oak_planks')['off_record'] == 0, 'an old flat-roof cell repaired: still a cell'
    assert rp('60,75,60', 'spruce_stairs')['off_record'] == 1, 'positive control: one above the new roof is off the record'
    assert rp('60,74,60', 'spruce_stairs', op='clear', kind='reorient')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a misoriented new stair: RO1 with the generation\'s own palette'
    assert rp('60,73,60', 'oak_planks', op='clear', kind='reorient')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'the old flat roof is never dug as a reorient'
    assert rp('60,73,60', 'oak_planks', op='clear', kind='reroof')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'nor by any reroof dig: the retrofit digs nothing'
    # reroof-02 RT2: the retrofit's listed digs -- a decor ornament (listed + a decor cell, retired by then), canopy leaves, clutter;
    # the doorstep a head cell; the decor's retirement row judges nothing
    dc1 = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'plot': 'decor', 'blueprint': 'decor@1', 'at': T0MS - 60_000, 'structure_complete': True, 'home': {'x': 0, 'y': 70, 'z': 0},
           'instances': [], 'cells': [{'at': [61, 72, 59], 'role': 'D1-post', 'seg': 'D1-L0'}, {'at': [61, 73, 59], 'role': 'D1-lamp', 'seg': 'D1-L0'}, {'at': [62, 73, 59], 'role': 'D1-lamp', 'seg': 'D1-L1'}], 'clear': [], 'replant': []}
    dc2 = dict(dc1, gen=2, prev=1, at=T0MS - 30_000, cells=[dc1['cells'][2]], retired=[dict(c, by='reroof:house1', retiredAt=T0MS - 30_000) for c in dc1['cells'][:2]], retiredAt=T0MS - 30_000)
    hdig = dict(hnew, gen=3, prev=2, cells=hnew['cells'] + [{'at': [60, 71, 58], 'role': 'fill', 'seg': 'reroof-earth'}, {'at': [61, 73, 59], 'role': 'roofstair', 'seg': 'reroof-roof-edge'}],
                clear=[{'at': [61, 72, 59], 'kind': 'decor_post', 'name': 'spruce_fence'}, {'at': [61, 73, 59], 'kind': 'decor_post', 'name': 'lantern'},
                       {'at': [63, 73, 59], 'kind': 'decor_post', 'name': 'lantern'}, {'at': [62, 74, 60], 'kind': 'leaves', 'name': 'spruce_leaves'}])
    dbk = PP(house1={'record': hdig, 'records_files': 3, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [hold, hnew, hdig]}, decor={'record': dc2, 'records_files': 2, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [dc1, dc2]})
    rd = lambda c, block, kind='decor_post', pp=dbk, op='clear', seg='reroof-roof-edge': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=house1 op=%s seg=%s cell=%s block=%s kind=%s' % (op, seg, c, block, kind), s=14)], pp)
    assert rd('61,72,59', 'spruce_fence')['clear_outside'] == 0 and rd('61,73,59', 'lantern')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'RT2: a listed decor post (retired cells of the decor chain), fence and lantern'
    assert rd('61,73,59', 'minecraft:lantern')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a namespaced block reads the same'
    assert rd('61,73,59', 'lantern', pp=PP(house1={'record': hdig, 'records_files': 3, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [hold, hnew, hdig]}, decor={'record': dc2, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [dc2]}))['clear_outside'] == 0, 'RT2: a cell only the head\'s `retired` names (its first generation pruned) is still a decor cell'
    assert rd('63,73,59', 'lantern')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'RT2: listed, but no decor cell there'
    assert rd('61,73,59', 'lantern', pp=PP(house1={'record': hdig, 'records_files': 3, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [hold, hnew, hdig]}, decor={'record': dc1, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [dc1]}))['clear_outside'] == 1, 'RT2: a decor cell never retired (no hand-over) is never dug'
    dco = dict(dc2, retired=[dict(c, by='reroof:shop') for c in dc2['retired']])
    assert rd('61,73,59', 'lantern', pp=PP(house1={'record': hdig, 'records_files': 3, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [hold, hnew, hdig]}, decor={'record': dco, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [dco]}))['clear_outside'] == 1, 'RT2: retired for ANOTHER building\'s retrofit'
    assert rd('62,73,59', 'lantern')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'RT2: a decor cell the head does not list'
    assert rd('61,73,59', 'spruce_fence')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'RT2: listed with another block'
    assert rd('61,73,59', 'lantern', pp=PP(house1={'record': hdig, 'records_files': 3, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [hold, hnew, hdig]}))['clear_outside'] == 1, 'RT2: no decor record: never'
    assert rd('63,73,59', 'lantern')['_lists']['clear_outside'] == ['board-a-Alpha 63,73,59 decor_post lantern decor_post'], rd('63,73,59', 'lantern')['_lists']
    assert rd('62,74,60', 'spruce_leaves', kind='leaves')['clear_outside'] == 0 and rd('62,75,60', 'spruce_leaves', kind='leaves')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'canopy leaves: G2\'s list (listed clean, unlisted outside)'
    assert rd('60,74,60', 'dirt', kind='foreign')['clear_outside'] == 0 and rd('60,76,60', 'dirt', kind='foreign')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'clutter in a head roof cell: the foreign rule (its own cell clean, off it outside)'
    assert rd('60,71,58', 'cobblestone', op='place', seg='reroof-earth', kind='fill')['off_record'] == 0 and rd('60,71,57', 'cobblestone', op='place', seg='reroof-earth', kind='fill')['off_record'] == 1, 'the doorstep: a head cell (G1)'
    rr2 = J(base + [R('_build', 'project=decor op=retired cell=61,73,59 kind=reroof:house1 gen=2', s=14)], dbk)
    m00 = J(base, dbk)['mutations']
    assert rr2['off_record'] == 0 and rr2['clear_outside'] == 0 and rr2['zone'] == 0 and rr2['mutations'] == m00, ('the retirement row judges nothing', rr2)
    # enchantshop-01 EF1: the shell's cells, a fit slot with ITS item, the labels on their cells; anything else off the record
    erec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'blueprint': 'enchantshop@1', 'plot': 'enchantshop', 'cells': [{'at': [30, 65, 30], 'role': 'base', 'seg': 'pad'}],
            'fit': [{'slot': 'anvil', 'item': 'anvil', 'at': [31, 66, 30]}, {'slot': 'shelf-01', 'item': 'bookshelf', 'at': [32, 66, 30]}],
            'signs': [{'at': [33, 67, 30], 'wall': [34, 67, 30], 'lines': ['ANVIL']}], 'clear': [], 'replant': []}
    eb = PP(enchantshop={'record': erec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}})
    ep = lambda c, block, seg='fit': J(base + [R('_build', 'project=enchantshop op=place seg=%s cell=%s block=%s' % (seg, c, block), s=14)], eb)['off_record']
    assert ep('30,65,30', 'cobblestone', seg='pad') == 0, 'a shell cell'
    assert ep('31,66,30', 'anvil') == 0 and ep('32,66,30', 'bookshelf') == 0, 'a fit slot with its own item'
    assert ep('31,66,30', 'bookshelf') == 1 and ep('32,66,30', 'dirt') == 1, 'a slot with another block'
    assert ep('35,66,30', 'anvil') == 1, 'a fit item off every slot'
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=place seg=pad cell=31,66,30 block=anvil', s=14)], eb)['off_record'] == 1, 'positive control: the slot is the workshop\'s only'
    es = lambda c, ok='1', why='written': J(base + [R('_enchantshop_sign', 'op=place at=%s chest=enchantshop:anvil label=ANVIL ok=%s why=%s' % (c, ok, why), s=14)], eb)
    assert es('33,67,30')['off_record'] == 0 and es('33,67,30')['enchant_sign_places'] == 1, 'a label on its cell'
    assert es('36,67,30')['off_record'] == 1, 'a label off every label cell'
    assert es('36,67,30', ok='0', why='no_wall')['off_record'] == 0, 'nothing placed: never judged'
    assert es('36,67,30', ok='0', why='no_editor')['off_record'] == 1, 'placed, text unconfirmed: judged'
    # its completion is counted, never the workshop's (the world evidence and H8 stay the workshop's)
    rc = J(base + [R('_structure_complete', 'project=monument op=complete kind=ziggurat@1 gen=9', s=30, bot='board-b-Alpha')], both)
    assert rc['complete_towns'] == 2 and rc['projects_complete'] == 1, rc
    # leases: the ziggurat's own three slots; a fourth is a breach; the workshop's two unchanged
    L3 = {'slot3': [(1, {'holder': 'C', 'at': 0, 'until': 1}, 5)]}
    assert J(base, PP(monument={'record': mrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': L3}))['lease_overlap'] == 0
    assert J(base, PP(monument={'record': mrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {'slot4': L3['slot3']}}))['lease_overlap'] == 1
    assert J(base, PP(house1={'record': hrec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': L3}))['lease_overlap'] == 1, 'a house admits two'
    # the pathfinder's dirt and stone scaffold is never a loss (the ziggurat places them); a part still is
    assert J(base + [R('_build_bag', 'project=monument exit=complete silent=0 dirt=20/0/0/5/0/10 andesite=4/0/0/0/0/1', s=50)])['bag_loss'] == 0
    assert J(base + [R('_build_bag', 'project=house1 exit=complete silent=0 glass_pane=0/3/0/2/0/0', s=50)])['bag_loss'] == 1
    # G5 from the felling's own evidence: fewer saplings than replant cells; a tree clear without the field is unjudged
    assert J([x for x in base if 'kind=tree' not in x['detail']] + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=0/1', s=9)])['felled_unplanted'] == 1
    # FF1 (fellfirst-01): the same short felling marked first=1 (no sapling of its kind anywhere) is counted, never H5 (positive control above)
    ff = J([x for x in base if 'kind=tree' not in x['detail']] + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=0/1 first=1', s=9)])
    assert ff['felled_unplanted'] == 0 and ff.get('felled_first', 0) == 1, ('FF1: a fell-first felling is no H5', ff['felled_unplanted'], ff.get('felled_first', 0))
    # PT1 (11-06): PER-TREE B4 -- a tagged record (its tree clears and replants carry `tree`): a short record-wide count is no H5
    trec = dict(rec, clear=[{'at': [12, 71, 10], 'kind': 'tree', 'name': 'oak_log', 'tree': 0}, {'at': [14, 71, 10], 'kind': 'tree', 'name': 'oak_log', 'tree': 1}],
                replant=[{'cell': [20, 70, 20], 'sapling': 'oak_sapling', 'tree': 0}, {'cell': [21, 70, 20], 'sapling': 'oak_sapling', 'tree': 1}])
    tpr = {'record': trec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'zones_ok': True}
    tpj = {'board-a': tpr, 'board-b': tpr}
    nob = [x for x in base if 'kind=tree' not in x['detail']]
    pt0 = J(nob + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=1/2', s=9)], tpj)
    assert pt0['felled_unplanted'] == 0 and pt0['g5_unjudged'] == 1, ('PT1: the tree owed its own replant: unjudged, never H5', pt0['felled_unplanted'], pt0['g5_unjudged'])
    # (Claude PT1 review BLOCKER 1) a replant of the tree SKIPPED since is still owed at the felling: unjudged, never clean
    trec2 = dict(trec, replant=[{'cell': [20, 70, 20], 'sapling': 'oak_sapling', 'tree': 0, 'state': 'skipped'}, {'cell': [21, 70, 20], 'sapling': 'oak_sapling', 'tree': 1}])
    tpr2 = dict(tpr, record=trec2); tpj2 = {'board-a': tpr2, 'board-b': tpr2}
    pt1 = J(nob + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=0/1', s=9)], tpj2)
    assert pt1['felled_unplanted'] == 0 and pt1['g5_unjudged'] == 1, ('PT1: a skipped replant of the tree: unjudged', pt1['felled_unplanted'], pt1['g5_unjudged'])
    # a tree that never owed a replant (the record's replants are all another tree's): clean
    trec3 = dict(trec, replant=[{'cell': [21, 70, 20], 'sapling': 'oak_sapling', 'tree': 1}])
    tpr3 = dict(tpr, record=trec3); tpj3 = {'board-a': tpr3, 'board-b': tpr3}
    pt3 = J(nob + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=0/1', s=9)], tpj3)
    assert pt3['felled_unplanted'] == 0 and pt3['g5_unjudged'] == 0, ('PT1: the tree never owed a replant: clean', pt3['felled_unplanted'], pt3['g5_unjudged'])
    # (BLOCKER 2) EVERY generation in force: an older one in which the tree owed a replant keeps the row unjudged
    assert g5_pertree([trec, trec3], [12, 71, 10], None) == 1, 'PT1: the most any generation in force owed'
    # (Codex PT1 r2 BLOCKER) the cell in BOTH lists with different tags: the most any match owed (here 1), never the first match's 0
    trec4 = dict(trec3, clear=[{'at': [12, 71, 10], 'kind': 'tree', 'name': 'oak_log', 'tree': 2}], fell={'clear': [{'at': [12, 71, 10], 'kind': 'tree', 'name': 'oak_log', 'tree': 1}]})
    assert g5_pertree([trec4], [12, 71, 10], None) == 1, 'PT1: every clear listing the cell'
    assert g5_pertree([dict(trec4, fell={'clear': [{'at': [12, 71, 10], 'kind': 'tree', 'name': 'oak_log'}]})], [12, 71, 10], None) is None, 'PT1: any untagged match -> the old rule'
    assert g5_pertree([rec], [12, 71, 10], None) is None and g5_pertree([trec], [12, 71, 10], None) == 1 and g5_pertree([trec3], [12, 71, 10], None) == 0, 'PT1: per-tree only when tagged'
    assert J([x for x in base if 'kind=tree' not in x['detail']] + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log kind=tree', s=9)])['g5_unjudged'] == 1
    assert J([x for x in base if 'kind=tree' not in x['detail']] + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=0/0', s=9)])['g5_unjudged'] == 1, 'Codex read r2 P2-4: 0/0 against a record with a replant'
    # G5R (phase 1 item 4, 11-06): the denominator is judged against the generations IN FORCE at the felling, never only the
    # current record (47 of 52 live g5_unjudged items were a later generation or a replant skipped since)
    ms = lambda d: int(d.timestamp() * 1000)
    g1 = dict(rec, gen=1, at=ms(t0 - dt.timedelta(hours=1)), replant=[{'cell': [20 + i, 70, 20], 'sapling': 'oak_sapling'} for i in range(5)])
    g2 = dict(rec, gen=2, at=ms(t0 + dt.timedelta(seconds=100)), replant=[{'cell': [20, 70, 20], 'sapling': 'oak_sapling'}])
    tree = lambda sap, sec: [x for x in base if 'kind=tree' not in x['detail']] + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=%s' % sap, s=sec)]
    ch = PP(shop={'record': g2, 'chain': [g1, g2], 'records_files': 2, 'leases': {}})
    r5 = J(tree('2/5', 9), ch)
    assert r5['g5_unjudged'] == 0 and r5['felled_unplanted'] == 1, ('G5R: 2/5 under the generation in force (5 replants) is JUDGED: a short felling', r5['g5_unjudged'], r5['felled_unplanted'])
    assert J(tree('2/5', 9), PP(shop={'record': g2, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}}))['g5_unjudged'] == 1, 'G5R positive control: against the current record alone it is unjudged'
    assert J(tree('1/7', 9), ch)['g5_unjudged'] == 1, 'G5R: a denominator no generation in force had stays unjudged'
    assert J(tree('2/5', -7200), ch)['g5_unjudged'] == 1, 'G5R: a felling before the first generation was sited judges against none'
    sk = dict(rec, replant=[{'cell': [20, 70, 20]}, {'cell': [21, 70, 20]}, {'cell': [22, 70, 20], 'state': 'skipped'}])
    skp = PP(shop={'record': sk, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}})
    assert J(tree('3/3', 9), skp)['g5_unjudged'] == 0 and J(tree('2/2', 9), skp)['g5_unjudged'] == 0, 'G5R: a replant skipped AFTER the felling: 3/3 or 2/2 both judged'
    assert J(tree('4/4', 9), skp)['g5_unjudged'] == 1, 'G5R: more than the generation ever listed stays unjudged'
    odd = PP(shop={'record': dict(rec, replant=[None, {'cell': [20, 70, 20]}]), 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}})
    assert J(tree('1/1', 9), odd)['g5_unjudged'] == 0, 'G5R: a non-dict replant entry is skipped, never a crash'
    assert J([x for x in base if 'kind=tree' not in x['detail']] + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=2/1', s=9)])['g5_unjudged'] == 1, 'an impossible fraction'
    # shop-01 F2: a SKIPPED replant is no obligation -- 0/0 against a record whose only replant is skipped is judged and clean
    recs = dict(rec, replant=[{'cell': [20, 70, 20], 'sapling': 'oak_sapling', 'state': 'skipped'}])
    projs = {p: dict(proj[p], record=recs) for p in proj}
    felled00 = [x for x in base if 'kind=tree' not in x['detail']] + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log kind=tree saplings=0/0', s=9)]
    rs = J(felled00 + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=replant_skipped cell=20,70,20 result=accepted', s=8)], projects=projs)
    assert rs['g5_unjudged'] == 0 and rs['felled_unplanted'] == 0 and rs.get('replant_skipped') == 1, rs
    # F4: a stuck build is a named row the read counts (never silent)
    rst = J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=deferred seg=pad cell=10,70,10 n=1', s=30), R('_build', 'project=shop op=stuck_on_cell cell=10,70,10 visits=3 n=1', s=31)])
    assert rst.get('deferred') == 1 and rst.get('stuck_on_cell') == 1, rst
    rsu = J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=stuck_unwritten cell=10,70,10 n=1', s=30)])
    assert rsu.get('stuck_unwritten') == 1, rsu
    rss = J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=stuck_on_cell cell=10,70,10 visits=3 n=1 segs=earth+pad waiting=168 stalled=true', s=31)])
    assert len(rss.get('stalled_pools') or {}) == 1 and list(rss['stalled_pools'].values()) == ['168'], rss
    assert not (rst.get('stalled_pools') or {}), rst
    # G6 on the bot's own build rows: the stand IN or ON the unknown cell; not after it is known again; not after a new connection
    unk = R('_build', 'project=shop op=unknown cell=12,71,10 block=oak_log', s=40)
    on = R('_build_seg', 'project=shop seg=pad open=1 done=1', s=41, feet=[12, 72, 10])
    inside = R('_build_seg', 'project=shop seg=pad open=1 done=1', s=41, feet=[12, 71, 10])
    walk = R('_pos', '', s=41, feet=[12, 72, 10])
    assert J(base + [unk, on])['unknown_stood'] == 1
    assert J(base + [unk, inside])['unknown_stood'] == 1, 'feet in the cell'
    assert J(base + [unk, walk])['unknown_stood'] == 0, 'another skill walking over it (the server may have resynced it): not judged'
    assert J(base + [unk, R('_build', 'project=shop op=known cell=12,71,10 block=air', s=40.5), on])['unknown_stood'] == 0
    assert J(base + [unk, R('_build_mode', 'phase=mode v=1 shop=on', s=40.5), on])['unknown_stood'] == 0, 'a reconnect: a new connection'
    assert J(base + [unk, R('_build_mode', 'phase=mode v=1 shop=on', s=40.5, feet=[12, 72, 10])])['unknown_stood'] == 0, 'the mode row itself, standing there (Codex read r2 P2-2)'
    # G8 a part the visit cannot account for; cobblestone/planks short = scaffold, never a loss
    assert J(base + [R('_build_bag', 'project=shop exit=no_material silent=0 glass_pane=0/4/0/2/1/0', s=50)])['bag_loss'] == 1
    assert J(base + [R('_build_bag', 'project=shop exit=complete silent=0 oak_planks=0/49/0/40/0/0', s=50)])['bag_loss'] == 0
    assert J(base + [R('_build_bag', 'project=shop exit=complete silent=0 torch=0/2/0/2/0/3', s=50)])['bag_loss'] == 0, 'a pickup is not a loss'
    for ex, si in (('died', '0'), ('aborted', '-'), ('complete', '1'), ('error', '0'), ('-', '-')):
        rj = J(base + [R('_build_bag', 'project=shop exit=%s silent=%s glass_pane=0/4/0/2/0/0' % (ex, si), s=50)])
        assert rj['bag_loss'] == 0 and rj['bag_unjudged'] == 1, ('Claude read r2 P2: not judged', ex, si)
    # ORACLE: a town building with no readable record; with no readable zones file
    assert J(base, {**proj, 'board-a': PR(record=None, records_files=0)})['oracle_missing'] == 1
    assert J(base, {**proj, 'board-b': PR(zones_ok=False)})['zone_unknown'] == 1
    # G7 is REPORTED, not a gate (a re-placement is physically a repair): counted, and absent from the gated fields' failures
    r7 = J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=place seg=pad cell=10,70,10 block=stone_bricks', s=60)])
    assert r7['placed_twice'] == 1 and r7['off_record'] == 0
    # arms
    assert J(base + [R('_build_visit', 'project=shop outcome=off', arm='control', bot='hive-b-A')])['arms_breach'] == 1
    # EXPOSURE is WORLD evidence: completion rows without a count after them, or without a rise, are not ready
    assert J(base, g=gens[:1])['complete_towns_room'] == 0, 'no count after the completion yet'
    assert J(base, g=[gens[0], (gens[1][0], day(1, 0))])['complete_towns_room'] == 1, 'board-b shows no building'
    assert J(base, g=[])['complete_towns_room'] == 0, 'no mirror'
    assert J(base, g=[gens[0], (gens[1][0], day(1, 1, where_a=False))])['complete_towns_room'] == 1, 'Codex read r2 P2-5: a lit room ELSEWHERE in board-a does not count'
    assert J(base, g=[gens[0], (gens[1][0], {**day(1, 1), 'board-a': {'buildings': 1, 'building_rooms': [room(11, 10, lit=False)], 'litter_cells': 100}})])['complete_towns_room'] == 1, 'an unlit room is no building'
    X = [731, 120, 760, 737, 125, 768]   # the sandbox8 record's cell extent
    assert rooms_in_box({'building_rooms': [json.dumps({'centre': [734.4, 121, 764.0], 'lit': True})]}, X) == 1, 'the sandbox8 positive control'
    assert rooms_in_box({'building_rooms': [json.dumps({'centre': [734.4, 140, 764.0], 'lit': True})]}, X) == 0, 'Codex read r3 P2-2: a room ABOVE the workshop'
    assert rooms_in_box({}, X) is None and rooms_in_box({'building_rooms': ['{"cen']}, X) is None and rooms_in_box(None, X) is None, 'Codex read r3 P2-1: missing or malformed is unknown'
    gm = [(gens[0][0], {k: v for k, v in day(0, 0).items() if k != 'board-a'}), gens[1]]
    assert J(base, g=gm)['complete_towns_room'] == 1, 'board-a has no room data before the deploy: unknown, not zero'
    assert J(base, g=[(gens[0][0], {**day(0, 0), 'board-a': {'buildings': 1, 'building_rooms': [room(11, 10)], 'litter_cells': 100}}), gens[1]])['complete_towns_room'] == 1, 'a room in the box BEFORE the deploy: board-a does not count'
    assert J([x for x in base if x['bot'] != 'board-b-Alpha'])['complete_towns_room'] == 1, 'one town'
    # litter DiD: canary towns' change less the control towns'
    rl = J(base, g=[gens[0], (gens[1][0], day(1, 1, la=180, lb=140, lc=110))])
    assert abs(rl['litter_did'] - (60 - 10)) < 1e-9, rl['litter_did']
    # read_projects over real files: a MISSING zones file is known (no zones), a corrupt one is not; the wall lease is read
    import tempfile
    root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='shopread-st-')
    for town, zbody in (('board-a', None), ('board-b', '{"zo')):
        d = os.path.join(root, '_pool-%s' % town); os.makedirs(d)
        if zbody is not None:
            open(os.path.join(d, 'protected-zones.json'), 'w').write(zbody)
        open(os.path.join(d, 'project-0_70_0.shop.g1.json'), 'w').write(json.dumps({'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'cells': [{'at': [1, 2, 3]}]}))
        open(os.path.join(d, 'project-0_70_0.shop.seg-wall-F.lease.g1.json'), 'w').write(json.dumps(L('A', 0, 1)))
    pj = read_projects(root)
    assert pj['board-a']['zones_ok'] is True and pj['board-b']['zones_ok'] is False, pj
    good = {'version': 1, 'zones': [{'id': 'v', 'box': [0, 0, 0, 10, 10, 10]}]}
    assert zones_valid(good) and zones_valid({'version': 1, 'zones': []})
    for bad in ({}, {'version': 1, 'zones': None}, {'version': 2, 'zones': []}, {'version': 1, 'zones': [{'box': [0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1]}]},
                {'version': 1, 'zones': [{'id': 'v', 'box': [5, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1]}]}, {'version': 1, 'zones': [{'id': 'v', 'box': [0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1], 'floor_y': 9}]}, None, []):
        assert not zones_valid(bad), ('Codex read r2 P2-3', bad)
    assert pj['board-a']['record']['gen'] == 1 and list(pj['board-a']['leases']) == ['seg-wall-F'], pj['board-a']
    # the record head walk: a broken chain link is not the head
    b1 = json.dumps({'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'cells': []}).encode()
    b2 = json.dumps({'gen': 2, 'prev': 1, 'prevSha': hashlib.sha256(b1).hexdigest(), 'cells': [{'at': [1, 2, 3]}]}).encode()
    b3 = json.dumps({'gen': 3, 'prev': 2, 'prevSha': 'f' * 64, 'cells': []}).encode()
    assert record_head([(1, b1), (2, b2), (3, b3)])['gen'] == 2
    # no-harm: a rung collapse with enough rows -> DOWN; few rows -> UNEXPOSED
    rows = []
    for arm, per, ok, n in (('canary', 'pre', 9, 10), ('canary', 'post', 2, 10), ('control', 'pre', 9, 10), ('control', 'post', 9, 10)):
        for i in range(n):
            rows.append(R('craft', '', arm=arm, per=per, status='success' if i < ok else 'failed', item='stone_pickaxe', bot='board-a-A' if arm == 'canary' else 'hive-b-A'))
    rr = combine(*judge(rows, {}, {}))
    assert rr['rung_ok'] == 0 and verdict_of(rr, 'rung_ok') == 'DOWN', rr
    assert verdict_of(combine(*judge(rows[:5], {}, {})), 'rung_ok') == 'UNEXPOSED'
    # storehub-01 SH1 kept_thrown (the hall clerk's well throws judged by the keep lists) and the hall's signs / cells (G1)
    hrec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 2, 'blueprint': 'storehub@1', 'plot': 'storehub', 'box': [-21, -49, -3, -39],
            'cells': [{'at': [-10, 65, -40], 'role': 'chest', 'seg': 'fit-out'}, {'at': [-10, 64, -41], 'role': 'base', 'seg': 'pad'}],
            'chests': [{'at': [-10, 65, -40], 'section': 'stone', 'role': 'home', 'sign': [-10, 66, -40], 'wall': [-10, 66, -39], 'stand': [-10, 65, -41]}],
            'clear': [], 'replant': []}
    HP = {'board-a': {'record': rec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'zones_ok': True,
                      'projects': {'shop': {'record': rec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}},
                                   'storehub': {'record': hrec, 'chain': [dict(hrec, gen=1, chests=[]), hrec], 'records_files': 2, 'leases': {}}}}}
    def hub(rows_):
        o, l = judge(rows_, HP, {'board-a': [[-1, 60, -1, 1, 80, 1]]})
        return o, l
    o, l = hub([R('_well_dispose', 'via=clerk slots=30->29 offlist=0 tossed=10 n=10 stop=done items=flint:10'),
                R('_storehub_clerk', 'phase=visit junked=flint:10 src=-9,71,-2 took=flint:10 stop=done'),
                R('_well_dispose', 'slots=30->29 offlist=0 swords=1 n=1 stop=done items=iron_sword:1'),
                R('_storehub_clerk', 'phase=cargo_gone junked=- src=- took=iron_ingot:3 stop=cargo_gone')])
    assert o['kept_thrown'] == 0 and o['bag_loss'] == 0 and o['hub_well_rows'] == 1 and o['hub_clerk_rows'] == 2, (dict(o), dict(l))
    o, l = hub([R('_well_dispose', 'via=clerk slots=30->29 n=13 stop=done items=flint:10,iron_ingot:3')])
