    assert o['kept_thrown'] == 1 and o['bag_loss'] == 1 and l['bag_loss'] == ['SH1 kept_thrown board-a-Alpha iron_ingot:3 (well via=clerk)'], (dict(o), dict(l))
    o, l = hub([R('_storehub_clerk', 'phase=visit junked=white_wool:2,flint:4 src=-9,71,-2 stop=done')])
    assert o['kept_thrown'] == 1 and l['kept_thrown'] == ['board-a-Alpha clerk junked white_wool:2'], 'a decoration is building material in entrusted cargo'
    o, l = hub([R('_well_dispose', 'via=clerk slots=30->29 offlist=0 n=1 stop=done')])
    assert o['kept_unjudged'] == 1 and o['kept_thrown'] == 0 and o['bag_unjudged'] == 1, 'a cut row is reported, never a trip on a guess'
    o, l = hub([R('_well_dispose', 'via=clerk lost=flint:10,iron_ingot:3 slots=30->29 n=13 stop=aborted ' + 'x' * 260 + ' items=flint:10,iron_in')])
    assert o['kept_thrown'] == 1 and o['kept_unjudged'] == 0, 'the early lost= is judged whatever the cut left of items='
    o, l = hub([R('_well_dispose', 'via=clerk slots=30->29 n=10 stop=done ' + 'x' * 260 + ' items=flint:10,iron_in')])
    assert o['kept_unjudged'] == 1 and o['kept_thrown'] == 0, 'a cut items= with no lost= is unjudged (its last name may be cut)'
    o, l = hub([R('_storehub_clerk', 'phase=visit junked=diamond_pickaxe:1 stop=done', arm='control')])
    assert o['kept_thrown'] == 0, 'the canary arm only (the showcase: every bot)'
    # the hall's signs: the cell above a hall chest of any generation, else off_record; the hall's cells are G1's like any record's
    o, l = hub([R('_hub_sign', 'op=place at=-10,66,-40 chest=-10,65,-40 label=STONE ok=1'), R('_hub_sign', 'op=edit at=-10,66,-41 chest=-10,65,-40 label=STONE ok=1'),
                R('_build', 'project=storehub op=place cell=-10,65,-40 block=chest'), R('_build', 'project=storehub op=place cell=-10,64,-41 block=cobblestone')])
    assert o['off_record'] == 0 and o['hub_sign_places'] == 1 and o['placements'] == 2, (dict(o), dict(l))
    o, l = hub([R('_hub_sign', 'op=place at=-10,67,-40 chest=-10,65,-40 label=STONE ok=1'), R('_build', 'project=storehub op=place cell=-11,65,-40 block=chest')])
    assert o['off_record'] == 2 and l['off_record'] == ['board-a-Alpha -10,67,-40 hub_sign', 'board-a-Alpha -11,65,-40 chest'], (dict(o), dict(l))
    o, l = hub([R('_hub_sign', 'op=place at=0,66,0 chest=0,65,0 label=STONE ok=1')])
    assert o['zone'] == 1, 'a sign in a protected zone'
    o, l = hub([R('_hub_sign', 'op=place at=0,66,0 chest=0,65,0 label=STONE ok=0 why=%s' % w) for w in ('no_sign', 'no_wall', 'out_of_reach', 'not_placed')])
    assert o['off_record'] == 0 and o['zone'] == 0 and o['hub_sign_places'] == 0, 'nothing was placed: never judged'
    o, l = hub([R('_hub_sign', 'op=place at=-10,67,-40 chest=-10,65,-40 label=STONE ok=0 why=no_editor'), R('_hub_sign', 'op=place at=-10,67,-40 chest=-10,65,-40 label=STONE ok=0 why=error:timeout')])
    assert o['off_record'] == 1 and o['hub_sign_unjudged'] == 1, 'placed without its text: judged; an error is reported'
    # never G6: a clerk standing at the well on a cell its own builder row named unknown
    o, l = hub([R('_build_mode', 'phase=mode v=1 shop=on'), R('_build', 'project=shop op=unknown cell=5,71,14', s=1),
                R('_well_dispose', 'via=clerk n=1 stop=done items=flint:1', s=2, feet=[5, 71, 14])])
    assert o['unknown_stood'] == 0, dict(o)
    assert hub_kept('iron_ingot') and hub_kept('white_wool') and hub_kept('oak_sapling') and not hub_kept('flint') and not hub_kept('andesite')
    # the never-list is a second guard: an allowlist widened by mistake still never passes a kept kind
    global HUB_THROWABLE
    saved = HUB_THROWABLE
    HUB_THROWABLE = saved | {'iron_ingot', 'oak_log'}
    try:
        assert hub_kept('iron_ingot') and hub_kept('oak_log'), 'the never-list holds a widened allowlist'
    finally:
        HUB_THROWABLE = saved
    # THE COPY: the lists are bots/src/storehub.mjs's (checked when the module is beside this file's repo)
    here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    mods = [m for m in (os.environ.get('SHOPREAD_STOREHUB') or '', os.path.join(here, '..', '..', '..', 'bots', 'src', 'storehub.mjs'), os.path.join(here, 'storehub.mjs')) if m and os.path.exists(m)]
    if not mods:
        print('shopread selftest: SKIPPED the HUB_THROWABLE / HUB_NEVER_THROW copy check (no storehub.mjs: set SHOPREAD_STOREHUB; bots/test/storehub.test.mjs checks it from the bot side)')
    for mod in mods[:1]:
        js = open(mod).read()
        m = re.search(r"export const HUB_THROWABLE = Object\.freeze\(\[(.*?)\]\)", js, re.S)
        assert m and set(re.findall(r"'([a-z_]+)'", m.group(1))) == set(HUB_THROWABLE), 'HUB_THROWABLE differs from storehub.mjs'
        m = re.search(r"export const HUB_NEVER_THROW = /(.*)/\n", js)
        assert m and m.group(1) == HUB_NEVER_THROW.pattern, 'HUB_NEVER_THROW differs from storehub.mjs'
    # decorator-01 THE COPY: DECOR_SPREAD and DECOR_BLOOMS are bots/src/decorator.mjs's (checked when the module is beside this file's repo)
    dmods = [m for m in (os.environ.get('SHOPREAD_DECORATOR') or '', os.path.join(here, '..', '..', '..', 'bots', 'src', 'decorator.mjs'), os.path.join(here, 'decorator.mjs')) if m and os.path.exists(m)]
    if not dmods:
        print('shopread selftest: SKIPPED the DECOR_SPREAD / DECOR_BLOOMS copy check (no decorator.mjs: set SHOPREAD_DECORATOR)')
    for mod in dmods[:1]:
        js = open(mod).read()
        m = re.search(r"  spread: (\d+),", js)
        assert m and int(m.group(1)) == DECOR_SPREAD, 'DECOR_SPREAD differs from decorator.mjs NURSERY.spread'
        m = re.search(r"  fernR: (\d+),", js)
        assert m and int(m.group(1)) == DECOR_FERN_R, 'DECOR_FERN_R differs from decorator.mjs DECOR.fernR'
        # flowers-01: FLOWER.wildR, when this decorator.mjs carries it (main before the flowers-01 fold does not)
        m = re.search(r"  wildR: (\d+),", js)
        if m:
            assert int(m.group(1)) == DECOR_WILD_R, 'DECOR_WILD_R differs from decorator.mjs FLOWER.wildR'
        else:
            print('shopread selftest: SKIPPED the DECOR_WILD_R copy check (this decorator.mjs has no FLOWER.wildR: set SHOPREAD_DECORATOR)')
        m = re.search(r"export const HEDGE_LEAVES = Object\.freeze\(\[(.*?)\]\)", js, re.S)
        assert m and set(re.findall(r"'([a-z_]+)'", m.group(1))) == set(DECOR_LEAVES), 'DECOR_LEAVES differs from decorator.mjs HEDGE_LEAVES'
        m = re.search(r"export const SMALL_FLOWERS = Object\.freeze\(\[(.*?)\]\)", open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(mod), 'park.mjs')).read(), re.S)
        assert m and set(re.findall(r"'([a-z_]+)'", m.group(1))) | {'short_grass', 'tall_grass'} == set(DECOR_BLOOMS), 'DECOR_BLOOMS differs from decorator.mjs BLOOM_BLOCKS'
    # Claude DC5 r1 BLOCKER: no decor refusal tag may read as tree debris in the showcase tripwire's H2 split (its LAST word is taken
    # as the block): every tag checked against infra/showcase/showcase_trip.py's TREE_DEBRIS when it is beside this file's repo
    tmods = [m for m in (os.environ.get('SHOPREAD_TRIP') or '', os.path.join(here, '..', '..', '..', 'infra', 'showcase', 'showcase_trip.py')) if m and os.path.exists(m)]
    if not tmods:
        print('shopread selftest: SKIPPED the decor tags vs TREE_DEBRIS check (no showcase_trip.py: set SHOPREAD_TRIP)')
    for mod in tmods[:1]:
        m = re.search(r"TREE_DEBRIS = re\.compile\((r'.*?')\)\n", open(mod).read(), re.S)
        assert m, 'TREE_DEBRIS not found in showcase_trip.py'
        td = re.compile(eval(''.join(re.findall(r"r'((?:[^'\\]|\\.)*)'", m.group(1))).join(["r'", "'"])))
        assert td.match('poppy') and td.match('fern'), 'positive control: the debris regex reads flowers and ferns'
        for tag in ('fern_rule', 'leaves_rule', 'wild_rule', 'lawn_meal', 'bloom', 'meal', 'pot', 'guarded', 'in_park', 'tidy_cell', 'decor_post', 'forest_fell', 'forest_leafrule', 'forest_meal', 'farm_retire'):
            assert not td.match(tag), 'a decor refusal tag reads as tree debris: %s' % tag
    # sinkwaive-01 SK1 (11-06; bots/src/sinks.mjs sinkWaiveDue, skills.mjs buildSink): a stone sink's WAIVER row -- `_build
    # project=tower|road|shrine op=waived cell=.. why=.. gen=..` -- names a record cell the build gives up (no stand reached it); it
    # changes no block, so it is NO MUTATION: never G1/G2/G3, never counted in `mutations` (as the wall's, paths' and tidy's own
    # op=waived rows already are). Locked here with a positive control and a text mutant of the judge's mutation-op list.
    wv = 'project=tower op=waived cell=40,71,40 gen=6 why=no_stand'
    zt = {'board-a': [[40, 0, 40, 40, 200, 40]]}
    m_b = J(base, SP_())['mutations']
    sk = J(base + [R('_build', wv, s=14)], SP_(), zones=zt)
    assert sk['zone'] == 0 and sk['off_record'] == 0 and sk['clear_outside'] == 0 and sk['mutations'] == m_b, ('a waiver is no mutation', sk['zone'], sk['mutations'], m_b)
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=tower op=place seg=T1-L01-N cell=40,71,40 block=cobblestone', s=14)], SP_(), zones=zt)['zone'] == 1, 'positive control: a placement on that cell in the zone is G3'
    MUT_OPS = "            if op in ('place', 'clear', 'replant', 'meal'):\n"
    src_sr = open(os.path.abspath(__file__)).read()
    assert src_sr.count(MUT_OPS) == 1, 'ANCHOR MISSING OR NOT UNIQUE'
    ns_m = {'__name__': 'shopread_mutant', '__file__': os.path.abspath(__file__)}
    exec(compile(src_sr.replace(MUT_OPS, "            if op in ('place', 'clear', 'replant', 'meal', 'waived'):\n"), 'shopread_mutant', 'exec'), ns_m)
    skm = ns_m['combine'](*ns_m['judge'](base + [R('_build', wv, s=14)], SP_(), zt, cut=CUT, gens=gens))
    assert skm['zone'] == 1, 'MUTANT SURVIVED: op=waived as a mutation'
    # prodserve-01 PS1 (11-06; bots/src/towndress.mjs dressOrphans, skills.mjs buildSink + serviceBegin): (a) a DRESS batch's host-gone
    # waiver -- `_build project=dress op=waived cell=.. why=host_gone gen=..` (a light whose support another project dug: the old wall's
    # wall-top torch 20,73,-20) -- names a record cell the batch gives up; it changes no block: NO MUTATION, never G1/G2/G3, as SK1's
    # (its feet are still judged by G6, below).
    # (b) the service tier's `_service plot=.. reason=.. last_min=.. overdue_min=..` row is a DECISION row, not a build row: no defect,
    # no mutation, nothing counted. A service visit's own rows are the project's existing ones (G9 crop harvest, CF1 cane harvest, a
    # farm's place/replant, SH1 the clerk), judged as before. Positive control: a dress placement on that cell in its zone is G3.
    dw = 'project=dress op=waived cell=20,73,-20 gen=11 why=host_gone'
    zd = {'board-a': [[20, 0, -20, 20, 200, -20]]}
    m_d = J(base, SP_())['mutations']
    ps = J(base + [R('_build', dw, s=14), R('_service', 'plot=canefarm reason=harvest last_min=never overdue_min=first', s=15)], SP_(), zones=zd)
    assert ps['zone'] == 0 and ps['off_record'] == 0 and ps['clear_outside'] == 0 and ps['mutations'] == m_d, ('PS1: a dress waiver and a service row are no mutation', ps['zone'], ps['mutations'], m_d)
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=dress op=place seg=L1-P3 cell=20,73,-20 block=torch', s=14)], SP_(), zones=zd)['zone'] == 1, 'PS1 positive control: a dress placement in the zone is G3'
    skd = ns_m['combine'](*ns_m['judge'](base + [R('_build', dw, s=14)], SP_(), zd, cut=CUT, gens=gens))
    assert skd['zone'] == 1, 'MUTANT SURVIVED: a dress op=waived as a mutation (PS1)'
    # PS1 (Codex PS1 review): a waiver is NO MUTATION, but it is still a positioned build row -- G6 (unknown_stood) judges its feet like
    # every `_build` row's (the emitter carries the bot's snapshot): a waiver logged standing on a cell the bot's own op=unknown named is
    # G6; standing elsewhere, clean. Never exempt from the position checks.
    unk_d = R('_build', 'project=dress op=unknown cell=20,72,-20', s=13)
    assert J(base + [unk_d, R('_build', dw, s=14, feet=[20, 73, -20])], SP_(), zones=zd)['unknown_stood'] == 1, 'PS1: a waiver stood on its own unknown cell is G6'
    assert J(base + [unk_d, R('_build', dw, s=14, feet=[25, 70, -25])], SP_(), zones=zd)['unknown_stood'] == 0, 'PS1 control: a waiver stood elsewhere is clean'
    # stepwell-05 SW5 (11-06; bots/src/stepwell.mjs stepwellCrewDue/stepwellBuildersNow): THE CREW -- a visit that reads every listed log
    # down publishes a generation with builders_max raised to STEPWELL_CREW (2, at most 3) BEFORE any second slot is taken; G4 judges
    # slot names against the HEAD record's builders_max (as for every project): slot2 under the raised head is clean, slot2 under a head
    # of 1 and slot3 under a head of 2 are G4. Its new rows are no defect: `_build_visit outcome=shared|felling_one` (counted only),
    # `_stepwell op=crew` (not a build row), a `_build_bag exit=shared` judged like any clean exit.
    sw1 = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'blueprint': 'stepwell@1', 'plot': 'stepwell', 'builders_max': 1, 'cells': [{'at': [60, 66, 60], 'role': 'floor', 't': 1}],
           'clear': [{'at': [60, 67, 60], 'kind': 'cut', 't': 1}], 'replant': []}
    sw2 = dict(sw1, gen=2, prev=1, builders_max=2)
    crewL = {'slot1': [(1, L('showcase-A', 0, 10**12), None)], 'slot2': [(1, L('showcase-B', 1_000, 10**12), None)]}
    SWP = lambda head, leases=crewL: PP(stepwell={'record': head, 'records_files': head['gen'], 'leases': leases, 'chain': [sw1, head] if head is not sw1 else [sw1]})
    assert J(base, SWP(sw2))['lease_overlap'] == 0, 'two builders on their own slots under the raised head'
    assert J(base, SWP(sw1))['lease_overlap'] == 1, 'positive control: slot2 under a head of 1 is G4'
    assert J(base, SWP(sw2, dict(crewL, slot3=[(1, L('showcase-C', 2_000, 10**12), None)])))['lease_overlap'] == 1, 'slot3 under a head of 2 is G4'
    crew_rows = [R('_build_visit', 'project=stepwell outcome=shared stop=shared placed=40 refused=0 silent=0 cleared=80', s=14),
                 R('_build_visit', 'project=stepwell outcome=felling_one stop=felling_one', s=15),
                 R('_stepwell', 'op=crew y=- at=- why=accepted n=2 ms=- gen=2', s=13),
                 R('_build_bag', 'project=stepwell exit=shared silent=0 stone_bricks=200/64/0/40/0/224', s=16)]
    swr = J(base + crew_rows, SWP(sw2))
    for k in ('off_record', 'clear_outside', 'zone', 'lease_overlap', 'bag_loss', 'unknown_stood'):
        assert swr[k] == 0, ('stepwell-05 rows', k, swr[k])
    assert J(base + [R('_build_bag', 'project=stepwell exit=shared silent=0 stone_bricks=200/64/0/40/0/200', s=16)], SWP(sw2))['bag_loss'] == 1, 'positive control: a shared exit is judged (24 stone bricks short)'
    saved_lo = lease_overlaps
    globals()['lease_overlaps'] = lambda leases, builders_max=BUILDERS_MAX: saved_lo(leases, 99)
    try:
        assert J(base, SWP(sw1))['lease_overlap'] == 0, 'the mutant must fail the head-of-1 assertion'
    finally:
        globals()['lease_overlaps'] = saved_lo
    # ordernowait-01 TH1 (11-06; bots/src/ordernowait.mjs, OWNER 11-06: the town hall and the cottage stop waiting for the enchanting
    # workshop to stand): the TOWN HALL now sites and builds while the workshop is open. It logs no new row kind -- `op=sited`, places
    # on its record's cells, its earthwork's cuts and its trees (`op=clear kind=cut|tree`), a reorient of a state cell, the keeper's
    # `tidy op=plot_retired kind=X2` -- and each is judged by the existing generic rules (the record's cells, its `clear` list,
    # its replant count), as the cottage's are (RO1 above). Locked here with positive controls and a mutant of the clear-list rule.
    # KNOWN GAP, NOT NEW (Codex TH1 r1): buildrun.mjs's trunk felling above a listed tree cell logs one `op=fell cell=<the listed cell>
    # n=<logs>` row and no per-log cell, and this read judges no `op=fell` row -- for EVERY project with trees on its clear list
    # (workshop, hall, enchantshop, stepwell, tidy...), not only the town hall. Its X2 plot lies on the wall extension, outside every
    # protected zone; closing the gap (a per-log row, or judging `fell` against the column above its listed cell) is its own change.
    tpal = {'roof': ['spruce_slab'], 'ridge': ['spruce_slab'], 'wall': ['spruce_planks', '$planks'], 'post': ['spruce_log']}
    trec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'prev': None, 'blueprint': 'townhall@1', 'plot': 'townhall', 'plotId': 'X2', 'palette': tpal,
            'cells': [{'at': [70, 71, 70], 'role': 'wall', 'seg': 'wall-F'}, {'at': [71, 75, 70], 'role': 'roof', 'seg': 'roof-inner', 'ws': {'type': 'bottom'}}],
            'clear': [{'at': [72, 71, 72], 'kind': 'cut'}, {'at': [74, 71, 74], 'kind': 'tree'}], 'replant': [{'at': [80, 71, 80]}]}
    thb = PP(townhall={'record': trec, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [trec]})
    TH = lambda d, pp=thb: J(base + [R('_build', d, s=14)], pp)
    clean = lambda r: all(r[k] == 0 for k in ('off_record', 'clear_outside', 'zone', 'felled_unplanted', 'g5_unjudged'))
    assert clean(TH('project=townhall op=sited cell=70,70 kind=townhall@1/S/X2/2cells gen=1')), 'the siting row judges nothing'
    assert clean(TH('project=tidy op=plot_retired kind=X2 gen=81')), 'the keeper retiring X2 judges nothing'
    assert TH('project=townhall op=place seg=wall-F cell=70,71,70 block=spruce_planks')['off_record'] == 0, 'a head record cell'
    assert TH('project=townhall op=place seg=wall-F cell=79,71,70 block=spruce_planks')['off_record'] == 1, 'positive control: off the record is G1'
    assert TH('project=townhall op=clear cell=72,71,72 block=dirt kind=cut')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'an earthwork cut on its clear list'
    assert TH('project=townhall op=clear cell=73,71,72 block=dirt kind=cut')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: a cut off the list is G2'
    assert clean(TH('project=townhall op=clear cell=74,71,74 block=spruce_log kind=tree saplings=1/1')), 'a listed tree, its sapling planted'
    assert TH('project=townhall op=clear cell=74,71,74 block=spruce_log kind=tree saplings=0/1')['felled_unplanted'] == 1, 'positive control: felled unplanted is H5'
    assert TH('project=townhall op=clear seg=roof-inner cell=71,75,70 block=spruce_slab kind=reorient')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a reorient of its own slab (RO1)'
    assert TH('project=townhall op=clear seg=wall-F cell=70,71,70 block=spruce_planks kind=reorient')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: a stateless cell is never reoriented'
    MUT_CL = "                        ok = any(k in {ck(c['at']) for c in rr.get('clear') or [] if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('at')} for rr in (rec, rec_pre) if rr is not None)\n"   # forest-01: the rule now reads rec_pre too
    src_th = open(os.path.abspath(__file__)).read()
    assert src_th.count(MUT_CL) == 1, 'ANCHOR MISSING OR NOT UNIQUE'
    ns_t = {'__name__': 'shopread_mutant', '__file__': os.path.abspath(__file__)}
    exec(compile(src_th.replace(MUT_CL, "                        ok = True\n"), 'shopread_mutant', 'exec'), ns_t)
    thm = ns_t['combine'](*ns_t['judge'](base + [R('_build', 'project=townhall op=clear cell=73,71,72 block=dirt kind=cut', s=14)], thb, {}, cut=CUT, gens=gens))
    assert thm['clear_outside'] == 0, 'MUTANT SURVIVED: the clear-list rule'
    # copperrun-02 CR2 (11-06): the copper trip's rows -- `gather` of copper ore far from town (success and failed), and a
    # `_copper_trip` row (not a ROW_KIND) -- as the showcase tripwire marks them (arm canary, post). They REACH the judge (the gather
    # counter moves: the positive control) and move no gate. A text mutant that judges a gather row as off_record is caught.
    cr_rows = [R('gather', 'tunnelled 6 blocks to copper_ore; collected 7 copper_ore', s=13, bot='showcase-A', feet=[120, 41, -70]),
               R('gather', 'copper_ore found but every candidate is buried', s=14, status='failed', bot='showcase-A', feet=[120, 41, -70]),
               R('gather', 'collected 3 deepslate_copper_ore', s=15, bot='showcase-B')]
    cr_trip = [R('_copper_trip', 'phase=banked at=120,41,-70 block=copper_ore gained=21 ms=410000 why=gather_success', s=16, bot='showcase-A')]
    cr_z = {'board-a': [[100, -64, -90, 140, 90, -50]]}
    gates = ('off_record', 'clear_outside', 'zone', 'lease_overlap', 'felled_unplanted', 'unknown_stood', 'bag_loss', 'g5_unjudged', 'oracle_missing', 'zone_unknown')
    cr_b = J(base, zones=cr_z)
    cr = J(base + cr_rows + [r for r in cr_trip if r['name'] in ROW_KINDS], zones=cr_z)
    assert cr.get('gather_post_canary', 0) == cr_b.get('gather_post_canary', 0) + 3 and cr.get('gather_ok_post_canary', 0) == cr_b.get('gather_ok_post_canary', 0) + 2, 'CR2 positive control: the copper gather rows reach the judge'
    assert '_copper_trip' not in ROW_KINDS, 'CR2: the trip row is not a judged kind'
    for k in gates:
        assert cr[k] == cr_b[k], ('CR2: a copper trip row moved a gate', k, cr[k], cr_b[k])
    assert J(base + [R('_build', 'project=shop op=clear cell=120,41,-70 block=copper_ore kind=foreign', s=13)], zones=cr_z)['zone'] == 1, 'CR2 positive control: a BUILD row on that cell in the zone is G3'
    CR_ANCH = "            out['gather_ok_%s_%s' % (per, arm)] += int(r['status'] == 'success')\n"
    src_cr = open(os.path.abspath(__file__)).read()
    assert src_cr.count(CR_ANCH) == 1, 'ANCHOR MISSING OR NOT UNIQUE'
    ns_c = {'__name__': 'shopread_mutant', '__file__': os.path.abspath(__file__)}
    exec(compile(src_cr.replace(CR_ANCH, CR_ANCH + "            out['off_record'] += 1\n"), 'shopread_mutant', 'exec'), ns_c)
    crm = ns_c['combine'](*ns_c['judge'](base + cr_rows, proj, cr_z, cut=CUT, gens=gens))
    assert crm['off_record'] > cr_b['off_record'], 'MUTANT SURVIVED: a gather row judged as off_record'
    # forest-01 FR1-FR4 + RF1 (11-06; bots/src/forest.mjs, skills.mjs buildForest): THE MANAGED FOREST and THE IN-WALL FARM'S RETIREMENT
    frec = {'v': 1, 'gen': 2, 'prev': 1, 'blueprint': 'forest@1', 'plot': 'forest', 'box': [98, 98, 112, 112], 'level': 64,
            'cells': [{'at': [100, 63, 100], 'role': 'soil', 'seg': 'S1'}, {'at': [100, 64, 100], 'role': 'plot', 'seg': 'S1', 'stand': [100, 65, 101]},
                      {'at': [103, 64, 100], 'role': 'floor', 'seg': 'F'}],
            'clear': [{'at': [104, 65, 100], 'kind': 'cut', 'name': 'dirt'}], 'trees': [{'base': [106, 65, 106], 'logs': [[106, 65, 106], [106, 66, 106]], 'stand': [107, 65, 106]}], 'replant': []}
    t0ms = int(t0.timestamp() * 1000)
    fold = {'v': 1, 'gen': 3, 'prev': 2, 'blueprint': 'treefarm@1', 'plot': 'treefarm', 'box': [8, 4, 16, 12], 'at': t0ms - 10**9,
            'cells': [{'at': [10, 66, 6], 'role': 'soil', 'seg': 'R-1'}, {'at': [10, 67, 6], 'role': 'plot', 'seg': 'R-1'}, {'at': [12, 67, 8], 'role': 'light', 'seg': 'L'}],
            'clear': [{'at': [11, 67, 5], 'kind': 'cut', 'name': 'dirt'}], 'trees': [], 'replant': []}
    fret = dict(fold, gen=4, prev=3, cells=[], clear=[], zones=[], retired=list(fold['cells']), retiredClear=list(fold['clear']), was=fold['box'], box=None,
                state='retired', retiredAt=t0ms + 20_000, at=t0ms + 20_000)
    FP = lambda farm_chain=(fold, fret): PP(forest={'record': frec, 'records_files': 2, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [frec]},
                                             treefarm={'record': farm_chain[-1], 'records_files': len(farm_chain), 'leases': {}, 'chain': list(farm_chain)})
    FJ = lambda detail, s=14, zones=None, pr=None: J(base + [R('_build', detail, s=s)], pr or FP(), zones=zones)
    # FR1: a log in a plot column (the plot cell .. +12), a listed site-tree log; nothing else
    assert FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=100,70,100 block=spruce_log kind=grown')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a log in a plot column'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=100,64,100 block=spruce_log kind=grown')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'the stump (the sunk plot cell)'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=100,77,100 block=spruce_log kind=grown')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: past the trunk bound'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=101,66,100 block=spruce_log kind=grown')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'another column'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=100,66,100 block=dirt kind=grown')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'not a log'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=106,66,106 block=birch_log kind=site')['clear_outside'] == 0 and FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=106,67,106 block=birch_log kind=site')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a listed site-tree log; one above it is not'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=106,66,106 block=birch_log kind=grown')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a site tree is no plot column'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=100,64,100 block=log kind=grown_failed n=0 why=reach')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a failed row digs nothing: not judged'
    fm0 = J(base, FP())['mutations']
    assert FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=100,70,100 block=spruce_log kind=grown')['mutations'] == fm0 + 1 and FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=100,64,100 block=log kind=grown_failed')['mutations'] == fm0, 'a felling is a mutation; a failed row is not'
    fz = {'board-a': [[100, 0, 100, 100, 200, 100]]}
    assert FJ('project=forest op=harvest cell=100,70,100 block=spruce_log kind=grown', zones=fz)['zone'] == 1, 'a felling in a protected zone is G3'
    # FR2: leaves in the box + 1, level - 3 .. level + 5
    assert FJ('project=forest op=clear cell=101,66,100 block=spruce_leaves kind=stand')['clear_outside'] == 0 and FJ('project=forest op=clear cell=97,65,97 block=spruce_leaves kind=aisle')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'stand and aisle leaves (the margin + 1)'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=clear cell=96,65,100 block=spruce_leaves kind=aisle')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: outside the box + 1'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=clear cell=101,70,100 block=spruce_leaves kind=stand')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'above the band'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=clear cell=101,66,100 block=dirt kind=stand')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'not leaves'
    # forest-02: strips at their own levels -- the band runs to levelMax + 5
    frec2 = dict(frec, gen=3, prev=2, levelMax=66)
    FP2 = PP(forest={'record': frec2, 'records_files': 3, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [frec2]}, treefarm={'record': fret, 'records_files': 2, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [fold, fret]})
    assert FJ('project=forest op=clear cell=101,71,100 block=spruce_leaves kind=stand', pr=FP2)['clear_outside'] == 0, 'forest-02: a stand on the highest strip (levelMax 66 + 5)'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=clear cell=101,72,100 block=spruce_leaves kind=stand', pr=FP2)['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: past levelMax + 5'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=clear cell=101,71,100 block=spruce_leaves kind=stand')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a forest-01 record (no levelMax): level + 5 as before'
    # the forest's ordinary build rows: its cells and its clear list (G1/G2), a foreign dig of a plot's own floor
    assert FJ('project=forest op=place seg=S1 cell=100,64,100 block=spruce_sapling')['off_record'] == 0 and FJ('project=forest op=place seg=S1 cell=100,65,100 block=spruce_sapling')['off_record'] == 1, 'a sunk sapling on the record; one above it is not'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=clear cell=100,64,100 block=grass_block kind=foreign')['clear_outside'] == 0 and FJ('project=forest op=clear cell=104,65,100 block=dirt kind=cut')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'the plot\'s floor dug; a listed cut'
    # FR4: bone meal on a plot cell only, a mutation
    assert FJ('project=forest op=use cell=100,64,100 block=spruce_sapling kind=bonemeal')['off_record'] == 0, 'bone meal on a plot'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=use cell=103,64,100 block=dirt kind=bonemeal')['off_record'] == 1, 'positive control: off a plot'
    assert FJ('project=forest op=use cell=100,64,100 block=spruce_sapling kind=bonemeal', zones=fz)['zone'] == 1, 'a meal in a zone is G3'
    # no-change rows: no mutation
    for d in ('project=forest op=waived cell=100,64,100 kind=big gen=3', 'project=forest op=rotate kind=h0 gen=3', 'project=forest op=sweep n=2 kind=sapling',
              'project=forest op=garden_sited cell=9,66,5 kind=9_5_15_11 gen=7', 'project=treefarm op=retired kind=treefarm@1 gen=4'):
        rr = FJ(d, zones=fz)
        assert rr['mutations'] == fm0 and rr['zone'] == 0 and rr['off_record'] == 0 and rr['clear_outside'] == 0, ('no-change row', d)
    # RF1: the retirement's digs against the farm's chain (cells or retired)
    assert FJ('project=treefarm op=harvest cell=10,72,6 block=spruce_log kind=retire')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a log in the old plot column'
    assert FJ('project=treefarm op=harvest cell=11,72,6 block=spruce_log kind=retire')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'positive control: another column'
    assert FJ('project=treefarm op=clear cell=10,67,6 block=spruce_sapling kind=retire')['clear_outside'] == 0 and FJ('project=treefarm op=clear cell=12,67,8 block=torch kind=retire')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'its sapling, its torch'
    assert FJ('project=treefarm op=clear cell=10,67,6 block=torch kind=retire')['clear_outside'] == 1 and FJ('project=treefarm op=clear cell=13,67,8 block=torch kind=retire')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'a torch off a light cell'
    assert FJ('project=treefarm op=clear cell=11,69,6 block=spruce_leaves kind=stand')['clear_outside'] == 0 and FJ('project=treefarm op=clear cell=30,69,6 block=spruce_leaves kind=stand')['clear_outside'] == 1, 'stand leaves in the old box (was) + 1; far off, a trip'
    assert FJ('project=treefarm op=harvest cell=1,71,1 block=oak_log kind=grown')['clear_outside'] == 0, 'the old farm\'s own harvest rows stay unjudged'
    # the retired head judges the farm's EARLIER rows with its cells and clears as they were (rec_pre); after retiredAt, not
    assert FJ('project=treefarm op=place seg=R-1 cell=10,67,6 block=spruce_sapling', s=14)['off_record'] == 0, 'an old planting, before the retirement'
    assert FJ('project=treefarm op=place seg=R-1 cell=10,67,6 block=spruce_sapling', s=30)['off_record'] == 1, 'positive control: after it, off the record'
    assert FJ('project=treefarm op=clear cell=11,67,5 block=dirt kind=cut', s=14)['clear_outside'] == 0 and FJ('project=treefarm op=clear cell=11,67,5 block=dirt kind=cut', s=30)['clear_outside'] == 1, 'an old listed cut, before and after'
    # tidy_guard: the farm's cells are the groundskeeper's from ITS OWN retiredAt (not the wall demolition's completion)
    demo_done = {'v': 1, 'gen': 1, 'blueprint': 'walldemo@1', 'plot': 'walldemo', 'cells': [], 'structure_complete': True, 'completeAt': t0ms - 10**8}
    gpr = FP()['board-a']
    gpr = dict(gpr, projects=dict(gpr['projects'], walldemo={'record': demo_done, 'records_files': 1, 'leases': {}, 'chain': [demo_done]}))
    gg = tidy_guard(gpr)
    assert tidy_guarded([10, 67, 6], t0ms + 10_000, gg), 'the farm\'s plot cell is guarded before its retirement (an early demolition does not release it)'
    assert not tidy_guarded([10, 67, 6], t0ms + 30_000, gg), 'released at its retiredAt'
    assert tidy_guarded([100, 64, 100], t0ms + 30_000, gg), 'positive control: the forest\'s plot is guarded'
    for anch, mut, check, msg in (
            ("                    and c['at'][0] == cell[0] and c['at'][2] == cell[2] and c['at'][1] <= cell[1] <= c['at'][1] + top:\n",
             "                    and c['at'][0] == cell[0] and c['at'][2] == cell[2] and c['at'][1] <= cell[1] <= c['at'][1] + top + 10:\n",
             lambda ns: ns['combine'](*ns['judge'](base + [R('_build', 'project=forest op=harvest cell=100,77,100 block=spruce_log kind=grown', s=14)], FP(), {}, cut=CUT, gens=gens))['clear_outside'] == 1, 'the trunk bound'),
            ("            ra = (rec['retiredAt'] if plot == TREEFARM else done_at) if isinstance(rec.get('retiredAt'), (int, float)) else None\n",
             "            ra = done_at if isinstance(rec.get('retiredAt'), (int, float)) else None\n",
             lambda ns: ns['tidy_guarded']([10, 67, 6], t0ms + 10_000, ns['tidy_guard'](gpr)), 'the farm released at its own retiredAt'),
            ("            if rec is not None and plot in WALL_PLOTS + (TREEFARM,) and rec.get('retired') and isinstance(rec.get('retiredAt'), (int, float)) \\\n",
             "            if rec is not None and plot in WALL_PLOTS and rec.get('retired') and isinstance(rec.get('retiredAt'), (int, float)) \\\n",
             lambda ns: ns['combine'](*ns['judge'](base + [R('_build', 'project=treefarm op=place seg=R-1 cell=10,67,6 block=spruce_sapling', s=14)], FP(), {}, cut=CUT, gens=gens))['off_record'] == 0, 'the retired farm\'s earlier rows'),
            ("            if (op == 'harvest' and ((plot == FOREST and not str(f.get('kind') or '').endswith('_failed')) or (plot == TREEFARM and f.get('kind') == 'retire'))) \\\n",
             "            if (op == 'harvest' and ((plot == '_none_' and not str(f.get('kind') or '').endswith('_failed')) or (plot == TREEFARM and f.get('kind') == 'retire'))) \\\n",
             lambda ns: ns['combine'](*ns['judge'](base + [R('_build', 'project=forest op=harvest cell=100,70,100 block=spruce_log kind=grown', s=14)], FP(), fz, cut=CUT, gens=gens))['zone'] == 1, 'a felling is G3'),
            ("            elif op == 'harvest' and plot == FOREST and not str(f.get('kind') or '').endswith('_failed'):\n",
             "            elif op == 'harvest' and plot == FOREST:\n",
             lambda ns: ns['combine'](*ns['judge'](base + [R('_build', 'project=forest op=harvest cell=100,64,100 block=log kind=grown_failed', s=14)], FP(), {}, cut=CUT, gens=gens))['clear_outside'] == 0, 'a failed row is not judged')):
        src_f = open(os.path.abspath(__file__)).read()
        assert src_f.count(anch) == 1, ('ANCHOR MISSING OR NOT UNIQUE', anch[:60])
        ns_f = {'__name__': 'shopread_mutant', '__file__': os.path.abspath(__file__)}
        exec(compile(src_f.replace(anch, mut), 'shopread_mutant', 'exec'), ns_f)
        assert not check(ns_f), 'MUTANT SURVIVED: %s' % msg
    # market-01 MK1-MK3: the market's job blocks outside the zone (+ MARKET_PAD), the six makeable blocks, two a day; a run's merchant
    # outside the zone, at most MARKET_SPEND spent; a sale only of MARKET_GOODS. Positive controls first: the rows reach the judge.
    mz = {'board-a': [[49, -64, -59, 157, 320, 45]]}
    assert set(MARKET_KINDS) <= set(BUILD_KINDS), 'the showcase tripwire loads BUILD_KINDS rows: the market rows must be among them'
    def mk(rows_, zones=mz):
        o, l = judge(rows_, proj, zones)
        return o, l
    o, l = mk([R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=smithing_table at=44,70,-3 cluster=2@60,0 d=16.2'),
               R('_market_job', 'op=claim block=smithing_table at=44,70,-3 cluster=2@60,0 claimed:toolsmith', s=12),
               R('_market_job', 'op=skip why=no_free_jobless', s=20),
               R('_market_run', 'stop=traded with=toolsmith L1 at=44,70,-2 goods=coal:15 sold=coal:15 earned=1 spent=1 bought=brick:10 ms=19000', s=30),
               R('_trade', 'op=sell item=coal n=15 with=toolsmith', s=31), R('_trade', 'op=buy item=brick n=10 with=toolsmith', s=32),
               R('_trade', 'op=sell item=water_bucket n=2 with=wandering_trader', s=33)])
    assert o['market_places'] == 1 and o['market_runs'] == 1 and o['market_sales'] == 2, ('POSITIVE: the rows reach the judge', dict(o))
    assert o['off_record'] == 0 and o['zone'] == 0 and o['bag_loss'] == 0, (dict(o), dict(l))
    o, l = mk([R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=smithing_table at=46,70,-3 cluster=2@60,0 d=14')])
    assert o['zone'] == 1 and l['zone'] == ['board-a-Alpha 46,70,-3 smithing_table market_job'], 'inside the pad (49 - 3 = 46)'
    o, l = mk([R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=smithing_table at=60,70,0 cluster=2@60,0 d=1')])
    assert o['zone'] == 1, 'inside the village'
    o, l = mk([R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=lectern at=40,70,0 cluster=2@60,0 d=20')])
    assert o['off_record'] == 1 and l['off_record'] == ['board-a-Alpha 40,70,0 lectern market_block'], 'not a makeable job block'
    o, l = mk([R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=barrel at=40,70,%d' % i, s=i * 3600) for i in range(3)])
    assert o['off_record'] == 1 and l['off_record'] == ['board-a-Alpha 40,70,2 barrel market_cap'], 'the third in 24 h'
    o, l = mk([R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=barrel at=40,70,%d' % i, s=i * 13 * 3600) for i in range(3)])
    assert o['off_record'] == 0, 'two a day: the third is 26 h after the first'
    o, l = mk([R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=barrel cluster=2@60,0')])
    assert o['bag_unjudged'] == 1 and o['market_places'] == 0, 'no at=: reported, never judged on a guess'
    o, l = mk([R('_market_run', 'stop=traded with=fletcher L1 at=50,70,0 goods=stick:32 sold=stick:32 earned=1 spent=0 bought=none')])
    assert o['zone'] == 1 and l['zone'] == ['board-a-Alpha 50,70,0 fletcher market_run'], 'a run inside the village'
    o, l = mk([R('_market_run', 'stop=traded with=wandering_trader at=-28,67,9 goods=water_bucket:2 sold=water_bucket:2 earned=4 spent=9 bought=cherry_log:72')])
    assert o['bag_loss'] == 1 and l['bag_loss'] == ['MK2 market_spend board-a-Alpha spent=9'], 'over the run budget'
    o, l = mk([R('_market_run', 'stop=skip:night')])
    assert o['zone'] == 0 and o['bag_loss'] == 0 and o['market_runs'] == 1, 'a skip row: no merchant, nothing judged'
    o, l = mk([R('_trade', 'op=sell item=diamond n=1 with=toolsmith'), R('_trade', 'op=sell item=lapis_lazuli n=12 with=cleric'), R('_trade', 'op=buy item=diamond_pickaxe n=1 with=toolsmith')])
    assert o['bag_loss'] == 2 and l['bag_loss'] == ['MK3 market_sold board-a-Alpha diamond:1', 'MK3 market_sold board-a-Alpha lapis_lazuli:12'], dict(l)
    o, l = mk([R('_trade', 'op=sell item=diamond n=1 with=toolsmith', arm='control'), R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=lectern at=60,70,0', per='pre')])
    assert o['bag_loss'] == 0 and o['zone'] == 0 and o['off_record'] == 0, 'the canary arm after the cut only (the showcase: every bot)'
    o, l = mk([R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=smithing_table at=46,70,-3'), R('_build', 'project=shop op=unknown cell=5,71,14', s=1)], zones={})
    assert o['zone'] == 0, 'no zones: no zone line'
    # market-02: a walk-in trade inside the village is PRESENCE (walkin=1), never zone; beds outside the pad, 2 a day by the foot
    o, l = mk([R('_market_run', 'stop=traded with=librarian L2 at=380,70,-330 goods=coal:15 sold=none earned=0 spent=8 bought=lantern:8 walkin=1 zone=village-taiga-23_-20')], zones={'board-a': [[301, -64, -404, 447, 86, -265]]})
    assert o['zone'] == 0 and o['market_walkins'] == 1, ('a walk-in trade in the village', dict(o), dict(l))
    o, l = mk([R('_market_run', 'stop=traded with=librarian L2 at=380,70,-330 goods=coal:15 sold=none earned=0 spent=8 bought=lantern:8 zone=village-taiga-23_-20')], zones={'board-a': [[301, -64, -404, 447, 86, -265]]})
    assert o['zone'] == 1 and o['market_walkins'] == 0, 'POSITIVE: the same row without walkin=1 is a run inside the village'
    o, l = mk([R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=white_bed at=40,70,0 cluster=4@60,0 d=20'), R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=white_bed at=41,70,0 cluster=4@60,0 why=bed_head', s=1),
               R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=white_bed at=40,70,4 cluster=4@60,0 d=20', s=60), R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=white_bed at=41,70,4 cluster=4@60,0 why=bed_head', s=61),
               R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=composter at=40,70,8 cluster=1@60,0 d=20', s=90), R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=smithing_table at=40,70,12 cluster=1@60,0 d=20', s=95)])
    assert o['off_record'] == 0 and o['zone'] == 0 and o['market_places'] == 6, ('two beds (four halves) + two job blocks in a day', dict(o), dict(l))
    o, l = mk([R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=red_bed at=40,70,%d' % (i * 4), s=i * 60) for i in range(3)])
    assert o['off_record'] == 1 and l['off_record'] == ['board-a-Alpha 40,70,8 red_bed market_cap'], 'the third bed in 24 h'
    o, l = mk([R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=white_bed at=40,70,0'), R('_market_job', 'op=placed block=white_bed at=46,70,0 why=bed_head', s=1)])
    assert o['zone'] == 1 and l['zone'] == ['board-a-Alpha 46,70,0 white_bed market_job'], 'a bed half in the pad'
    # THE COPY: market.mjs's lists, when the bots tree is beside this file's repo
    mjs = os.environ.get('SHOPREAD_MARKET') or os.path.join(here, '..', '..', '..', 'bots', 'src', 'market.mjs')
    if os.path.exists(mjs):
        src_m = open(mjs).read()
        gm = re.search(r'export const GOODS = Object\.freeze\(\{(.*?)\n\}\)', src_m, re.S)
        jm = re.search(r'export const JOB_RECIPES = Object\.freeze\(\{(.*?)\n\}\)', src_m, re.S)
        assert gm and jm, 'market.mjs GOODS / JOB_RECIPES not found'
        assert set(re.findall(r'(\w+): \{ keep', gm.group(1))) == set(MARKET_GOODS), 'MARKET_GOODS is not market.mjs GOODS'
        assert set(re.findall(r'^  (\w+): \{', jm.group(1), re.M)) == set(MARKET_BLOCKS), 'MARKET_BLOCKS is not market.mjs JOB_RECIPES'
        for k, v in (('zonePad', MARKET_PAD), ('jobsPerDay', MARKET_PER_DAY), ('spendPerRun', MARKET_SPEND), ('bedsPerDay', MARKET_BEDS_PER_DAY)):
            assert re.search(r'\b%s: %d,' % (k, v), src_m), 'MARKET.%s is not %d' % (k, v)
    else:
        print('shopread selftest: SKIPPED the market lists vs bots/src/market.mjs check (no market.mjs: set SHOPREAD_MARKET)')
    print('shopread selftest: ok')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    if '--selftest' in sys.argv:
        selftest(); sys.exit(0)
    from lib.telemetry import Events
    ovr = os.environ.get('CANARY_DRYRUN')
    if ovr:
        CAN, CV, ISO = ovr.split(':', 2)
        CUT = dt.datetime.fromisoformat(ISO.replace('Z', '+00:00'))
    else:
        man = json.load(open('/srv/mcbots/trial-manifest.json'))
        CUT = dt.datetime.fromisoformat(man['declared_at'].replace('Z', '+00:00'))
        CAN = man['canary_pool']; CV = man.get('canary_code_version') or ''
    CANS = {x.strip() for x in str(CAN).split(',') if x.strip()}
    now = dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc)
    elapsed = (now - CUT).total_seconds() / 60
    assert elapsed > 0 and CAN, 'no canary declared'
    W = min(elapsed, float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 and not sys.argv[1].startswith('-') else 360)
    END, PRE = CUT + dt.timedelta(minutes=W), CUT - dt.timedelta(minutes=W)

    def load_window(since, until):
        ev = Events.load(paths='/var/log/mcai/*/skill-*.jsonl', since=since, until=until)
        key = lambda r: (str(r.get('t')), ((r.get('bot') or {}).get('name')), r.get('name'), r.get('detail'))
        out, seen = [], set()
        for r in ev.rows:
            if key(r) not in seen:
                out.append(r); seen.add(key(r))
        for k in range(0, (until.date() - since.date()).days + 1):
            tag = (since.date() + dt.timedelta(days=k + 1)).strftime('%Y%m%d')
            for g in glob.glob('/var/log/mcai/*/skill-*.jsonl-%s.gz' % tag):
                for r in Events.load(paths=g, since=since, until=until).rows:
                    if key(r) not in seen:
                        out.append(r); seen.add(key(r))
        return out

    raw = load_window(PRE, END)
    rows, walked, offbuild = [], 0, 0
    bots = defaultdict(set)
    for r in raw:
        b = (r.get('bot') or {}).get('name')
        t = r.get('t')
        if not b or t is None:
            continue
        walked += 1
        arm = 'canary' if pool_of(b) in CANS else 'control'
        per = 'post' if t >= CUT else 'pre'
        ver = (((r.get('raw') or {}).get('code') or {}).get('version') or '')
        if arm == 'canary' and per == 'post' and CV and ver and not ver.startswith(CV):
            offbuild += 1
            continue
        bots[(per, arm)].add(b)
        sk = ((r.get('raw') or {}).get('skill') or {})
        name = sk.get('name') or r.get('name')
        feet = pos_of({'pos': (r.get('bot') or {}).get('pos')}) if arm == 'canary' and per == 'post' else None
        if name in ROW_KINDS or feet:
            item = (sk.get('args') or {}).get('item') if isinstance(sk.get('args'), dict) else None
            rows.append({'t': t, 'per': per, 'arm': arm, 'name': name if name in ROW_KINDS else '_pos', 'status': sk.get('status'),
                         'detail': (r.get('detail') or '') if name in ROW_KINDS else '', 'bot': b, 'item': item, 'feet': feet})
    projects = read_projects()
    plans = read_plans()
    zones_by_pool = {}
    for pool, keys in plans.items():
        for key, info in keys.items():
            for wkey in info['worlds']:
                world = json.loads(wkey) if wkey != '"__unparseable__"' else None
                zones_by_pool.setdefault(pool, []).extend(zones_of(info['dir'], world))
    gens = structcount_gens()
    surveys = load_surveys()
    out, lists = judge(rows, projects, zones_by_pool, cut=CUT, gens=gens, surveys=surveys)
    res = combine(out, lists)
    L = res['_lists']
    hrs = {k: len(v) * W / 60 for k, v in bots.items()}
    recs = {p: pr['record'] for p, pr in projects.items() if pr.get('record')}
    G = lambda k: out.get(k, 0)
    print('DENOMINATORS rows walked %d (canary rows on another build skipped %d) | bots pre canary %d control %d | post canary %d control %d | window %d min | NOMINAL bot-h (bots x window) post canary %.1f control %.1f'
          % (walked, offbuild, len(bots[('pre', 'canary')]), len(bots[('pre', 'control')]), len(bots[('post', 'canary')]), len(bots[('post', 'control')]), W,
             hrs.get(('post', 'canary'), 0), hrs.get(('post', 'control'), 0)))
    print('             oracle: towns with a workshop record %d (canary %d) | canary towns mutating %d | lease files %d over %d names | canary build rows with a position %d | structure counts %d (newest %s)'
          % (len(recs), sum(1 for p in recs if p in CANS), G('towns_mutating'), G('lease_files'), G('lease_names'), G('build_rows_with_pos'), len(gens), gens[-1][0].strftime('%m-%d %H:%MZ') if gens else '-'))
    print('LIVENESS     licence rows canary %d | build visits canary %d -> %s' % (G('licence_canary'), G('visits_canary'), 'ok' if res['liveness'] else 'INERT'))
    print('VISITS       stops %s | placed %d refused %d silent %d cleared %d planted %d unknown %d | sited %d | bag rows %d (names %d, cobble/planks scaffold %d)'
          % ({k[6:]: v for k, v in sorted(out.items()) if k.startswith('visit_')}, G('sum_placed'), G('sum_refused'), G('sum_silent'),
             G('sum_cleared'), G('sum_planted'), G('sum_unknown'), G('sited'), G('bag_rows'), G('bag_names'), G('bag_scaffold')))
    print('             stops other than complete (first 6) %s' % L.get('why', [])[:6])
    print('ARMS         control build rows after the deploy %d (must be 0) %s' % (res['arms_breach'], L.get('control_rows', [])[:3]))
    print('ORACLE       towns mutating without a readable record %d %s | without a readable zones file %d %s'
          % (res['oracle_missing'], L.get('oracle_missing', [])[:3], res['zone_unknown'], L.get('zone_unknown', [])[:3]))
    print('DEFECTS      G1 off the record %d of %d placements %s | G2 clears outside the list %d of %d %s | G3 in a zone %d of %d mutations %s'
          % (res['off_record'], G('placements'), L.get('off_record', [])[:3], res['clear_outside'], G('clears'), L.get('clear_outside', [])[:3], res['zone'], G('mutations'), L.get('zone', [])[:3]))
    print('             G4 lease overlaps %d of %d files %s | G5 fellings with a replant cell unplanted %d of %d tree clears (unjudged %d) %s'
          % (res['lease_overlap'], G('lease_files'), L.get('lease_overlap', [])[:3], res['felled_unplanted'], G('tree_clears'), res['g5_unjudged'], L.get('felled_unplanted', [])[:3]))
    print('             G6 unknown cells stood on %d of %d unknown rows %s | G8 bag losses %d of %d names (%d of %d bag rows unjudged: not a clean exit %s) | REPORTED G7 placed twice %d %s'
          % (res['unknown_stood'], G('unknown_rows'), L.get('unknown_stood', [])[:3], res['bag_loss'], G('bag_names'), res['bag_unjudged'], G('bag_rows'), L.get('bag_unjudged', [])[:2], res['placed_twice'], L.get('placed_twice', [])[:3]))
    print('             STALLED POOLS %d %s (a stuck cell every open segment waits on: read as stuck, not as no effect)'
          % (len(res.get('stalled_pools') or {}), sorted((res.get('stalled_pools') or {}).items())[:5]))
    print('             REPORTED replants moved %d deferred %d skipped %d %s | cells deferred %d | STUCK ON CELL %d %s | stuck map unwritten %d %s'
          % (res.get('replant_moved', 0), res.get('replant_deferred', 0), res.get('replant_skipped', 0), L.get('replant_skipped', [])[:3], res.get('deferred', 0), res.get('stuck_on_cell', 0), L.get('stuck_on_cell', [])[:3], res.get('stuck_unwritten', 0), L.get('stuck_unwritten', [])[:3]))
    print('NO HARM      gather DiD %s -> %s | pickaxe rungs DiD %s -> %s | litter DiD (cells per town, canary less control) %s | deaths: the default gate (two-death floor) | immobile: immobiledid'
          % tuple([x for n in ('gather_ok', 'rung_ok') for x in ('null' if res['%s_did' % n] is None else '%+.3f' % res['%s_did' % n], verdict_of(res, n))]
                  + ['null' if res['litter_did'] is None else '%+.1f' % res['litter_did']]))
    print('             (gather canary %d/%d -> %d/%d control %d/%d -> %d/%d | rungs canary %d/%d -> %d/%d control %d/%d -> %d/%d ok/rows; UNEXPOSED = a cell under %d rows, never ok)'
          % tuple([G('%s_%s' % (a, b)) for b in ('pre_canary', 'post_canary', 'pre_control', 'post_control') for a in ('gather_ok', 'gather')]
                  + [G('%s_%s' % (a, b)) for b in ('pre_canary', 'post_canary', 'pre_control', 'post_control') for a in ('rung_ok', 'rung')] + [MIN_CELL]))
    print('EXPOSURE     canary towns complete by their rows %d %s | with a lit room in the record extent (PRELIMINARY) %d (need %d conformant); exposure -> %s | INSTRUMENT control gather %d craft %d -> %s'
          % (G('complete_towns'), L.get('complete_towns', []), G('complete_towns_room'), EXPOSE_TOWNS, 'READY' if res['exposure_ready'] else 'NOT YET',
             G('gather_post_control'), G('craft_post_control'), 'ok' if res['instrument_control'] else 'BLIND'))
    print('             CONFORMANCE (the exposure) source %s: towns conformant %d (need %d) %s | PRELIMINARY world per town %s'
          % ('present (%d towns surveyed)' % len(surveys[1]) if surveys[0] else 'ABSENT (%s): never READY' % SURVEY_DIR, G('complete_towns_conform'), EXPOSE_TOWNS, L.get('conformance', []), L.get('world', [])))
    try:
        if ovr:
            raise RuntimeError('CANARY_DRYRUN set -- not emitting')
        sys.path.insert(0, os.path.expanduser('~')); sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp')
        from readjson import emit
        emit('shopread', W, {k: v for k, v in res.items() if not k.startswith('_')})
    except Exception as e:
        print('emit failed:', e)
